{

  {
    TableNo=8061230;
    OnRun=VAR
            cduImport_L@8053001 : Codeunit 8053600;
            recSetup_L@8053000 : Record 8053602;
            recSpy_L@1000000000 : Record 8053612;
          BEGIN
            //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            // Lancement de l'interface depuis le moteur d'interface p'riodiquement
            //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            //  Entr'es
            //    - Var Rec : ParamStre de l'interface
            //  Sorties
            //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            // --> 20150521-Gen10-CMC02.02.00-2903-FHS01
            //BEFORE
            {
            // --> 20150410-Gen10-CMC02.02.00-2903-FHS01
            recSpy_L.INIT;
            recSpy_L.Type := recSpy_L.Type::"Begin";
            recSpy_L.Date := WORKDATE;
            recSpy_L.Time := TIME;
            recSpy_L.INSERT(TRUE);
            COMMIT;
            // <-- 20150410-Gen10-CMC02.02.00-2903-FHS01
            }
            //AFTER
            // <-- 20150521-Gen10-CMC02.02.00-2903-FHS01
            // --> 20170407-WI00-TER01.01.00-3254-BSI01
            recSetup_L.GET();
            CASE recSetup_L."Deployment Type" OF
              recSetup_L."Deployment Type"::"On-premise" : BEGIN
            //Lancement du traitement du journal d'import
            cduManCon_G.RunPendingEvent();

            //Lancement du traitement du journal d'export
            RunPendingEvent();

                //recSetup_L.GET();
            IF recSetup_L."SAS Manual after Error" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              cduImport_L.ImportSimpleOrderWithoutInErro();
            END
            ELSE BEGIN
              cduImport_L.ImportAllSimpleOrder();
            END;
                CLEARLASTERROR;
              END;
              recSetup_L."Deployment Type"::"On-line" : BEGIN

                //Lancement du traitement du journal d'import
            // --> 20170407-WI00-TER01.01.00-3254-BSI01
                cduManCon_G.RunPendingEvent_D365;
                //Lancement du traitement du journal d'export
                RunPendingEvent_D365;
            CLEARLASTERROR;
              END;
            END; // END CASE

            // --> 20150521-Gen10-CMC02.02.00-2903-FHS01
            //BEFORE
            {
            // --> 20150410-Gen10-CMC02.02.00-2903-FHS01
            recSpy_L.Type := recSpy_L.Type::"End";
            recSpy_L.Date := WORKDATE;
            recSpy_L.Time := TIME;
            recSpy_L.INSERT(TRUE);
            // <-- 20150410-Gen10-CMC02.02.00-2903-FHS01
            }
            //AFTER
            // <-- 20150521-Gen10-CMC02.02.00-2903-FHS01
          END;

  }

    PROCEDURE RunPendingEvent@8053608();
    VAR
      recJournal_L@8053600 : Record 8053606;
      recJournalUpdate_L@8053601 : Record 8053606;
      recCrmOrganisation_L@8053602 : Record 8053075;
      cduSynchroError_L@8053603 : Codeunit 8053605;
      recJournalSearch_L@8053604 : Record 8053606;
      i_L@8053605 : Integer;
      recJournalLine_L@8053606 : Record 8053607;
      recAutomation_L@8053607 : Automation "{90B5DB41-ED3C-488E-84F5-6647F85F95A2} 1.0:{F313095A-9ADF-3065-9FD4-FD3674235D4D}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class";
      recSetup_L@8053000 : Record 8053602;
      Skip_L@8053001 : Boolean;
      recAutomation2_L@8053002 : Automation "{90B5DB41-ED3C-488E-84F5-6647F85F95A2} 1.0:{F313095A-9ADF-3065-9FD4-FD3674235D4D}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class";
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Traite toutes les demandes qui ne sont pas encore trait'es
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      CodeCompany_G := cduManCon_G.GetCompanyMcCode();

      //Recherche du code de la soci't' actuelle
      recCrmOrganisation_L.RESET;
      recCrmOrganisation_L.SETRANGE("Company Name", COMPANYNAME);
      recCrmOrganisation_L.FINDFIRST;

      i_L := 0;

      //Parcours de toutes les lignes du journal n'ayant pas fini d'^tre trait'es
      recJournal_L.RESET;
      recJournal_L.SETCURRENTKEY(Closed, Type);
      recJournal_L.SETRANGE(Closed, FALSE);
      IF recJournal_L.ISEMPTY = FALSE THEN
        // --> 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //IF cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
          //cduManCon_G.GetAutomation(recAutomation_L);
        //AFTER
        IF cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion() AND cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion2() THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.GetAutomation(recAutomation_L);
          cduManCon_G.GetAutomation2(recAutomation2_L);
        // <-- 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
          REPEAT
            i_L += 1;
            // --> 20150521-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2951-FHS01
            //BEFORE
            {
            // --> 20150428-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2951-FHS01
            Skip_L := FALSE;
            IF i_L = 15 THEN BEGIN
              recSetup_L.GET();
              recSetup_L."Stop Export Order" := Skip_L;
            END;
            IF Skip_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
            // <-- 20150428-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2951-FHS01
            }
            //AFTER
            // <-- 20150521-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2951-FHS01
              SetFilterOnJournalByType(recJournal_L, i_L);
              IF recJournal_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
                REPEAT
                  recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L.Type, recJournal_L."Document No.");

                  //Lancement de l'ordre (RunEvent via codeunit externe)
                  CLEAR(cduSynchroError_L);
                  // --> 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
                  //BEFORE
                  //cduSynchroError_L.SetCrmConnexion(recAutomation_L);
                  //AFTER
                  cduSynchroError_L.SetCrmConnexion(recAutomation_L, recAutomation2_L);
                  // <-- 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
                  IF NOT(cduSynchroError_L.RUN(recJournalUpdate_L)) THEN BEGIN
                    recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L.Type, recJournal_L."Document No.");
                    recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text"));
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L.Type, recJournal_L."Document No.");
                    recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" := '';
                  END;

                  IF recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" <> Text8053698 THEN BEGIN
                    //Mise . jour du journal
                    IF recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" = '' THEN BEGIN
                      recJournalUpdate_L.Closed := TRUE;
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      IF STRPOS(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text", Text8053640) = 1 THEN BEGIN
                        recJournalUpdate_L.Closed := TRUE;
                        recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" := COPYSTR(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text", STRLEN(Text8053640) + 1,
                          STRLEN(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text"));
                      END ELSE BEGIN
                        recJournalUpdate_L.Closed := FALSE;
                      END;
                    END;
                    recJournalUpdate_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
                  END;
                  COMMIT;
                UNTIL recJournal_L.NEXT = 0;
              END;
            // --> 20150521-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2951-FHS01
            //BEFORE
            {
            // --> 20150428-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2951-FHS01
            END;
            // <-- 20150428-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2951-FHS01
            }
            //AFTER
            // <-- 20150521-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2951-FHS01
          UNTIL i_L = 35;
        END;

      cduManCon_G.ClearConnector();

      PurgeJournal();
    END;

    PROCEDURE RunPendingEventWithFilter@8053605(VAR recJournalExport_P@8053606 : Record 8053606);
    VAR
      recJournal_L@8053600 : Record 8053606;
      recJournalUpdate_L@8053601 : Record 8053606;
      recCrmOrganisation_L@8053602 : Record 8053075;
      cduSynchroError_L@8053603 : Codeunit 8053605;
      recJournalSearch_L@8053604 : Record 8053606;
      i_L@8053605 : Integer;
      Count_L@8053608 : Integer;
      ValBuffer_L@8053607 : Integer;
      j_L@8053609 : Integer;
      recAutomation_L@8053610 : Automation "{90B5DB41-ED3C-488E-84F5-6647F85F95A2} 1.0:{F313095A-9ADF-3065-9FD4-FD3674235D4D}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class";
      recSetup_L@8053001 : Record 8053602;
      Skip_L@8053000 : Boolean;
      recAutomation2_L@8053002 : Automation "{90B5DB41-ED3C-488E-84F5-6647F85F95A2} 1.0:{F313095A-9ADF-3065-9FD4-FD3674235D4D}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class";
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Traite toutes les demandes qui ne sont pas encore trait'es
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      CodeCompany_G := cduManCon_G.GetCompanyMcCode();

      //Recherche du code de la soci't' actuelle
      recCrmOrganisation_L.RESET;
      recCrmOrganisation_L.SETRANGE("Company Name", COMPANYNAME);
      recCrmOrganisation_L.FINDFIRST;

      i_L := 0;

      cduManCon_G.OpenWindow();

      //Parcours de toutes les lignes du journal n'ayant pas fini d'^tre trait'es
      recJournal_L.RESET;
      recJournal_L.SETCURRENTKEY(Closed, Type);
      recJournal_L.COPYFILTERS(recJournalExport_P);
      recJournal_L.SETRANGE(Closed, FALSE);
      IF recJournal_L.ISEMPTY = FALSE THEN
        // --> 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //IF cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
          //cduManCon_G.GetAutomation(recAutomation_L);
        //AFTER
        IF cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion() AND cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion2() THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.GetAutomation(recAutomation_L);
          cduManCon_G.GetAutomation2(recAutomation2_L);
        // <-- 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
          REPEAT
            i_L += 1;
            j_L := 0;
            cduManCon_G.NewStepWindow(i_L);
            // --> 20150521-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2951-FHS01
            //BEFORE
            {
            // --> 20150428-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2951-FHS01
            Skip_L := FALSE;
            IF i_L = 15 THEN BEGIN
              recSetup_L.GET();
              recSetup_L."Stop Export Order" := Skip_L;
            END;
            IF Skip_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
            // <-- 20150428-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2951-FHS01
            }
            //AFTER
            // <-- 20150521-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2951-FHS01
              SetFilterOnJournalByType(recJournal_L, i_L);
              Count_L := recJournal_L.COUNT;
              IF recJournal_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
                REPEAT
                  j_L += 1;
                  ValBuffer_L := ROUND(j_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                  cduManCon_G.UpdateWindow(2, ValBuffer_L);

                  recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L.Type, recJournal_L."Document No.");

                  //Lancement de l'ordre (RunEvent via codeunit externe)
                  CLEAR(cduSynchroError_L);
                  // --> 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
                  //BEFORE
                  //cduSynchroError_L.SetCrmConnexion(recAutomation_L);
                  //AFTER
                  cduSynchroError_L.SetCrmConnexion(recAutomation_L, recAutomation2_L);
                  // <-- 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
                  IF NOT(cduSynchroError_L.RUN(recJournalUpdate_L)) THEN BEGIN
                    recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L.Type, recJournal_L."Document No.");
                    recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text"));
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L.Type, recJournal_L."Document No.");
                    recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" := '';
                  END;

                  IF recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" <> Text8053698 THEN BEGIN
                    //Mise . jour du journal
                    IF recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" = '' THEN BEGIN
                      recJournalUpdate_L.Closed := TRUE;
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      IF STRPOS(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text", Text8053640) = 1 THEN BEGIN
                        recJournalUpdate_L.Closed := TRUE;
                        recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" := COPYSTR(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text", STRLEN(Text8053640) + 1,
                          STRLEN(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text"));
                      END ELSE BEGIN
                        recJournalUpdate_L.Closed := FALSE;
                      END;
                    END;
                    recJournalUpdate_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
                  END;
                  COMMIT;
                UNTIL recJournal_L.NEXT = 0;
              END;
            // --> 20150521-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2951-FHS01
            //BEFORE
            {
            // --> 20150428-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2951-FHS01
            END;
            // <-- 20150428-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2951-FHS01
            }
            //AFTER
            // <-- 20150521-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2951-FHS01
          UNTIL i_L = 35;
        END;

      cduManCon_G.ClearConnector();

      PurgeJournal();
      cduManCon_G.CloseWindow();
    END;

    PROCEDURE RunPendingCCCEvent@8053006();
    VAR
      recJournal_L@8053600 : Record 8053606;
      recJournalUpdate_L@8053601 : Record 8053606;
      recCrmOrganisation_L@8053602 : Record 8053075;
      cduSynchroError_L@8053603 : Codeunit 8053605;
      recJournalSearch_L@8053604 : Record 8053606;
      i_L@8053605 : Integer;
      recJournalLine_L@8053606 : Record 8053607;
      recAutomation_L@8053607 : Automation "{90B5DB41-ED3C-488E-84F5-6647F85F95A2} 1.0:{F313095A-9ADF-3065-9FD4-FD3674235D4D}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class";
      recAutomation2_L@8053000 : Automation "{90B5DB41-ED3C-488E-84F5-6647F85F95A2} 1.0:{F313095A-9ADF-3065-9FD4-FD3674235D4D}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class";
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Traite toutes les demandes qui ne sont pas encore trait'es
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      CodeCompany_G := cduManCon_G.GetCompanyMcCode();

      //Recherche du code de la soci't' actuelle
      recCrmOrganisation_L.RESET;
      recCrmOrganisation_L.SETRANGE("Company Name", COMPANYNAME);
      recCrmOrganisation_L.FINDFIRST;

      i_L := 0;

      //Parcours de toutes les lignes du journal n'ayant pas fini d'^tre trait'es
      recJournal_L.RESET;
      recJournal_L.SETCURRENTKEY(Closed, Type);
      recJournal_L.SETRANGE(Closed, FALSE);
      recJournal_L.SETFILTER(Type, '%1|%2|%3', recJournal_L.Type::ContratCadreCollect, recJournal_L.Type::LigneContratCadreCollect,
        recJournal_L.Type::DetailLigneContratCadreCollect);
      IF recJournal_L.ISEMPTY = FALSE THEN
        // --> 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //IF cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
          //cduManCon_G.GetAutomation(recAutomation_L);
        //AFTER
        IF cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion() AND cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion2() THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.GetAutomation(recAutomation_L);
          cduManCon_G.GetAutomation2(recAutomation2_L);
        // <-- 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
          REPEAT
            i_L += 1;
            CASE i_L OF
              1: recJournal_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournal_L.Type::ContratCadreCollect);
              2: recJournal_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournal_L.Type::LigneContratCadreCollect);
              3: recJournal_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournal_L.Type::DetailLigneContratCadreCollect);
            END;
            IF recJournal_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
              REPEAT
                recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L.Type, recJournal_L."Document No.");

                //Lancement de l'ordre (RunEvent via codeunit externe)
                CLEAR(cduSynchroError_L);
                // --> 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
                //BEFORE
                //cduSynchroError_L.SetCrmConnexion(recAutomation_L);
                //AFTER
                cduSynchroError_L.SetCrmConnexion(recAutomation_L, recAutomation2_L);
                // <-- 20150504-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2968-FHS01
                IF NOT(cduSynchroError_L.RUN(recJournalUpdate_L)) THEN BEGIN
                  recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L.Type, recJournal_L."Document No.");
                  recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text"));
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L.Type, recJournal_L."Document No.");
                  recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" := '';
                END;

                IF recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" <> Text8053698 THEN BEGIN
                  //Mise . jour du journal
                  IF recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" = '' THEN BEGIN
                    recJournalUpdate_L.Closed := TRUE;
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    IF STRPOS(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text", Text8053640) = 1 THEN BEGIN
                      recJournalUpdate_L.Closed := TRUE;
                      recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" := COPYSTR(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text", STRLEN(Text8053640) + 1,
                        STRLEN(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text"));
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      recJournalUpdate_L.Closed := FALSE;
                    END;
                  END;
                  recJournalUpdate_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
                END;
                COMMIT;
              UNTIL recJournal_L.NEXT = 0;
            END;
          UNTIL i_L = 3;
        END;

      cduManCon_G.ClearConnector();

      PurgeJournal();
    END;

    PROCEDURE RunEvent@8053603(recJournal_P@8053601 : Record 8053606) Result_L : Text[250];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Gestion des demandes reÎues du CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

          Result_L := '';

        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::SubFamily : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncSubFamily(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::Specie : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncSpecies(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::Quota : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          // --> 20151013-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01

        recJournal_P.Type::Abaque : BEGIN

    PROCEDURE PurgeJournal@8053641();
    VAR
      recJournal_L@8053601 : Record 8053606;
      recJournalLine_L@8053600 : Record 8053607;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Purge du journal d'export
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      recJournal_L.RESET;
      recJournal_L.SETCURRENTKEY(Closed, Type);
      recJournal_L.SETRANGE(Closed, TRUE);
      recJournal_L.SETRANGE(Release, FALSE);
      recJournal_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournal_L.Type::TPGHeader);
      //suppression des lignes associ'e aux TPG ferm'es
      IF recJournal_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
        REPEAT
          recJournalLine_L.RESET;
          recJournalLine_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournalLine_L.Type::TPGHeader);
          recJournalLine_L.SETFILTER("Document No.", recJournalLine_L."Document No." + '*');
          recJournalLine_L.DELETEALL;

          recJournalLine_L.RESET;
          recJournalLine_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournalLine_L.Type::TPGLine);
          recJournalLine_L.SETFILTER("Document No.", recJournalLine_L."Document No." + '*');
          recJournalLine_L.DELETEALL;
        UNTIL recJournal_L.NEXT = 0;

      recJournal_L.SETRANGE(Type);
      recJournal_L.DELETEALL(TRUE);

      COMMIT;
    END;

    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE TxtToDate@8053619(Txt_P@8053600 : Text[8]) Result_L : Date;
    VAR
      Year_L@8053601 : Integer;
      Month_L@8053602 : Integer;
      Day_L@8053603 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Tranforme un texte en date suivant le format YYYYMMDD
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Txt_P : Texte . tranformer
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Date de retour
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EVALUATE(Year_L, COPYSTR(Txt_P, 1, 4));
      EVALUATE(Month_L, COPYSTR(Txt_P, 5, 2));
      EVALUATE(Day_L, COPYSTR(Txt_P, 7, 2));
      Result_L := DMY2DATE(Day_L, Month_L, Year_L);
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncAccount@8053606(Code_P@8053601 : Text[50];TypeAction_P@8053600 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      recContact_L@8053602 : Record 5050;
      recCustomer_L@8053603 : Record 18;
      recVendor_L@8053604 : Record 23;
      recContBusRel_L@8053605 : Record 5054;
      SyncCustomer_L@8053608 : Boolean;
      SyncVendor_L@8053606 : Boolean;
      EntityName_L@8053607 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053609 : Text[50];
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053610 : Text[50];
      Finded_L@8053611 : Boolean;
      UserGuid_L@8053612 : Text[50];
      recMasterRelation_L@8053613 : Record 8053076;
      recThirdPartyToCreate_L@8053614 : Record 8053600;
      recShipToAddress_L@8053615 : Record 222;
      recUnloading_L@8053616 : Record 8053169;
      recGuid_L@8053617 : Record 8053601;
      UnloadingPlaceCode_L@8053618 : Text[54];
      recGuidUpdate_L@8053619 : Record 8053601;
      GetGuidFromContact_L@8053620 : Boolean;
      Separator_L@8053621 : Text[1];
      CityGuid_L@8053622 : Text[50];
      PostalGuid_L@8053623 : Text[50];
      CountryGuid_L@8053624 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid2_L@8053625 : Text[50];
      ContactGuid_L@8053626 : Text[50];
      recSetup_L@8053627 : Record 8053602;
      recCampaign_L@8053628 : Record 8052991;
      StatusCode_L@1000000000 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un compte (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'account';
      SyncCustomer_L := FALSE;
      SyncVendor_L := FALSE;
      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        recSetup_L.GET;
        Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();
        recContact_L.GET(Code_P);
        IF recContact_L."Contact CRM" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          EXIT;
        END;
        //Si contact de type personne alors pas de synchronisation
        IF recContact_L.Type <> recContact_L.Type::Company THEN
          EXIT;

        IF CodeCompany_G = '' THEN
          CodeCompany_G := cduManCon_G.GetCompanyMcCode();

        recCampaign_L.RESET;
        recCampaign_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Code", "Starting Date", "Ending Date");
        // --> 20150630-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1844-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //recCampaign_L.SETFILTER("Starting Date", '<%1', WORKDATE);
        //recCampaign_L.SETFILTER("Ending Date", '>%1', WORKDATE);
        //AFTER
        recCampaign_L.SETFILTER("Starting Date", '<=%1', WORKDATE);
        recCampaign_L.SETFILTER("Ending Date", '>=%1', WORKDATE);
        // <-- 20150630-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1844-FHS01
        IF recCampaign_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        END;

        recContBusRel_L.RESET;
        recContBusRel_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Link to Table", "Contact No.", "MC Company Code");
        recContBusRel_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recContBusRel_L."Link to Table"::Customer);
        recContBusRel_L.SETRANGE("Contact No.", Code_P);
        recContBusRel_L.SETRANGE("MC Company Code", CodeCompany_G);
        IF recContBusRel_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          recCustomer_L.GET(recContBusRel_L."No.");
          SyncCustomer_L := TRUE;
        END;

        recContBusRel_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recContBusRel_L."Link to Table"::Vendor);
        IF recContBusRel_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          recVendor_L.GET(recContBusRel_L."No.");
          SyncVendor_L := TRUE;
        END;

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053626, recContact_L."No.");
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(recContact_L."Dynamics CRM Id")) THEN
            TxtCrmGuid_L := recContact_L."Dynamics CRM Id";
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053626, recContact_L."No.", recContact_L."Dynamics CRM Id");
        END;

        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            TxtCrmGuid_L := '';
          END;
        END;

        TxtCrmGuid2_L := TxtCrmGuid_L;

        //Recherche du Tiers MS . Cr'' pour retrouver un 'ventuel GUID
        IF cduManCon_G.SearchThirdPartyToCreate(recContact_L."No.", recThirdPartyToCreate_L, CodeCompany_G, '') THEN BEGIN
          //Contact existant
          TxtCrmGuid2_L := recThirdPartyToCreate_L."Dynamics CRM id";
          cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053626, recContact_L."No.", recThirdPartyToCreate_L."Dynamics CRM id");
        END;

        // --> 20151222-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2617-BS01
        StatusCode_L := '';
        // <-- 20151222-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2617-BS01
        PostalGuid_L := '';
        CityGuid_L := '';
        CountryGuid_L := '';
        IF TxtCrmGuid2_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_city_id', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_country_id', '');
          // --> 20151222-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2617-BS01
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '');
          // <-- 20151222-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2617-BS01
          IF cduManCon_G.GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid2_L) THEN BEGIN
            PostalGuid_L := cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id'));
            CityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_city_id'));
            CountryGuid_L := cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_country_id'));
            // --> 20151222-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2617-BS01
            StatusCode_L := cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode);
            // <-- 20151222-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2617-BS01
          END;
        END;

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'name', recContact_L.Name); //Name

        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_synchronizationstate', '175490002'); // mettre Synchronis'
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_young', cduManCon_G.FormatBoolToCrm(recContact_L.IsYoungFarmerCtt(
          recCampaign_L."No."))); //Young Farmer
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'accountnumber', recContact_L."No."); //No.
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'telephone1', recContact_L."Phone No."); //Phone No.
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_shortname', COPYSTR(recContact_L."Search Name", 1, 10)); //Search Name
        //cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'telephone2', recContact_L."Mobile Phone No."); //Extension No.
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'fax', recContact_L."Fax No."); //Fax No.
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'emailaddress1', recContact_L."E-Mail"); //E-Mail
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'websiteurl', recContact_L."Home Page"); //Home Page
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'address1_line1', recContact_L.Address); //Address
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'address1_line2', recContact_L."Address 2"); //Address 2
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'address1_line3', recContact_L.County); //County

        //Il faut faire le lien avec la table de relation
        IF recContact_L."Post Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Post Code
          TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053613, recContact_L."Post Code");
          cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recContact_L."Post Code", TxtEntityGuid_L, recContact_L.FIELDCAPTION("Post Code"));
          IF PostalGuid_L <> TxtEntityGuid_L THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
          END;
        END;

        IF recContact_L.City <> '' THEN BEGIN //City
          TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053612, recContact_L."Post Code" + Separator_L + recContact_L.City);
          cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recContact_L.City, TxtEntityGuid_L, recContact_L.FIELDCAPTION(City));
          IF CityGuid_L <> TxtEntityGuid_L THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_city_id', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
          END;
        END;

        IF recContact_L."Country/Region Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Country/Region Code
          TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053611, recContact_L."Country/Region Code");
          cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recContact_L."Country/Region Code", TxtEntityGuid_L, recContact_L.FIELDCAPTION("Country/Region Code"));
          IF CountryGuid_L <> TxtEntityGuid_L THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_country_id', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
          END;
        END;

        IF recContact_L."Customer Blocked" = recContact_L."Customer Blocked"::" " THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_frozenbalance', '0'); //Customer Not Blocked
        END ELSE BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_frozenbalance', '1'); //Customer Blocked
        END;

        //Les donn'es suivantes viennent du client s'il existe
        IF SyncCustomer_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          IF recCustomer_L."Payment Method Code" = recSetup_L."Account Payment" THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_paymentmethod', '175490000');
          END;
          IF recCustomer_L."Payment Method Code" = recSetup_L."Levy Payment" THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_paymentmethod', '175490001');
          END;

          recContact_L.CALCFIELDS("Balance (LCY)"); //Balance (LCY)
          recContact_L.SETFILTER("Date Filter", '..%1', WORKDATE);
          recContact_L.CALCFIELDS("Balance Due (LCY)"); //Balance (LCY)
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_balancing', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recContact_L."Balance (LCY)"));
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_amoutoutstandingbalancetodate',
            cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recContact_L."Balance Due (LCY)"));
        END;

        //Les donn'es suivantes viennent du fournisseur s'il existe
        IF SyncVendor_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          //Setting-off Allowed
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_authorizationofcompensation', cduManCon_G.FormatBoolToCrm(
            recVendor_L."Setting-off Allowed"));
        END;

        //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
          UserGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053631, USERID);

          Finded_L := FALSE;

          //Recherche du Tiers MS . Cr'' pour retrouver un 'ventuel GUID
          IF cduManCon_G.SearchThirdPartyToCreate(recContact_L."No.", recThirdPartyToCreate_L, CodeCompany_G, '') THEN BEGIN
            //Contact existant
            TxtCrmGuid_L := recThirdPartyToCreate_L."Dynamics CRM id";
            Finded_L := cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END;

          IF Finded_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_potentialpig', '175490002');      //Ne sait pas
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_potentialplant', '175490002');    //Ne sait pas
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_potentialpoultry', '175490002');  //Ne sait pas
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_potentialrabbit', '175490002');   //Ne sait pas
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_potentialruminant', '175490002'); //Ne sait pas
            Finded_L := cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END;

          IF Finded_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            //on remplit le GUID sur le contact
            //"Dynamics CRM Id"
            recContact_L."Dynamics CRM Id" := cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(TxtCrmGuid_L);
            recContact_L.MODIFY;

            //on remplit le GUID sur le client
            IF SyncCustomer_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN //"Dynamics CRM Id"
              IF ISNULLGUID(recCustomer_L."Dynamics CRM Id") THEN BEGIN
                recCustomer_L."Dynamics CRM Id" := cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(TxtCrmGuid_L);
                recCustomer_L.MODIFY;
              END;
            END;

            //on remplit le GUID sur le fournisseur
            IF SyncVendor_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              IF ISNULLGUID(recVendor_L."Dynamics CRM Id") THEN BEGIN //"Dynamics CRM Id"
                recVendor_L."Dynamics CRM Id" := cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(TxtCrmGuid_L);
                recVendor_L.MODIFY;
              END;
            END;

            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053626, recContact_L."No.", TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //Mise .  jour r'ussie
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053626, recContact_L."No.", TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Mise . jour 'chou'e
          END;
        END;


        // --> 20151222-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2617-BS01
        IF StatusCode_L <> '2' THEN // On ne synchronise pas le statut si le compte est d'sactiv' dans la CRM
        // <-- 20151222-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2617-BS01
        CASE recContact_L."Activity State" OF
          recContact_L."Activity State"::Active: BEGIN //Actif
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
          END;

          recContact_L."Activity State"::"Active Without Activity": BEGIN //Actif sans activit'
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 175490000); //Active - Actif sans activit'
          END;

          recContact_L."Activity State"::"Not Active": BEGIN //Non actif
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 2); // Inactive - Inactive
          END;
        END;

        IF recCustomer_L."No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
          //Mise . jour de l'adresse du contact/client
          cduManCon_G.AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::"Ship-to Address", recCustomer_L."No.", TextModify, COMPANYNAME);
          cduManCon_G.AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::"Unloading Place", recCustomer_L."No.", TextModify, COMPANYNAME);

          //Synchronisation des adresses de livraison du client
          recShipToAddress_L.RESET;
          recShipToAddress_L.SETRANGE("Customer No.", recCustomer_L."No.");
          IF recShipToAddress_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
            REPEAT
              cduManCon_G.AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::"Ship-to Address", recCustomer_L."No." + Separator_L +
                recShipToAddress_L.Code, TextModify, COMPANYNAME);

              //Synchronisation des lieux de d'chargement
              recUnloading_L.RESET;
              recUnloading_L.SETRANGE("Customer No.", recShipToAddress_L."Customer No.");
              recUnloading_L.SETRANGE("Ship-to Code", recShipToAddress_L.Code);
              IF recUnloading_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
                REPEAT
                  cduManCon_G.AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::"Unloading Place", recCustomer_L."No." + Separator_L +
                    recShipToAddress_L.Code + Separator_L + recUnloading_L.Code, TextModify, COMPANYNAME);
                UNTIL recUnloading_L.NEXT = 0;

              recGuid_L.RESET;
              recGuid_L.SETRANGE("Type Data", recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Unloading Place");
              recGuid_L.SETFILTER("Code Nav", recShipToAddress_L."Customer No." + Separator_L + recShipToAddress_L.Code +
                Separator_L + '*');
              IF recGuid_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
                REPEAT
                  UnloadingPlaceCode_L := COPYSTR(recGuid_L."Code Nav", STRPOS(recGuid_L."Code Nav", Separator_L) + 1,
                    STRLEN(recGuid_L."Code Nav"));
                  UnloadingPlaceCode_L := COPYSTR(UnloadingPlaceCode_L, STRPOS(UnloadingPlaceCode_L, Separator_L) + 1,
                    STRLEN(UnloadingPlaceCode_L));
                  IF NOT(recUnloading_L.GET(recShipToAddress_L."Customer No.", recShipToAddress_L.Code, UnloadingPlaceCode_L))
                  THEN BEGIN
                    cduManCon_G.SetState('cmc_locationunloading', recGuid_L.GUID, 2);
                    recGuidUpdate_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data", recGuid_L."Code Nav");
                    recGuidUpdate_L.DELETE;
                  END;
                UNTIL recGuid_L.NEXT = 0;

            UNTIL recShipToAddress_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        ContactGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053626, Code_P);
        //supression du contact(Nav)/Compte(CRM) ?
        IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(ContactGuid_L)) THEN
          cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, ContactGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::Account, 2, 1);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncOrderHeader@8053607(Code_P@8053601 : Text[50];TypeAction_P@8053600 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      recJrnlLine_L@8053602 : Record 8053607;
      recGuid_L@8053603 : Record 8053601;
      ModifyDoc_L@8053604 : Boolean;
      OrderGuid_L@8053605 : Text[50];
      StatusCode_L@8053606 : Integer;
      recJrnlLine2_L@8053607 : Record 8053607;
      LineCode_L@8053608 : Text[50];
      EntityName_L@8053609 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053610 : Text[50];
      UpdateSimpleOrder_L@8053611 : Boolean;
      OrderNumber_L@8053612 : Code[20];
      GuidSimpleOrder_L@8053613 : Text[50];
      Count_L@8053614 : Integer;
      TxtSimpleOrderGuid_L@8053615 : Text[50];
      ShiptToGuid_L@8053616 : Text[50];
      recJrn_L@8053617 : Record 8053607;
      TxtAccountGuid_L@8053618 : Text[50];
      recContact_L@8053619 : Record 5050;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'une commande valid'e (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_validorder';

      //Recherche des donn'es . envoyer pour mettre . jour la commande
      recJrnlLine_L.RESET;
      recJrnlLine_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJrnlLine_L.Type::Order);
      recJrnlLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", Code_P);
      recJrnlLine_L.SETRANGE(Entity, EntityName_L);
      recJrnlLine_L.SETRANGE(Code, '');
      recJrnlLine_L.SETFILTER(Field, '<>%1', 'statuscode');
      IF recJrnlLine_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
        OrderNumber_L := '';
        recGuid_L.RESET;
        recGuid_L.SETRANGE("Type Data", recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Simple Order Header");
        recGuid_L.SETRANGE("Code Nav", Code_P);
        IF recGuid_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          GuidSimpleOrder_L := recGuid_L.GUID;
          EntityName_L := 'salesorder';
          cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          IF cduManCon_G.ExistRecord(EntityName_L, GuidSimpleOrder_L) THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'ordernumber', '');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
            IF cduManCon_G.GetRecord(EntityName_L, GuidSimpleOrder_L) THEN BEGIN
              OrderNumber_L := COPYSTR(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'ordernumber'), 1, MAXSTRLEN(OrderNumber_L));
              TxtSimpleOrderGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id');
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            GuidSimpleOrder_L := '';
          END;
        END;

        //Recherche de l'adresse par d'faut du client
        IF recJrn_L.GET(recJrnlLine_L.Type, recJrnlLine_L."Document No.", recJrnlLine_L.Entity, recJrnlLine_L.Code,
          'cmc_account')
        THEN BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_additionaladdress';

          cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
          IF STRLEN(recJrn_L.Data) < 30 THEN BEGIN
            TxtAccountGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053626, recJrn_L.Data);
            IF TxtAccountGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
              recContact_L.GET(COPYSTR(recJrn_L.Data, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recContact_L."No.")));
              IF recContact_L."Contact CRM" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
                EXIT;
              END;
              IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(recContact_L."Dynamics CRM Id")) THEN
                TxtAccountGuid_L := recContact_L."Dynamics CRM Id";
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            TxtAccountGuid_L := recJrn_L.Data;
          END;
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_account_id', '=', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtAccountGuid_L));
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '=', '175490003'); //adresse principale
          IF cduManCon_G.SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L) > 0 THEN BEGIN
            ShiptToGuid_L := cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'));
          END;
        END;

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        EntityName_L := 'cmc_validorder';

        //pr'paration du code statut
        recJrnlLine2_L.GET(recJrnlLine_L.Type::Order, Code_P, EntityName_L, '', 'statuscode');
        EVALUATE(StatusCode_L, recJrnlLine2_L.Data);

        //recherche si la commande existe d'j. dans le CRM
        ModifyDoc_L := FALSE;
        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        IF recGuid_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Order Header", Code_P) THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.ExistRecord(EntityName_L, recGuid_L.GUID) THEN
            //Commande trouv'e
            ModifyDoc_L := TRUE;
            OrderGuid_L := recGuid_L.GUID;
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, OrderGuid_L, 1);
        END;

        IF ModifyDoc_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_ordernumber', '=', Code_P);
          Count_L := cduManCon_G.SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
          IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
            OrderGuid_L := cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'));
            ModifyDoc_L := TRUE;
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, OrderGuid_L, 1);
          END;
        END;

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_orderreference', OrderNumber_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_salesorder_id', TxtSimpleOrderGuid_L);
        REPEAT
          //envoie des donn'es
          IF recJrnlLine_L.Field = TextAccount THEN BEGIN
            IF STRLEN(recJrnlLine_L.Data) < 30 THEN BEGIN
              TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053626, recJrnlLine_L.Data);
              IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
                recJrnlLine_L.Data := cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCrmGuid_L);
                recJrnlLine_L.MODIFY;
              END ELSE BEGIN
                cduManCon_G.AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Account, recJrnlLine_L.Data, TextModify, '');
                COMMIT;
                cduManCon_G.ClearConnector();
                ERROR(Text8053674, recJrnlLine_L.Data);
              END;
            END;
          END;
          IF recJrnlLine_L.Field = 'cmc_deliveryaddress' THEN BEGIN
            TxtCrmGuid_L := '';
            IF STRLEN(recJrnlLine_L.Data) < 30 THEN BEGIN
              TxtCrmGuid_L := ShiptToGuid_L;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF ISNULLGUID(cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(recJrnlLine_L.Data)) THEN BEGIN
                TxtCrmGuid_L := ShiptToGuid_L;
              END;
            END;
            IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(TxtCrmGuid_L))) THEN BEGIN
                recJrnlLine_L.Data := cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCrmGuid_L);
                recJrnlLine_L.MODIFY;
              END;
            END
          END;

          IF recJrnlLine_L.Data <> TextNullGuid THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, recJrnlLine_L.Field, recJrnlLine_L.Data);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, recJrnlLine_L.Field, '#NULL#');
          END;
        UNTIL recJrnlLine_L.NEXT = 0;
        IF ModifyDoc_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, OrderGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.UpdateGuid(Text8053624, Code_P, OrderGuid_L);
            TxtCrmGuid_L := OrderGuid_L;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, OrderGuid_L);
        END;

        LineCode_L := '';

        EntityName_L := 'cmc_validorderline';
        //Suppression des toutes les lignes de la commande dans le CRM
        //recherche des lignes de la commande
        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        //filtrage pour ne r'cup'rer que les lignes de la commande
        cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_validorder', '=', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(OrderGuid_L));
        //suppression de toutes les lignes
        cduManCon_G.DeleteMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);

        recGuid_L.RESET;
        recGuid_L.SETRANGE("Type Data", recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Order Line");
        recGuid_L.SETFILTER("Code Nav", Code_P + cduManCon_G.GetSeparator() + '*');
        recGuid_L.DELETEALL(TRUE);

        //Mise . jour ou ajout des lignes associ'es
        recJrnlLine_L.RESET;
        recJrnlLine_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJrnlLine_L.Type::Order);
        recJrnlLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", Code_P);
        recJrnlLine_L.SETRANGE(Entity, 'cmc_validorderline');
        recJrnlLine_L.SETFILTER(Field, '<>%1', 'statuscode');
        IF recJrnlLine_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            //synchronisation des lignes
            IF LineCode_L <> recJrnlLine_L.Code THEN BEGIN
              LineCode_L := recJrnlLine_L.Code;
              SyncOrderLine(Code_P, recJrnlLine_L.Code, OrderGuid_L, ShiptToGuid_L);
            END;
          UNTIL recJrnlLine_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncOrderLine@8053623(Code_P@8053601 : Text[50];LineCode_P@8053600 : Text[50];OrderHeaderGuid_P@8053607 : Text[50];ShiptToGuid_P@8053610 : Text[50]);
    VAR
      recJrnlLine_L@8053603 : Record 8053607;
      recGuid_L@8053602 : Record 8053601;
      recJrnlLine2_L@8053604 : Record 8053607;
      StatusCode_L@8053605 : Integer;
      LineGuid_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      ModifyDoc_L@8053608 : Boolean;
      EntityName_L@8053609 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053611 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation des lignes d'une commande valid'e (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //    - OrderHeaderGuid_P : GUID de l'ent^te de commande
      //    - ShiptToGuid_P : Adresse de livraison du client par d'faut
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //Recherche des donn'es . envoyer pour mettre . jour la commande
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_validorderline';

      ModifyDoc_L := FALSE;
      cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
      IF recGuid_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Order Line", Code_P + cduManCon_G.GetSeparator() + LineCode_P) THEN BEGIN
        IF cduManCon_G.ExistRecord(recJrnlLine_L.Entity, recGuid_L.GUID) THEN BEGIN
          //Commande trouv'e
          ModifyDoc_L := TRUE;
          LineGuid_L := recGuid_L.GUID;
        END;
      END;

      recJrnlLine_L.RESET;
      recJrnlLine_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJrnlLine_L.Type::Order);
      recJrnlLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", Code_P);
      recJrnlLine_L.SETRANGE(Entity, EntityName_L);
      recJrnlLine_L.SETRANGE(Code, LineCode_P);
      recJrnlLine_L.SETFILTER(Field, '<>%1', 'statuscode');
      IF recJrnlLine_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
        //pr'paration du code statut
        recJrnlLine2_L.GET(recJrnlLine_L.Type::Order, Code_P, EntityName_L, LineCode_P, 'statuscode');
        EVALUATE(StatusCode_L, recJrnlLine2_L.Data);
        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_validorder', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(OrderHeaderGuid_P));
        REPEAT
          //envoie des donn'es
          IF recJrnlLine_L.Field = 'cmc_addditionaladdress' THEN BEGIN
            TxtCrmGuid_L := '';
            IF STRLEN(recJrnlLine_L.Data) < 30 THEN BEGIN
              TxtCrmGuid_L := ShiptToGuid_P;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF ISNULLGUID(cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(recJrnlLine_L.Data)) THEN BEGIN
                TxtCrmGuid_L := ShiptToGuid_P;
              END;
            END;
            IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(TxtCrmGuid_L))) THEN BEGIN
                recJrnlLine_L.Data := cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCrmGuid_L);
                recJrnlLine_L.MODIFY;
              END;
            END
          END;

          IF recJrnlLine_L.Data <> TextNullGuid THEN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, recJrnlLine_L.Field, recJrnlLine_L.Data);
        UNTIL recJrnlLine_L.NEXT = 0;
        //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
        IF ModifyDoc_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          //Cr'ation de la donn'e dans le CRM
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, LineGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.UpdateGuid(Text8053625, Code_P, LineGuid_L);
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, LineGuid_L);
        END;

        //Mise . jour du statut
        cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, LineGuid_L, StatusCode_L);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncItem@8053609(Code_P@8053601 : Text[50];TypeAction_P@8053600 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      recItem_L@8053603 : Record 27;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053604 : Text[50];
      recGuid_L@8053000 : Record 8053601;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un article (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'product';

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        IF STRPOS(Code_P, cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator) > 0 THEN BEGIN
          IF NOT(recItem_L.GET(COPYSTR(Code_P, 1, STRPOS(Code_P, cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator()) - 1))) THEN
            EXIT;
        END ELSE
          IF NOT(recItem_L.GET(Code_P)) THEN
            EXIT;
        cduManCon_G.SyncItemVariant(recItem_L);
      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression de l'article
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, Code_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::Item, 2, 1);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncAddress@8053610(Code_P@8053604 : Text[50];TypeAction_P@8053601 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053600 : Text[50];
      CustomerCode_L@8053606 : Code[20];
      AddressCode_L@8053605 : Code[10];
      recCustomer_L@8053607 : Record 18;
      recAddress_L@8053608 : Record 222;
      TypeAddress_L@8053609 : Integer;
      GuidAddress_L@8053610 : Text[50];
      recGuid_L@8053611 : Record 8053601;
      Separator_L@8053612 : Text[1];
      recContactRelation_L@1000000002 : Record 5054;
      recContact_L@1000000000 : Record 5050;
      ContactFinded_L@1000000001 : Boolean;
      TxtCrmAccountGuid_L@1000000003 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'une adresse (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_additionaladdress';
      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();
      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

        //R'cup'ration du code client et du code de l'adresse
        IF STRPOS(Code_P, Separator_L) > 0 THEN BEGIN
          CustomerCode_L := COPYSTR(COPYSTR(Code_P, 1, STRPOS(Code_P, Separator_L) - 1), 1, MAXSTRLEN(CustomerCode_L));
          AddressCode_L := COPYSTR(Code_P, STRPOS(Code_P, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(AddressCode_L));
          IF NOT(recAddress_L.GET(CustomerCode_L, AddressCode_L)) THEN
            EXIT;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          CustomerCode_L := COPYSTR(Code_P, 1, MAXSTRLEN(CustomerCode_L));
          AddressCode_L := '';
        END;

        IF NOT(recCustomer_L.GET(CustomerCode_L)) THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.ClearConnector();
          ERROR(TextError8053600, CustomerCode_L, recCustomer_L.TABLECAPTION);
        END;

        //Recherche du contact li' au client
        ContactFinded_L := FALSE;
        recContactRelation_L.RESET;
        recContactRelation_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Link to Table", "No.", "MC Company Code");
        recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Customer);
        recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("No.", recCustomer_L."No.");
        recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("MC Company Code", cduManCon_G.GetCompanyMcCode());
        IF recContactRelation_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          // --> 20150526-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3010-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //recContact_L.GET(recContactRelation_L."No.");
          //AFTER
          recContact_L.GET(recContactRelation_L."Contact No.");
          // <-- 20150526-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3010-FHS01
          IF (recContact_L."Contact CRM" = FALSE) OR (recContact_L.Type <> recContact_L.Type::Company) THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;

          //R'cup'ration du GUID
          TxtCrmAccountGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053626, recContact_L."No.");
          IF TxtCrmAccountGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmAccountGuid_L)) THEN
              ContactFinded_L := TRUE;
          END;
        // --> 20150526-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3010-FHS01
        END ELSE BEGIN
          // pas de relation trouv' = pas de synchro
          EXIT;
        // <-- 20150526-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-3010-FHS01
        END;

        TypeAddress_L := 1;
        IF AddressCode_L = '' THEN BEGIN
          EXIT;
          cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
          IF ContactFinded_L = TRUE THEN
            cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_account_id', '=', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCrmAccountGuid_L))
          ELSE
            cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_account_id', '=', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(recCustomer_L."Dynamics CRM Id")
      );
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '=', '175490003'); //adresse principale
          IF cduManCon_G.SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L) > 0 THEN BEGIN
            GuidAddress_L := cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id');
            cduManCon_G.UpdateGuid(Text8053635, recCustomer_L."No.", cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(GuidAddress_L));
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '=', recAddress_L.Name); //Name
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line1', '=', recAddress_L.Address); //Address
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line2', '=', recAddress_L."Address 2"); //Address 2
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line3', '=', recAddress_L.County); //County

          //Il faut faire le lien avec la table de relation
          IF recAddress_L."Post Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Post Code
            TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053613, recAddress_L."Post Code");
            cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id', '=', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
          END;

          IF recAddress_L.City <> '' THEN BEGIN //City
            TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053612, recAddress_L."Post Code" + Separator_L +
              recAddress_L.City);
            cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_city_id', '=', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
          END;

          IF recAddress_L."Country/Region Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Country/Region Code
            TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053611, recAddress_L."Country/Region Code");
            cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_country_id', '=', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
          END;

          CASE TypeAddress_L OF
            1:  cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '=', '175490000'); //livraison
            2:  cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '=', '175490001'); //facturation
            3:  cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '=', '175490002'); //exp'dition
          END;

          IF cduManCon_G.SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L) > 0 THEN BEGIN
            GuidAddress_L := cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id');
            cduManCon_G.UpdateGuid(Text8053635, recCustomer_L."No.", cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(GuidAddress_L));
          END;
        END;

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        IF ContactFinded_L = TRUE THEN
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_account_id', TxtCrmAccountGuid_L)
        ELSE
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_account_id', recCustomer_L."Dynamics CRM Id");

        IF AddressCode_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recAddress_L.Name); //Name
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line1', recAddress_L.Address); //Address
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line2', recAddress_L."Address 2"); //Address 2
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line3', recAddress_L.County); //County

          //Il faut faire le lien avec la table de relation
          IF recAddress_L."Post Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Post Code
            TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053613, recAddress_L."Post Code");
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id', TxtEntityGuid_L);
          END;

          IF recAddress_L.City <> '' THEN BEGIN //City
            TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053612, recAddress_L."Post Code" + Separator_L +
              recAddress_L.City);
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_city_id', TxtEntityGuid_L);
          END;

          IF recAddress_L."Country/Region Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Country/Region Code
            TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053611, recAddress_L."Country/Region Code");
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_country_id', TxtEntityGuid_L);
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recCustomer_L.Name); //Name
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line1', recCustomer_L.Address); //Address
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line2', recCustomer_L."Address 2"); //Address 2
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_line3', recCustomer_L.County); //County

          //Il faut faire le lien avec la table de relation
          IF recCustomer_L."Post Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Post Code
            TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053613, recCustomer_L."Post Code");
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id', TxtEntityGuid_L);
          END;

          IF recCustomer_L.City <> '' THEN BEGIN //City
            TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053612, recCustomer_L."Post Code" + Separator_L +
              recCustomer_L.City);
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_city_id', TxtEntityGuid_L);
          END;

          IF recCustomer_L."Country/Region Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Country
            TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053611, recCustomer_L."Country/Region Code");
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_country_id', TxtEntityGuid_L);
          END;
        END;

        CASE TypeAddress_L OF
          0:  cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '175490003'); //adresse principale
          1:  cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '175490000'); //livraison
          2:  cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '175490001'); //facturation
          3:  cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '175490002'); //exp'dition
        END;

        //Recherche du GUID de l'adresse de livraison
        IF TypeAddress_L = 0 THEN
          GuidAddress_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053635, Code_P)
        ELSE
          GuidAddress_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053636, Code_P);

        //mise . jour ou cr'ation de l'adresse
        IF GuidAddress_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.ExistRecord(EntityName_L, GuidAddress_L)) THEN
            GuidAddress_L := '';
        END;

        IF GuidAddress_L = '' THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, GuidAddress_L) THEN BEGIN
            IF TypeAddress_L = 0 THEN BEGIN
              cduManCon_G.UpdateGuid(Text8053635, recCustomer_L."No.", GuidAddress_L);
            END ELSE BEGIN
              cduManCon_G.UpdateGuid(Text8053636, recCustomer_L."No." + Separator_L + recAddress_L.Code, GuidAddress_L);
            END;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, GuidAddress_L) THEN BEGIN

          END;
        END;
      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression de l'article
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        IF STRPOS(Code_P, Separator_L) > 0 THEN
          TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053636, Code_P)
        ELSE
          TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053635, Code_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            IF STRPOS(Code_P, Separator_L) > 0 THEN
              cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Address Ship-to", 2, 1)
            ELSE
              cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::Address, 2, 1);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncUnloadingPlace@8053611(Code_P@8053601 : Text[50];TypeAction_P@8053600 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      recUnloadingPlace_L@8053603 : Record 8053169;
      recUnloadingMethod_L@8053606 : Record 8053168;
      recUnloadingVehSize_L@8053605 : Record 8053166;
      recUnloadingTime_L@8053604 : Record 8053167;
      GuidMethod_L@8053609 : Text[50];
      GuidSize_L@8053608 : Text[50];
      GuidTime_L@8053607 : Text[50];
      AddressGuid_L@8053610 : Text[50];
      TxtGuid_L@8053611 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053612 : Text[50];
      recGuid_L@8053613 : Record 8053601;
      CustomerCode_L@8053614 : Text[20];
      AddressCode_L@8053615 : Text[10];
      ThisCode_L@8053616 : Text[10];
      Code_L@8053617 : Text[50];
      AddrLivraison_L@8053618 : Boolean;
      Separator_L@8053619 : Text[1];
      recContact_L@8053000 : Record 5050;
      cduContactManag_L@8053001 : Codeunit 8053377;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un lieu de d'chargement (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_locationunloading';
      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        IF STRPOS(Code_P, Separator_L) <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          AddrLivraison_L := TRUE;
          CustomerCode_L := COPYSTR(COPYSTR(Code_P, 1, STRPOS(Code_P, Separator_L) - 1), 1, MAXSTRLEN(CustomerCode_L));
          Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_P, STRPOS(Code_P, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
          AddressCode_L := COPYSTR(COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1), 1, MAXSTRLEN(AddressCode_L));
          ThisCode_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(ThisCode_L));
        END ELSE BEGIN
          AddrLivraison_L := FALSE;
          CustomerCode_L := Code_P;
        END;

        IF recContact_L.GET(cduContactManag_L.GetContNoFromCustVentNo(CustomerCode_L, 0)) THEN BEGIN
          IF recContact_L."Contact CRM" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;
        END;

        IF (AddrLivraison_L = TRUE) AND (recUnloadingPlace_L.GET(CustomerCode_L, AddressCode_L, ThisCode_L)) THEN BEGIN
          //synchroniser les entit's li'es :
          IF recUnloadingPlace_L."Unloading Method Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Code mode d'chargement
            recUnloadingMethod_L.GET(recUnloadingPlace_L."Unloading Method Code");
            GuidMethod_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053641, recUnloadingPlace_L."Unloading Method Code");
            IF cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(GuidMethod_L) THEN BEGIN
              SyncUnloadingMethod(recUnloadingMethod_L.Code, TextModify);
              GuidMethod_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053641, recUnloadingPlace_L."Unloading Method Code");
            END;
          END;

          IF recUnloadingPlace_L."Vehicle Size Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Code gabarit v'hicule
            recUnloadingVehSize_L.GET(recUnloadingPlace_L."Vehicle Size Code");
            GuidSize_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053642, recUnloadingPlace_L."Vehicle Size Code");
            IF cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(GuidSize_L) THEN BEGIN
              SyncUnloadingSize(recUnloadingPlace_L."Vehicle Size Code", TextModify);
              GuidSize_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053642, recUnloadingPlace_L."Vehicle Size Code");
            END;
          END;

          IF recUnloadingPlace_L."Time Slot Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Code tranche Horaire
            recUnloadingTime_L.GET(recUnloadingPlace_L."Time Slot Code");
            GuidTime_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053643, recUnloadingPlace_L."Time Slot Code");
            IF cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(GuidTime_L) THEN BEGIN
              SyncUnloadingTime(recUnloadingPlace_L."Time Slot Code", TextModify);
              GuidTime_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053643, recUnloadingPlace_L."Time Slot Code");
            END;
          END;
        END;

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

        IF AddrLivraison_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recUnloadingPlace_L.Code); //Code
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recUnloadingPlace_L.Description); //Description
          AddressGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053636, recUnloadingPlace_L."Customer No." + cduManCon_G.GetSeparator +
                                               recUnloadingPlace_L."Ship-to Code");
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmode_id', GuidMethod_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_maxsize_id', GuidSize_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_schedule_id', GuidTime_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdress_id', AddressGuid_L);
          TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053637, recUnloadingPlace_L."Customer No." + Separator_L +
            recUnloadingPlace_L."Ship-to Code" +  Separator_L + recUnloadingPlace_L.Code);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', CustomerCode_L); //Code
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', Text8053679); //Description
          AddressGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053635, CustomerCode_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdress_id', AddressGuid_L);

          TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053637, recUnloadingPlace_L."Customer No.");
        END;

        //mise . jour ou cr'ation de l'adresse
        IF TxtGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L)) THEN
            TxtGuid_L := '';
        END;

        IF TxtGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN
            IF AddrLivraison_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              cduManCon_G.UpdateGuid(Text8053637, recUnloadingPlace_L."Customer No." + Separator_L +
                recUnloadingPlace_L."Ship-to Code" + Separator_L + recUnloadingPlace_L.Code, TxtGuid_L);
            END ELSE BEGIN
              cduManCon_G.UpdateGuid(Text8053637, CustomerCode_L, TxtGuid_L);
            END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          END;
        END;
      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression de l'article
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053637, Code_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Unloading Place", 2, 1);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncUnloadingMethod@8053612(Code_P@8053601 : Text[50];TypeAction_P@8053600 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      TxtGuid_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      recUnloadingMethod_L@8053604 : Record 8053168;
      recGuid_L@8053605 : Record 8053601;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'une m'thode de d'chargement (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_unloadingmodality';

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        recUnloadingMethod_L.GET(Code_P);

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '');
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalitytype', ''); //Unloading method
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'statuscode', ''); //permet de v'rifier s'il est toujours actif

        TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053641, Code_P);

        //mise . jour ou cr'ation
        IF TxtGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          // --> 20150402-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2884-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //IF NOT(cduManCon_G.ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
          //  TxtGuid_L := '';
          //END ELSE BEGIN
          //AFTER
          // <-- 20150402-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2884-FHS01
            IF cduManCon_G.GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
              // --> 20150402-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2884-FHS01
              //BEFORE
              //IF (cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code') <> Code_P) OR
              //   (cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name') <> recUnloadingMethod_L.Description) OR
              //   (cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmadolitytype') <> '175490002') OR
              //   (cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'statuscode') <> '1')
              //AFTER
              IF (cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'statuscode') <> '1')
              // <-- 20150402-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2884-FHS01
              THEN
                TxtGuid_L := '';
            END ELSE
              TxtGuid_L := '';
          // --> 20150402-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2884-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //END;
          //AFTER
          // <-- 20150402-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2884-FHS01
        END;

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', Code_P);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recUnloadingMethod_L.Description);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unloadingmodalitytype', '175490002'); //Unloading method

        IF TxtGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN
              cduManCon_G.UpdateGuid(Text8053641, Code_P, TxtGuid_L);
        END ELSE
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          END;
      END;

      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053641, Code_P);
        IF TxtGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtGuid_L)) THEN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Unloading Method", 2, 1);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncUnloadingSize@8053613(Code_P@8053601 : Text[50];TypeAction_P@8053600 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      TxtGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      EntityName_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      recGuid_L@8053604 : Record 8053601;
      recUnloadingVehSize_L@8053605 : Record 8053166;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un gabarit de d'chargement (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action

          //AFTER
          // <-- 20150402-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2884-FHS01

        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recSpecies_L.Name);
        // <-- 20150625-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-FHS01
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_subfamily', txtGuidSubFamily_L);

        IF TxtGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN
              cduManCon_G.UpdateGuid(Text8053651, Code_P, TxtGuid_L);
        END ELSE
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          END;
      END;
