      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053651, Code_P);
        IF TxtGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtGuid_L)) THEN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::Species, 2, 1);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncHistory@8053616();
    VAR
      recEntry_L@8053600 : Record 5802;
      recConnectorSetup_L@8053601 : Record 8053602;
      cduCatchError_L@8053602 : Codeunit 8053608;
      recAutomation_L@8053000 : Automation "{90B5DB41-ED3C-488E-84F5-6647F85F95A2} 1.0:{F313095A-9ADF-3065-9FD4-FD3674235D4D}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class";
      RecSalesLine_L@1000000000 : Record 37;
      RecGUID2_L@1000000001 : Record 8053609;
      Code_L@1000000002 : Code[50];
      Separator_L@1000000003 : Code[10];
      cduCatchError2_L@1000000004 : Codeunit 8053608;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation des historiques de ventes et achats (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      recConnectorSetup_L.GET();

      IF NOT(cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion()) THEN BEGIN
        cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
      END;
      cduManCon_G.GetAutomation(recAutomation_L);

      //Recherche s'il y a de nouvelle 'criture . envoyer de type appro
      recEntry_L.RESET;
      recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Entry No.", '>%1', recConnectorSetup_L.LastEntryApproHistorySended);
      recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Source Type", '%1|%2', recEntry_L."Source Type"::Customer, recEntry_L."Source Type"::Vendor);
      recEntry_L.SETRANGE("Item Ledger Entry Type", recEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry Type"::Sale);
      recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Document Type", '%1|%2', recEntry_L."Document Type"::"Sales Invoice",
        recEntry_L."Document Type"::"Sales Credit Memo");
      IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryActivity <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Activity Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryActivity);
      END;

      IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryFamily <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryFamily);
      END;

      IF recConnectorSetup_L."EntryApproHistorySub-Family" <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Sub-Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L."EntryApproHistorySub-Family");
      END;

      recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorApproHistory := '';
      IF recEntry_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          // --> 20180222-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-BSI01
          {before
          // --> 20160921-WI00-ADV01.01.00-20871-BSI01
          IF (recEntry_L."Invoiced Quantity" <> 0) AND (recEntry_L."Sales Amount (Actual)" <> 0) THEN BEGIN
          // <-- 20160921-WI00-ADV01.01.00-20871-BSI01
          after}
          IF (recEntry_L."Invoiced Quantity" <> 0) OR (recEntry_L."Sales Amount (Actual)" <> 0) THEN BEGIN
          // <-- 20180222-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-BSI01

          cduCatchError_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::HistoryAppro, recAutomation_L, recAutomation_L);
          cduCatchError_L.SetParameterHistory(recEntry_L);
          IF cduCatchError_L.RUN() THEN BEGIN
            recConnectorSetup_L.LastEntryApproHistorySended := recEntry_L."Entry No.";
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //erreur : on sauvegarde ce que l'on a pu faire
            recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorApproHistory := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorApproHistory));
            recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
            COMMIT;
            recEntry_L.FINDLAST;
          END;
          // <-- 20160921-WI00-ADV01.01.00-20871-BSI01
          END;
          // --> 20160921-WI00-ADV01.01.00-20871-BSI01
        UNTIL recEntry_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
      recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
      COMMIT;


      // --> 20160603-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2786-BSI01
      Code_L := '';
      // --> 20161013-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22362-BSI01
      Separator_L := '';
      // <-- 20161013-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22362-BSI01
      RecSalesLine_L.RESET;
      RecSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",RecSalesLine_L."Document Type"::Order);
      RecSalesLine_L.SETRANGE(Type,RecSalesLine_L.Type::Item);
      IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryActivity <> '' THEN BEGIN
        RecSalesLine_L.SETFILTER("Activity Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryActivity);
      END;
      IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryFamily <> '' THEN BEGIN
        RecSalesLine_L.SETFILTER("Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryFamily);
      END;
      IF recConnectorSetup_L."EntryApproHistorySub-Family" <> '' THEN BEGIN
        RecSalesLine_L.SETFILTER("Sub-Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L."EntryApproHistorySub-Family");
      END;
      recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorHistorySalesLine := '';
      // --> 20161013-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22362-BSI01
      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator;
      // <-- 20161013-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22362-BSI01
      IF RecSalesLine_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
        REPEAT
          Code_L := RecSalesLine_L."Document No." + Separator_L + FORMAT(RecSalesLine_L."Line No.");
          IF RecSalesLine_L.Quantity <> RecSalesLine_L."Quantity Invoiced" THEN BEGIN
            IF (NOT RecGUID2_L.GET(RecGUID2_L."Type Data"::HistorySalesLine,Code_L)) OR
               (RecGUID2_L.GET(RecGUID2_L."Type Data"::HistorySalesLine,Code_L) AND RecGUID2_L."Sale Line Modified")
            THEN BEGIN
              cduCatchError_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::HistorySalesLine, recAutomation_L, recAutomation_L);
              cduCatchError_L.SetParameterHistorySalesLine(RecSalesLine_L);
              // --> 20170612-WI00-ADV01.01.00-25019-BSI01
              cduCatchError_L.SetDeleteMissingOrderLines(FALSE);
              // <-- 20170612-WI00-ADV01.01.00-25019-BSI01
              // --> 20161013-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22362-BSI01
              COMMIT;
              // <-- 20161013-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22362-BSI01
              IF cduCatchError_L.RUN() THEN BEGIN
                recConnectorSetup_L."Last Sales Order Sended" := RecSalesLine_L."Document No.";
                recConnectorSetup_L."Last Sales Order Line Sended" := RecSalesLine_L."Line No.";
                // --> 20170720-WI00-ADV01.01.00-25019-BSI01
                IF (RecGUID2_L.GET(RecGUID2_L."Type Data"::HistorySalesLine,Code_L)) AND RecGUID2_L."Sale Line Modified" THEN BEGIN
                  RecGUID2_L."Sale Line Modified" := FALSE;
                  RecGUID2_L.MODIFY;
                  COMMIT;
                END;
                // <-- 20170720-WI00-ADV01.01.00-25019-BSI01
              END ELSE BEGIN
                //erreur : on sauvegarde ce que l'on a pu faire
                recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorHistorySalesLine := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT,1,
                                                             MAXSTRLEN(recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorHistorySalesLine));
                recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
                COMMIT;
                RecSalesLine_L.FINDLAST;
              END;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            // --> 20170720-WI00-ADV01.01.00-25019-BSI01
            {before
            // --> 20161013-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22362-BSI01
            IF (RecGUID2_L.GET(RecGUID2_L."Type Data"::HistorySalesLine,Code_L)) AND RecGUID2_L."Sale Line Modified" THEN BEGIN
              RecGUID2_L."Sale Line Modified" := FALSE;
              RecGUID2_L.MODIFY;
              COMMIT;
            END;
            // <-- 20161013-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22362-BSI01
            after}
            // Supprimer les commandes factur'es
            IF RecGUID2_L.GET(RecGUID2_L."Type Data"::HistorySalesLine,Code_L) THEN BEGIN
              IF cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion THEN BEGIN
                cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav('cmc_saleshistory', RecGUID2_L.GUID, RecGUID2_L."Type Data"::HistorySalesLine, 2, 1);
                cduManCon_G.ClearConnector;
              END;
              // --> 20171011-WI00-TER01.01.00-25019-BSI01
              //RecGUID2_L.DELETE;
              // <-- 20171011-WI00-TER01.01.00-25019-BSI01
              COMMIT;
            END;
            // <-- 20170720-WI00-ADV01.01.00-25019-BSI01
          END;
        UNTIL RecSalesLine_L.NEXT = 0;
      recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
      COMMIT;
      // <-- 20160603-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2786-BSI01

      // --> 20170612-WI00-ADV01.01.00-25019-BSI01
      CLEAR(cduCatchError2_L);
      cduCatchError2_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::HistorySalesLine, recAutomation_L, recAutomation_L);
      cduCatchError2_L.SetDeleteMissingOrderLines(TRUE);
      IF NOT cduCatchError2_L.RUN THEN BEGIN
        recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorHistorySalesLine := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT,1,
                                                     MAXSTRLEN(recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorHistorySalesLine));
        recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
        COMMIT;
      END;
      // <-- 20170612-WI00-ADV01.01.00-25019-BSI01

      //Recherche s'il y a de nouvelle 'criture . envoyer de type collect
      recEntry_L.RESET;
      recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Entry No.", '>%1', recConnectorSetup_L.LastEntryCollectHistorySended);
      recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Source Type", '%1|%2', recEntry_L."Source Type"::Customer, recEntry_L."Source Type"::Vendor);
      recEntry_L.SETRANGE("Item Ledger Entry Type", recEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry Type"::Purchase);
      recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Movement Type", recConnectorSetup_L."EntryColl Movement Type");
      recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Document Type", '');

      IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryCollectHistoryActivity <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Activity Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryCollectHistoryActivity);
      END;

      IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryCollectHistoryFamily <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryCollectHistoryFamily);
      END;

      IF recConnectorSetup_L."EntryCollectHistorySub-Family" <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Sub-Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L."EntryCollectHistorySub-Family");
      END;

      recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorCollectHistory := '';
      IF recEntry_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          cduCatchError_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::HistoryCollect, recAutomation_L, recAutomation_L);
          cduCatchError_L.SetParameterHistory(recEntry_L);
          IF cduCatchError_L.RUN() THEN BEGIN
            recConnectorSetup_L.LastEntryCollectHistorySended := recEntry_L."Entry No.";
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //erreur : on sauvegarde ce que l'on a pu faire
            recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorCollectHistory := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorCollectHistory));
            recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
            COMMIT;
            recEntry_L.FINDLAST;
          END;
        UNTIL recEntry_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
      cduManCon_G.ClearConnector();
      recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
      COMMIT;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncHistoryFromAccount@8053002(Contact_P@8053004 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      recEntry_L@8053003 : Record 5802;
      recConnectorSetup_L@8053002 : Record 8053602;
      cduCatchError_L@8053001 : Codeunit 8053608;
      recAutomation_L@8053000 : Automation "{90B5DB41-ED3C-488E-84F5-6647F85F95A2} 1.0:{F313095A-9ADF-3065-9FD4-FD3674235D4D}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class";
      recContactRelation_L@8053005 : Record 5054;
      recItemLedEntry_L@8053006 : Record 32;
      recEntry2_L@8053007 : Record 5802;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation l'historique d'un nouveau contact dans la CRM (compte) (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Contact_P : Contact dont on veut envoyer les 'critures
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      recConnectorSetup_L.GET();
      cduManCon_G.GetAutomation(recAutomation_L);

      recContactRelation_L.RESET;
      recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("MC Company Code", cduManCon_G.GetCompanyMcCode());
      recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("Contact No.", Contact_P);
      recContactRelation_L.SETFILTER("Link to Table", '%1|%2', recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Customer,
        recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Vendor);
      IF recContactRelation_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          //Recherche des 'critures articles
          recItemLedEntry_L.RESET;
          recItemLedEntry_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Source Type", "Source No.");
          IF recContactRelation_L."Link to Table" = recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Customer THEN
            recItemLedEntry_L.SETRANGE("Source Type", recItemLedEntry_L."Source Type"::Customer)
          ELSE
            recItemLedEntry_L.SETRANGE("Source Type", recItemLedEntry_L."Source Type"::Vendor);
          recItemLedEntry_L.SETRANGE("Source No.", recContactRelation_L."No.");
          // --> 20150930-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2336-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //recItemLedEntry_L.SETRANGE("Entry Type", recItemLedEntry_L."Entry Type"::Sale,
          //AFTER
          recItemLedEntry_L.SETFILTER("Entry Type", '%1|%2', recItemLedEntry_L."Entry Type"::Sale,
          // <-- 20150930-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2336-FHS01
            recItemLedEntry_L."Entry Type"::Purchase);
          IF recItemLedEntry_L.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              //Recherche des 'critures valeurs
              recEntry_L.RESET;
              recEntry_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Item Ledger Entry No.");
              recEntry_L.SETRANGE("Item Ledger Entry No.", recItemLedEntry_L."Entry No.");
              recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Source Type", '%1|%2', recEntry_L."Source Type"::Customer, recEntry_L."Source Type"::Vendor);
              // --> 20150930-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2336-FHS01
              //BEFORE
              //recEntry_L.SETRANGE("Item Ledger Entry Type", recEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry Type"::Sale,
              //AFTER
              recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Item Ledger Entry Type", '%1|%2', recEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry Type"::Sale,
              // <-- 20150930-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2336-FHS01
                recEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry Type"::Purchase);
              recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Document Type", '%1|%2|%3|%4', recEntry_L."Document Type"::"Sales Invoice",
                recEntry_L."Document Type"::"Sales Credit Memo", recEntry_L."Document Type"::"Purchase Invoice",
                recEntry_L."Document Type"::"Purchase Credit Memo");

              IF recEntry_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
                REPEAT
                  //Recherche s'il y a de nouvelle 'criture . envoyer de type appro
                  recEntry2_L.RESET;
                  recEntry2_L.SETRANGE("Entry No.", recEntry_L."Entry No.");
                  IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryActivity <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    recEntry2_L.SETFILTER("Activity Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryActivity);
                  END;
                  IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryFamily <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    recEntry2_L.SETFILTER("Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryFamily);
                  END;
                  IF recConnectorSetup_L."EntryApproHistorySub-Family" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    recEntry2_L.SETFILTER("Sub-Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L."EntryApproHistorySub-Family");
                  END;
                  recEntry2_L.SETRANGE("Item Ledger Entry Type", recEntry2_L."Item Ledger Entry Type"::Sale);
                  // --> 20150930-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2336-FHS01
                  //BEFORE
                  //recEntry2_L.SETFILTER("Document Type", '%1|%2|%3|%4', recEntry2_L."Document Type"::"Sales Invoice",
                  //AFTER
                  recEntry2_L.SETFILTER("Document Type", '%1|%2', recEntry2_L."Document Type"::"Sales Invoice",
                  // <-- 20150930-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2336-FHS01
                   recEntry2_L."Document Type"::"Sales Credit Memo");

                  recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorApproHistory := '';
                  IF NOT recEntry2_L.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
                    cduCatchError_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::HistoryAppro, recAutomation_L, recAutomation_L);
                    cduCatchError_L.SetParameterHistory(recEntry_L);
                    IF NOT cduCatchError_L.RUN() THEN BEGIN
                      //erreur : on sauvegarde ce que l'on a pu faire
                      recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorApproHistory := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1,
                        MAXSTRLEN(recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorApproHistory));
                      recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
                      COMMIT;
                    END;
                  END;

                  //Recherche s'il y a de nouvelle 'criture . envoyer de type collect
                  recEntry2_L.RESET;
                  recEntry2_L.SETRANGE("Entry No.", recEntry_L."Entry No.");
                  IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryCollectHistoryActivity <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    recEntry2_L.SETFILTER("Activity Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryCollectHistoryActivity);
                  END;
                  IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryCollectHistoryFamily <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    recEntry2_L.SETFILTER("Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryCollectHistoryFamily);
                  END;
                  IF recConnectorSetup_L."EntryCollectHistorySub-Family" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    recEntry2_L.SETFILTER("Sub-Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L."EntryCollectHistorySub-Family");
                  END;
                  recEntry2_L.SETRANGE("Item Ledger Entry Type", recEntry2_L."Item Ledger Entry Type"::Purchase);
                  recEntry2_L.SETFILTER("Document Type", '%1|%2', recEntry2_L."Document Type"::"Purchase Invoice",
                    recEntry2_L."Document Type"::"Purchase Credit Memo");

                  recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorCollectHistory := '';
                  IF NOT recEntry2_L.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
                    cduCatchError_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::HistoryCollect, recAutomation_L, recAutomation_L);
                    cduCatchError_L.SetParameterHistory(recEntry_L);
                    IF NOT cduCatchError_L.RUN() THEN BEGIN
                      //erreur : on sauvegarde ce que l'on a pu faire
                      recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorCollectHistory := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1,
                        MAXSTRLEN(recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorCollectHistory));
                      recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
                      COMMIT;
                    END;
                  END;
                UNTIL recEntry_L.NEXT = 0;
              END;
            UNTIL recItemLedEntry_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;
        UNTIL recContactRelation_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;

      cduManCon_G.ClearConnector();
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncApproLineHistory@8053617(recEntry_P@8053601 : Record 5802);
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      TxtGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      recContactRelation_L@8053600 : Record 5054;
      Amount_L@8053605 : Decimal;
      recContact_L@8053608 : Record 5050;
      recItem_L@8053610 : Record 27;
      recVariant_L@8053609 : Record 5401;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'une ligne d'historique de ventes et achats (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recEntry_P : 'criture valeur . envoyer
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_saleshistory';

      //recherche du compte
      recContactRelation_L.RESET;
      recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("No.", recEntry_P."Source No.");
      IF recEntry_P."Source Type"::Customer = recEntry_P."Source Type" THEN
        recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Customer)
      ELSE
        recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Vendor);
      IF recContactRelation_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        recContact_L.GET(recContactRelation_L."Contact No.");
        IF recContact_L."Contact CRM" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053626, recContact_L."No.");
          IF cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recContact_L."No.", TxtGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            // --> 20150807-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2095-FHS01
            IF NOT(HistoryExist(recEntry_P."Entry No.", 'EVA')) THEN BEGIN
            // <-- 20150807-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2095-FHS01
              cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

              cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_account', TxtGuid_L);

              //recherche du produit
              IF recEntry_P."Variant Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
                TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, recEntry_P."Item No.");
                // -->  20150925-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2319-FHS01
                //BEFORE
                //cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recEntry_P."Item No.", TxtGuid_L, recEntry_P.FIELDCAPTION("Item No."));
                //AfTER
                IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recEntry_P."Item No.", TxtGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
                  TxtGuid_L := SearchUnactiveProduct(recEntry_P."Item No.");
                  cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recEntry_P."Item No.", TxtGuid_L, recEntry_P.FIELDCAPTION("Item No."));
                END;
                // <--  20150925-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2319-FHS01
              END ELSE BEGIN
                TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, recEntry_P."Item No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() +
                  recEntry_P."Variant Code");
                // -->  20150925-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2319-FHS01
                //BEFORE
                //cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recEntry_P."Variant Code", TxtGuid_L, recEntry_P.FIELDCAPTION("Variant Code"));
                //AfTER
                IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recEntry_P."Variant Code", TxtGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
                  TxtGuid_L := SearchUnactiveProduct(recEntry_P."Item No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() + recEntry_P."Variant Code")
      ;
                  cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recEntry_P."Item No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() + recEntry_P."Variant Code", TxtGuid_L,
                    recEntry_P.FIELDCAPTION("Variant Code"));
                END;
                // <--  20150925-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2319-FHS01
              END;

              cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_product', TxtGuid_L);

              IF recEntry_P."Variant Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
                IF recItem_L.GET(recEntry_P."Item No.") THEN
                  cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', COPYSTR(recItem_L.Description + recItem_L."Description 2", 1, 250));
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF recVariant_L.GET(recEntry_P."Item No.", recEntry_P."Variant Code") THEN
                  cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name',  COPYSTR(recVariant_L.Description + recVariant_L."Description 2",
                  1, 250));
              END;

              //recherche de l'activit'
              TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053648, recEntry_P."Activity Code");
              cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recEntry_P."Activity Code", TxtGuid_L, recEntry_P.FIELDCAPTION("Activity Code"));
              cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_activity', TxtGuid_L);

              //recherche de la famille
              TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053649, recEntry_P."Family Code");
              cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recEntry_P."Family Code", TxtGuid_L, recEntry_P.FIELDCAPTION("Family Code"));
              cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_family', TxtGuid_L);

              //recherche de la campagne
              TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053622, recEntry_P."Campaign No.");
              cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recEntry_P."Campaign No.", TxtGuid_L, recEntry_P.FIELDCAPTION("Campaign No."));
              cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaign', TxtGuid_L);

              //recherche de la sous famille
              TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053650, recEntry_P."Sub-Family Code");
              cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recEntry_P."Sub-Family Code", TxtGuid_L, recEntry_P.FIELDCAPTION("Sub-Family Code"));
              cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_subfamily', TxtGuid_L);

              //recherche de l'espSce
              TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053651, recEntry_P."Species Code");
              cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recEntry_P."Species Code", TxtGuid_L, recEntry_P.FIELDCAPTION("Species Code"));
              cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_species', TxtGuid_L);

              cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_date', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recEntry_P."Posting Date"));
              cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_qutity', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(-recEntry_P."Invoiced Quantity"));
              Amount_L := recEntry_P."Sales Amount (Actual)";
              cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_amount', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(Amount_L));

              // --> 20150807-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2095-FHS01
              cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_no_seq', cduManCon_G.FormatIntToCrm(recEntry_P."Entry No."));
              cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type_ecriture', 'EVA');
              // <-- 20150807-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2095-FHS01

              IF NOT(cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L)) THEN
                cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
            // --> 20150807-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2095-FHS01
            END;
            // <-- 20150807-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2095-FHS01
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncCollectLineHistory@8053626(recEntry_P@8053601 : Record 5802);
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      TxtGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      recContactRelation_L@8053600 : Record 5054;
      Amount_L@8053605 : Decimal;
      Cost_L@8053604 : Decimal;
      recContact_L@8053608 : Record 5050;
      recItem_L@8053609 : Record 27;
      recVariant_L@8053610 : Record 5401;
      recItemLedgerEntry_L@8053000 : Record 32;
      recContrat_L@8053001 : Record 8052983;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'une ligne d'historique de ventes et achats (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recEntry_P : 'criture valeur . envoyer
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_collecthistory';

      //recherche du compte
      recContactRelation_L.RESET;
      recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("No.", recEntry_P."Source No.");
      IF recEntry_P."Source Type"::Customer = recEntry_P."Source Type" THEN BEGIN
        recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Customer);
      END ELSE BEGIN
        recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Vendor);
      END;

      IF recContactRelation_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        recContact_L.GET(recContactRelation_L."Contact No.");
        IF recContact_L."Contact CRM" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053626, recContact_L."No.");
          IF cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recContact_L."No.", TxtGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            // --> 20150807-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2095-FHS01
            IF NOT(HistoryExist(recEntry_P."Entry No.", 'EVC')) THEN BEGIN
            // <-- 20150807-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2095-FHS01
              cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_account_id', TxtGuid_L);

              //recherche du produit
              IF recEntry_P."Variant Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
                TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, recEntry_P."Item No.");
                // -->  20150925-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2319-FHS01
                //BEFORE
                //cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recEntry_P."Item No.", TxtGuid_L, recEntry_P.FIELDCAPTION("Item No."));
                //AfTER
                IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recEntry_P."Item No.", TxtGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
                  TxtGuid_L := SearchUnactiveProduct(recEntry_P."Item No.");
                  cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recEntry_P."Item No.", TxtGuid_L, recEntry_P.FIELDCAPTION("Item No."));
                END;
                // <--  20150925-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2319-FHS01
              END ELSE BEGIN
                TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, recEntry_P."Item No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() +
                  recEntry_P."Variant Code");
                // -->  20150925-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2319-FHS01
                //BEFORE
                //cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recEntry_P."Variant Code", TxtGuid_L, recEntry_P.FIELDCAPTION("Variant Code"));
                //AfTER
                IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recEntry_P."Variant Code", TxtGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
                  TxtGuid_L := SearchUnactiveProduct(recEntry_P."Item No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() + recEntry_P."Variant Code")
      ;
                  cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recEntry_P."Item No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() + recEntry_P."Variant Code", TxtGuid_L,
                    recEntry_P.FIELDCAPTION("Variant Code"));
                END;
                // <--  20150925-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2319-FHS01
              END;

              cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_product_id', TxtGuid_L);

              IF recEntry_P."Variant Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
                IF recItem_L.GET(recEntry_P."Item No.") THEN
                  cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productname', COPYSTR(recItem_L.Description + recItem_L."Description 2",
                  1, 250));
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF recVariant_L.GET(recEntry_P."Item No.", recEntry_P."Variant Code") THEN
                  cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productname', COPYSTR(recVariant_L.Description + recVariant_L."Description 2",
                  1, 250));
              END;

              //recherche de l'activit'
              TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053648, recEntry_P."Activity Code");
              cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recEntry_P."Activity Code", TxtGuid_L, recEntry_P.FIELDCAPTION("Activity Code"));
              cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_activity_id', TxtGuid_L);

              //recherche de la famille
              TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053649, recEntry_P."Family Code");
              cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recEntry_P."Family Code", TxtGuid_L, recEntry_P.FIELDCAPTION("Family Code"));
              cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_family_id', TxtGuid_L);

              //recherche de la campagne
              TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053622, recEntry_P."Campaign No.");
              cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recEntry_P."Campaign No.", TxtGuid_L, recEntry_P.FIELDCAPTION("Campaign No."));
              cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaign_id', TxtGuid_L);

              //recherche de la sous famille
              TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053650, recEntry_P."Sub-Family Code");
              cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recEntry_P."Sub-Family Code", TxtGuid_L, recEntry_P.FIELDCAPTION("Sub-Family Code"));
              cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_subfamily_id', TxtGuid_L);

              //recherche de l'espSce
              TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053651, recEntry_P."Species Code");
              cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recEntry_P."Species Code", TxtGuid_L, recEntry_P.FIELDCAPTION("Species Code"));
              cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_species_id', TxtGuid_L);

              cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectdate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recEntry_P."Posting Date"));
              cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectedquantity', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(
                recEntry_P."Item Ledger Entry Quantity"));

              // --> 20150807-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2095-FHS01
              cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_no_seq', cduManCon_G.FormatIntToCrm(recEntry_P."Entry No."));
              cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type_ecriture', 'EVC');
              // <-- 20150807-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2095-FHS01

              IF recItemLedgerEntry_L.GET(recEntry_P."Item Ledger Entry No.") THEN BEGIN
                IF recItemLedgerEntry_L."Collection Contract No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
                  IF recContrat_L.GET(recItemLedgerEntry_L."Collection Contract No.") THEN BEGIN
                    IF recContrat_L."Contract Type" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                      TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053671, recContrat_L."Contract Type");
                      IF TxtGuid_L = '' THEN
                        TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053672, recContrat_L."Contract Type");
                      IF TxtGuid_L <> '' THEN
                        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectmasteragreementtype_id', TxtGuid_L);
                    END;
                  END;
                END;
              END;
              IF NOT(cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
                cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
              END;
            // --> 20150807-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2095-FHS01
            END;
            // <-- 20150807-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2095-FHS01
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE HistoryExist@8053008(NoSeq_P@8053000 : Integer;Type_P@8053001 : Code[10]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053002 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Recherche si un historique existe d'j.
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - NoSeq_P : No S'quence
      //    - Type_P : Type d''criture
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Valeur existante en CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // --> 20150807-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2095-FHS01
      Result_L := FALSE;

      CASE TRUE OF
        Type_P = 'EVA': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_saleshistory';
        END;
        Type_P = 'EVC': BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_collecthistory';
        END;
      END;

      cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
      cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_no_seq', '=', cduManCon_G.FormatIntToCrm(NoSeq_P));
      cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type_ecriture', '=', Type_P);
      IF cduManCon_G.SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L) > 0 THEN
        Result_L := TRUE;

      EXIT(Result_L);
      // <-- 20150807-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2095-FHS01
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncQuota@8053621(Code_P@8053601 : Text[110];TypeAction_P@8053600 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      Doc1_L@8053609 : Text[30];
      Doc2_L@8053608 : Text[30];
      Doc3_L@8053607 : Text[30];
      Doc4_L@8053606 : Text[30];
      Type1_L@8053605 : Integer;
      Type2_L@8053604 : Integer;
      Date1_L@8053603 : Date;
      Date2_L@8053602 : Date;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053611 : Text[50];
      recGuid_L@8053610 : Record 8053609;
      EntityName_L@8053612 : Text[50];
      Txtdate_L@8053613 : Text[8];
      recQuota_L@8053614 : Record 8053260;
      Code_L@8053615 : Text[200];
      Modify_L@8053616 : Boolean;
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053617 : Text[50];
      recItem_L@8053618 : Record 27;
      Separator_L@8053619 : Text[1];
      recSetup_L@8053000 : Record 8053602;
      FieldName_L@8053621 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      FieldValue_L@8053620 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      recVariant_L@8053622 : Record 5401;
      UserIdGuid_L@8053623 : Text[50];
      UserId_L@8053624 : Code[50];
      recMappingSalesp_L@8053625 : Record 8053608;
      recSalespersonn_L@8053626 : Record 13;
      TxtItemGuid_L@8053627 : Text[50];
      Skip_L@8053628 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'une ligne de quota (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_productquota';
      Code_L := Code_P;
      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();

      Doc1_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
      Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));

      EVALUATE(Type1_L, COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1));
      Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
      Doc2_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
      Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));

      Doc3_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
      Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
      EVALUATE(Type2_L, COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1));
      Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
      Doc4_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
      Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
      Txtdate_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, MAXSTRLEN(Txtdate_L));
      Date1_L := TxtToDate(Txtdate_L);
      Txtdate_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Txtdate_L));
      Date2_L := TxtToDate(Txtdate_L);

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        // --> 20151002-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01
        recSetup_L.GET;
        // <-- 20151002-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01
        recQuota_L.RESET();
        recQuota_L.SETRANGE("Campaign No.", Doc1_L);
        recQuota_L.SETRANGE(Type, Type1_L);
        recQuota_L.SETRANGE("No.", Doc2_L);
        recQuota_L.SETRANGE("Production Activity Code", Doc3_L);
        recQuota_L.SETRANGE("Sales Type", Type2_L);
        recQuota_L.SETRANGE("Sales No.", Doc4_L);
        recQuota_L.SETRANGE("Starting Date", Date1_L);
        recQuota_L.SETRANGE("Ending Date", Date2_L);
        // --> 20151002-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01
        recQuota_L.SETFILTER("Doc. Category Code",'%1|%2',recSetup_L."Document Category for Sales",'');
        // <-- 20151002-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01
        IF recQuota_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            Skip_L := FALSE;
            IF (recQuota_L.Type <> recQuota_L.Type::Item) OR (recQuota_L."Starting Date" = 0D) OR
               (recQuota_L."Ending Date" = 0D)
            THEN BEGIN
              Skip_L := TRUE;
            END;
            IF Skip_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
              IF recQuota_L."Variant Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
                recItem_L.GET(recQuota_L."No.");
                TxtItemGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, recQuota_L."No.");
                // --> 20150107-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1851-FHS01
                //BEFORE
                //VerifGuidFinded(recQuota_L."No.", TxtItemGuid_L, recQuota_L.FIELDCAPTION("No."));
                //AFTER
                IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recQuota_L."No.", TxtItemGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
                  TxtItemGuid_L := SearchUnactiveProduct(recQuota_L."No.");
                  VerifGuidFinded(recQuota_L."No.", TxtItemGuid_L, recQuota_L.FIELDCAPTION("No."));
                END;
                // <-- 20150107-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1851-FHS01
              END ELSE BEGIN
                recVariant_L.GET(recQuota_L."No.", recQuota_L."Variant Code");
                TxtItemGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, recQuota_L."No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() +
                  recQuota_L."Variant Code");

                // --> 20150107-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1851-FHS01
                //BEFORE
                //VerifGuidFinded(recQuota_L."No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() + recQuota_L."Variant Code",
                //  TxtItemGuid_L, recQuota_L."Variant Code");
                //VerifGuidFinded(recQuota_L."No.", TxtItemGuid_L, recQuota_L.FIELDCAPTION("No."));
                //AFTER
                IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recQuota_L."No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() + recQuota_L."Variant Code",
                  TxtItemGuid_L))
                THEN BEGIN
                  TxtItemGuid_L := SearchUnactiveProduct(recQuota_L."No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() + recQuota_L."Variant Code");
                  VerifGuidFinded(recQuota_L."No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() + recQuota_L."Variant Code",
                    TxtItemGuid_L, recQuota_L."Variant Code");
                END;
                // <-- 20150107-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1851-FHS01
              END;

              IF NOT(cduManCon_G.ItemIsSynchronised(recItem_L)) THEN BEGIN
                Skip_L := TRUE;
              END;
            END;

            IF Skip_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
              Code_L := Code_P + Separator_L + recQuota_L."Variant Code" + Separator_L + recQuota_L."Activity Code" +
                Separator_L + recQuota_L."Family Code" + Separator_L + recQuota_L."Sub-Family Code" + Separator_L +
                recQuota_L."Species Code";

              cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

              //R'cup'ration du GUID
              TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053676, Code_L);

              Modify_L := FALSE;

              IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
                IF cduManCon_G.ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                  Modify_L := TRUE;
                END;
              END;

              //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
              CASE recQuota_L."Sales Type" OF
                recQuota_L."Sales Type"::Company: BEGIN
                  recSalespersonn_L.RESET;
                  IF recSalespersonn_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
                    REPEAT
                      UserId_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetUserID(recSalespersonn_L.Code);
                      IF UserId_L <> '' THEN
                        SyncSubQuota(recQuota_L, UserId_L, recItem_L, recVariant_L, TxtItemGuid_L);
                    UNTIL recSalespersonn_L.NEXT = 0;
                  END;
                  Skip_L := TRUE;
                END;
                recQuota_L."Sales Type"::"Sales Area": BEGIN
                  recSalespersonn_L.RESET;
                  recSalespersonn_L.SETRANGE("Sales Area Code", recQuota_L."Sales Area Code");
                  IF recSalespersonn_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
                    REPEAT
                      UserId_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetUserID(recSalespersonn_L.Code);
                      IF UserId_L <> '' THEN
                        SyncSubQuota(recQuota_L, UserId_L, recItem_L, recVariant_L, TxtItemGuid_L);
                    UNTIL recSalespersonn_L.NEXT = 0;
                  END;
                  Skip_L := TRUE;
                END;
                recQuota_L."Sales Type"::"Sales Zone": BEGIN
                  recSalespersonn_L.RESET;
                  recSalespersonn_L.SETRANGE("Sales Zone Code", recQuota_L."Sales Zone Code");
                  IF recSalespersonn_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
                    REPEAT
                      UserId_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetUserID(recSalespersonn_L.Code);
                      IF UserId_L <> '' THEN
                        SyncSubQuota(recQuota_L, UserId_L, recItem_L, recVariant_L, TxtItemGuid_L);
                    UNTIL recSalespersonn_L.NEXT = 0;
                  END;
                  Skip_L := TRUE;
                END;
                recQuota_L."Sales Type"::Salesperson: BEGIN
                  cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_naturequota', '175490000');
                  cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_systemuserid', UserIdGuid_L);
                END;
              END;
              IF Skip_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
                IF recQuota_L."No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
                  IF recQuota_L."Variant Code"  = '' THEN BEGIN
                    cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recItem_L.Description);
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recVariant_L.Description);
                  END;
                END;
                cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productid', TxtItemGuid_L);
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_quota', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recQuota_L."Initial Quantity"));
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_remainingquota', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recQuota_L.Quantity));
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_startdate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recQuota_L."Starting Date"));
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_enddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recQuota_L."Ending Date"));
                UserId_L := recMappingSalesp_L.GetUserID(recQuota_L."Salesperson Code");
                UserIdGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053631, UserId_L);

                //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
                IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
                  IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                    //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
                    cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053676, Code_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
                  END;
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                    //Mise .  jour r'ussie
                    cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    //Mise . jour 'chou'e
                    cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
                  END;
                END;
              END;
            END;
          UNTIL recQuota_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression du quota
        recGuid_L.RESET;
        recGuid_L.SETRANGE("Type Data", recGuid_L."Type Data"::Quota);
        recGuid_L.SETFILTER("Code Nav", Code_P + '*');
        IF recGuid_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            //R'cup'ration du GUID
            IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(recGuid_L.GUID)) THEN
              cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, recGuid_L.GUID, recGuid_L."Type Data"::Quota, 2, 2);
          UNTIL recGuid_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;
        cduManCon_G.AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::Quota, Code_P, TextModify, '');
        COMMIT;
        ERROR(Text8053691);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncSubQuota@8053651(recQuota_P@8053601 : Record 8053260;UserId_P@8053600 : Code[20];recItem_P@8053602 : Record 27;recVariant_P@8053603 : Record 5401;TxtItemGuid_P@8053604 : Text[50]);
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053605 : Text[50];
      UserIdGuid_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053607 : Boolean;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053608 : Text[50];
      Code_L@8053609 : Text[110];
      Separator_L@8053610 : Text[1];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'une ligne de quota (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      EntityName_L := 'cmc_productquota';

      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();
      Code_L := recQuota_P."Campaign No." + Separator_L + FORMAT(recQuota_P.Type, 1, 2) + Separator_L +
                recQuota_P."No." + Separator_L + recQuota_P."Production Activity Code" + Separator_L +
                FORMAT(recQuota_P."Sales Type", 1, 2) + Separator_L + recQuota_P."Sales No." + Separator_L +
                FORMAT(recQuota_P."Starting Date", 8, '<Year4><Month,2><Day,2>') + Separator_L +
                FORMAT(recQuota_P."Ending Date", 8, '<Year4><Month,2><Day,2>') + Separator_L +
                recQuota_P."Variant Code" + Separator_L + recQuota_P."Activity Code" + Separator_L +
                recQuota_P."Family Code" + Separator_L + recQuota_P."Sub-Family Code" + Separator_L +
                recQuota_P."Species Code" + Separator_L + recQuota_P."Salesperson Code"
                // --> 20151002-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01
                 + Separator_L + UserId_P;
                // <-- 20151002-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01


      //R'cup'ration du GUID
      TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053676, Code_L);

      Modify_L := FALSE;

      cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
      IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
        IF cduManCon_G.ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          Modify_L := TRUE;
        END;
      END;


      cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
      CASE recQuota_P."Sales Type" OF
        recQuota_P."Sales Type"::Company: BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_naturequota', '175490003');
        END;
        recQuota_P."Sales Type"::"Sales Area": BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_naturequota', '175490002');
        END;
        recQuota_P."Sales Type"::"Sales Zone": BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_naturequota', '175490001');
        END;
      END;

      IF recQuota_P."No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
        IF recQuota_P."Variant Code"  = '' THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recItem_P.Description);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recVariant_P.Description);
        END;

        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productid', TxtItemGuid_P);
      END;
      cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_quota', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recQuota_P."Initial Quantity"));
      cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_remainingquota', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recQuota_P.Quantity));
      cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_startdate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recQuota_P."Starting Date"));
      cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_enddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recQuota_P."Ending Date"));
      UserIdGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053631, UserId_P);
      cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_systemuserid', UserIdGuid_L);
      //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
      IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
          cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053676, Code_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          //Mise .  jour r'ussie
          cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
        END ELSE BEGIN
          //Mise . jour 'chou'e
          cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
        END;
      END;
      COMMIT;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncTranscription@8053620(Code_P@8053601 : Text[110];TypeAction_P@8053600 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      Code_L@8053603 : Text[110];
      EntityName_L@8053604 : Text[50];
      recAssolement_L@8053607 : Record 8053360;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053610 : Text[50];
      TxtCampaignGuid_L@8053612 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053614 : Boolean;
      recGuid_L@8053615 : Record 8053609;
      FieldName_L@8053606 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      FieldValue_L@8053605 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un modSle de transcription (assolement) (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_transcriptionpattern';
      Code_L := Code_P;

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        recAssolement_L.GET(Code_P);

        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053667, Code_P);
        TxtCampaignGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053622, recAssolement_L."Campaign No.");
        VerifGuidFinded(recAssolement_L."Campaign No.", TxtCampaignGuid_L, recAssolement_L.FIELDCAPTION("Campaign No."));

        Modify_L := FALSE;

        IF cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.SearchGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', Code_P, '', '', '', '');
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053667, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END;
        END;

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END;
        END;

        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recAssolement_L.Code);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recAssolement_L.Description);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaign_id', TxtCampaignGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_transcriptionpatterntype', '175490000'); //production v'g'tale

        //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053667, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
            //Mise .  jour r'ussie
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Mise . jour 'chou'e
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END;
      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression du modSle de transcription
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053667, Code_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::transcriptionpattern, 2, 2);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncArticleTranscription@8053622(Code_P@8053601 : Text[110];TypeAction_P@8053600 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053614 : Text[50];
      Code_L@8053613 : Text[110];
      EntityName_L@8053612 : Text[50];
      Doc1_L@8053611 : Text[30];
      Int1_L@8053610 : Integer;
      recAssolementLine_L@8053609 : Record 8053361;
      recAssolement_L@8053602 : Record 8053360;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053608 : Text[50];
      TxtCampaignGuid_L@8053607 : Text[50];
      TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      TxtTranscriptionPatternGuid_L@8053604 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053606 : Boolean;
      recGuid_L@8053605 : Record 8053609;
      Separator_L@8053615 : Text[1];
      Formula_L@8053616 : Text[250];
      FieldName_L@8053618 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      FieldValue_L@8053617 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un article de transcription (ligne assolement) (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_articletranscript';
      Code_L := Code_P;
      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        Doc1_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
        EVALUATE(Int1_L, COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L)));

        IF NOT recAssolementLine_L.GET(Doc1_L, Int1_L) THEN
          EXIT;
        IF NOT recAssolement_L.GET(recAssolementLine_L.Code) THEN
          EXIT;

        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053668, Code_P);
        TxtCampaignGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053622, recAssolement_L."Campaign No.");
        VerifGuidFinded(recAssolement_L."Campaign No.", TxtCampaignGuid_L, recAssolement_L.FIELDCAPTION("Campaign No."));

        TxtTranscriptionPatternGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053667, recAssolement_L.Code);
        VerifGuidFinded(recAssolement_L.Code, TxtTranscriptionPatternGuid_L, recAssolement_L.FIELDCAPTION(Code));

        Modify_L := FALSE;

        IF cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.SearchGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_transcriptionpattern_id', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(
            TxtTranscriptionPatternGuid_L), 'cmc_linenumber', cduManCon_G.FormatIntToCrm(recAssolementLine_L.Line), '', '');
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053668, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END;
        END;

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END;
        END;

        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        CASE recAssolementLine_L."Line Type" OF
          recAssolementLine_L."Line Type"::Formula: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_calculatedfield', '1');
            Formula_L := recAssolementLine_L.Range;
            Formula_L := ReplaceString(Formula_L, '+', ' @@@ ');
            Formula_L := ReplaceString(Formula_L, '@@@', '+');
            Formula_L := ReplaceString(Formula_L, '-', ' @@@ ');
            Formula_L := ReplaceString(Formula_L, '@@@', '-');
            Formula_L := ReplaceString(Formula_L, '*', ' @@@ ');
            Formula_L := ReplaceString(Formula_L, '@@@', '*');
            Formula_L := ReplaceString(Formula_L, '/', ' @@@ ');
            Formula_L := ReplaceString(Formula_L, '@@@', '/');
            Formula_L := ReplaceString(Formula_L, '[', ' @@@ ');
            Formula_L := ReplaceString(Formula_L, '@@@', '[');
            Formula_L := ReplaceString(Formula_L, ']', ' @@@ ');
            Formula_L := ReplaceString(Formula_L, '@@@', ']');
            Formula_L := ReplaceString(Formula_L, '(', ' @@@ ');
            Formula_L := ReplaceString(Formula_L, '@@@', '(');
            Formula_L := ReplaceString(Formula_L, ')', ' @@@ ');
            Formula_L := ReplaceString(Formula_L, '@@@', ')');
            Formula_L := ReplaceString(Formula_L, '  ', '@@@');
            Formula_L := ReplaceString(Formula_L, '@@@', ' ');
            IF STRPOS(Formula_L, ' ') = 1 THEN BEGIN
              REPEAT
                Formula_L := COPYSTR(Formula_L, 2, STRLEN(Formula_L));
              UNTIL STRPOS(Formula_L, ' ') <> 1;
            END;
            IF Formula_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              IF COPYSTR(Formula_L, STRLEN(Formula_L), 1) = ' ' THEN BEGIN
                REPEAT
                  Formula_L := COPYSTR(Formula_L, 1, STRLEN(Formula_L) - 1);
                UNTIL COPYSTR(Formula_L, STRLEN(Formula_L), 1) <> ' ';
              END;
            END;
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_formula', Formula_L);
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', '');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfilterid', '#NULL#');
          END;

          recAssolementLine_L."Line Type"::"Funnel Filter": BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_calculatedfield', '0');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_formula', '');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recAssolementLine_L.Range);
            TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053673, recAssolementLine_L.Range);
            VerifGuidFinded(recAssolementLine_L.Range, TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L, recAssolementLine_L.FIELDCAPTION(Range));
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfilterid', TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L);
          END;

          recAssolementLine_L."Line Type"::Title: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_calculatedfield', '1');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_formula', '');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recAssolementLine_L.Range);
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfilterid', '#NULL#');
          END;
        END;
        IF recAssolementLine_L."Is UAL" THEN
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', 'SAUC');
        IF (recAssolementLine_L."Fields Type" = recAssolementLine_L."Fields Type"::"Sales Amount") AND
           (recAssolementLine_L.Description <> '')
        THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_potencialsupply', '1');
        END ELSE BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_potencialsupply', '0');
        END;

        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_linenumber', cduManCon_G.FormatIntToCrm(recAssolementLine_L.Line));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_linereference', recAssolementLine_L."Row Ref. No.");
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recAssolementLine_L.Description);

        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_transcriptionpattern_id', TxtTranscriptionPatternGuid_L);

        //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053668, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
            //Mise .  jour r'ussie
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Mise . jour 'chou'e
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END;
      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression du modSle de transcription
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053668, Code_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::articletranscript, 2, 2);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncElementCampaign@8053624(Code_P@8053613 : Text[110];TypeAction_P@8053607 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053612 : Text[50];
      Code_L@8053611 : Text[110];
      EntityName_L@8053610 : Text[50];
      Doc1_L@8053609 : Text[30];
      Doc2_L@8053608 : Text[30];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053605 : Text[50];
      TxtCampaignGuid_L@8053604 : Text[50];
      TxtTranscriptionPatternGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      TxtAccountGuid_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053601 : Boolean;
      recGuid_L@8053600 : Record 8053609;
      recCropRotation_L@8053603 : Record 8053194;
      Separator_L@8053614 : Text[1];
      FieldName_L@8053616 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      FieldValue_L@8053615 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      recContact_L@8053000 : Record 5050;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un 'l'ment de campagne (CMC Crop Rotation) (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_elementcampaign';
      Code_L := Code_P;
      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        Doc1_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
        Doc2_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));

        recCropRotation_L.GET(Doc1_L, Doc2_L);
        IF recCropRotation_L.Freezed = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          EXIT;
        END;

        IF recContact_L.GET(recCropRotation_L."Contact No.") THEN BEGIN
          IF recContact_L."Contact CRM" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;
        END;

        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053659, Code_P);
        TxtCampaignGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053622, recCropRotation_L."Campaign No.");
        VerifGuidFinded(recCropRotation_L."Campaign No.", TxtCampaignGuid_L, recCropRotation_L.FIELDCAPTION("Campaign No."));

        TxtTranscriptionPatternGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053667, recCropRotation_L."Crop Rotation Table Code");
        VerifGuidFinded(recCropRotation_L."Crop Rotation Table Code", TxtTranscriptionPatternGuid_L,
          recCropRotation_L.FIELDCAPTION("Crop Rotation Table Code"));

        TxtAccountGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053626, recCropRotation_L."Contact No.");
        VerifGuidFinded(recCropRotation_L."Contact No.", TxtAccountGuid_L, recCropRotation_L.FIELDCAPTION("Contact No."));

        Modify_L := FALSE;

        IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END;
        END;

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_hastogeneratedetail', '0');
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_isdetailgenerated', '1');
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_codecampaign', recCropRotation_L."Campaign No.");
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recCropRotation_L."Campaign No.");
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_displaytype', '175490000');

        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_account_id', TxtAccountGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaign_id', TxtCampaignGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_transcriptionpattern_id', TxtTranscriptionPatternGuid_L);

        //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053659, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
            //Mise .  jour r'ussie
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Mise . jour 'chou'e
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END;
        cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 175490001);
      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression de l''l'ment de campagne
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053659, Code_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::elementcampaign, 2, 2);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncDetailsElementCampaign@8053625(Code_P@8053601 : Text[110];TypeAction_P@8053600 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053613 : Text[50];
      Code_L@8053612 : Text[110];
      EntityName_L@8053611 : Text[50];
      Doc1_L@8053610 : Text[30];
      Doc2_L@8053609 : Text[30];
      Int1_L@8053615 : Integer;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053608 : Text[50];
      TxtElementCampaignGuid_L@8053607 : Text[50];
      TxtTranscriptionArticleGuid_L@8053605 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053604 : Boolean;
      recGuid_L@8053603 : Record 8053609;
      recCropRotation_L@8053602 : Record 8053194;
      recCropRotationLine_L@8053614 : Record 8053195;
      Separator_L@8053606 : Text[1];
      AlreadyUpdated_L@8053616 : Boolean;
      FieldName_L@8053618 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      FieldValue_L@8053617 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      recContact_L@8053000 : Record 5050;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un d'tail d''l'ment de campagne (CMC Crop Rotation Line) (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_elementcampaigndetail';
      Code_L := Code_P;
      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        Doc1_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
        Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
        Doc2_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
        EVALUATE(Int1_L, COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L)));

        recCropRotationLine_L.GET(Doc1_L, Doc2_L, Int1_L);
        recCropRotation_L.GET(recCropRotationLine_L."Contact No.", recCropRotationLine_L."Campaign No.");
        IF recCropRotation_L.Freezed = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          EXIT;
        END;

        IF recContact_L.GET(recCropRotation_L."Contact No.") THEN BEGIN
          IF recContact_L."Contact CRM" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;
        END;

        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053657, Code_P);
        TxtElementCampaignGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053659, recCropRotationLine_L."Contact No." + Separator_L +
          recCropRotationLine_L."Campaign No.");
        VerifGuidFinded(recCropRotationLine_L."Contact No." + Separator_L + recCropRotationLine_L."Campaign No.",
          TxtElementCampaignGuid_L, recCropRotationLine_L.FIELDCAPTION("Campaign No."));

        TxtTranscriptionArticleGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053668, recCropRotationLine_L."Crop Rotation Table Code" +
          Separator_L + FORMAT(recCropRotationLine_L."Line No."));
        VerifGuidFinded(recCropRotationLine_L."Crop Rotation Table Code" + Separator_L + FORMAT(recCropRotationLine_L."Line No."),
          TxtTranscriptionArticleGuid_L, recCropRotationLine_L.FIELDCAPTION("Crop Rotation Table Code"));

        Modify_L := FALSE;
        AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;

        IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            Modify_L := TRUE;
            AlreadyUpdated_L := TRUE;
            cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_calculatedfield', '');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_article', '');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_linenumber', '');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unit', '');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_value', '');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_donotexecutecalcul', '');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_forceupdate', '');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_articletranscript_id', '');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_elementcampaign_id', '');
            IF cduManCon_G.GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
              CASE recCropRotationLine_L."Line Type" OF
                recCropRotationLine_L."Line Type"::Formula: BEGIN
                  IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_calculatedfield') <> '1' THEN
                    AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
                  IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_article') <> recCropRotationLine_L.Range THEN
                    AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
                END;
                recCropRotationLine_L."Line Type"::"Funnel Filter": BEGIN
                  IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_calculatedfield') <> '0' THEN
                    AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
                  IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_article') <> recCropRotationLine_L.Range THEN
                    AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
                END;
                recCropRotationLine_L."Line Type"::Title: BEGIN
                  IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_calculatedfield') <> '1' THEN
                    AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
                  IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_article') <> '' THEN
                    AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
                END;
              END;
              IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_linenumber') <> cduManCon_G.FormatIntToCrm(
                 recCropRotationLine_L."Line No.")
              THEN
                AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
              IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name') <> recCropRotationLine_L.Description THEN
                AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
              IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unit') <> '' THEN
                AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;

              // --> 20160224-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2740-BSI01
              {before
              IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_value') <> cduManCon_G.FormatIntToCrm(
                 recCropRotationLine_L."Area (Ha)")
              after}
              IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_value') <> cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(
                 recCropRotationLine_L."Area (Ha)")
              // <-- 20160224-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2740-BSI01
              THEN
                AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
              IF recCropRotationLine_L."Line Type" = recCropRotationLine_L."Line Type"::Title THEN BEGIN
                IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_donotexecutecalcul') <> '1' THEN
                  AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
              END ELSE BEGIN
                IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_donotexecutecalcul') <> '0' THEN
                  AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
              END;

              IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_forceupdate') <> '0' THEN
                AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;

              IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_hastogeneratedetail') <> '0' THEN
                AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
              IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_isdetailgenerated') <> '1' THEN
                AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
              IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_codecampaign') <> recCropRotation_L."Campaign No." THEN
                AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
              // --> 20160224-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2740-BSI01
              {before
              IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name') <> recCropRotation_L."Campaign No." THEN
              after}
              IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name') <> recCropRotationLine_L.Description THEN
              // <-- 20160224-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2740-BSI01
                AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
              IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_displaytype') <> '175490000' THEN
                AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
              IF NOT(cduManCon_G.CompareGuid(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_articletranscript_id'),
                 TxtTranscriptionArticleGuid_L))
              THEN
                AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
              IF NOT(cduManCon_G.CompareGuid(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_elementcampaign_id'),
                 TxtElementCampaignGuid_L))
              THEN
                AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            END;
          END;
        END;
