        IF AlreadyUpdated_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          EXIT;
        END;

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        CASE recCropRotationLine_L."Line Type" OF
          recCropRotationLine_L."Line Type"::Formula: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_calculatedfield', '1');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_article', recCropRotationLine_L.Range);
          END;
          recCropRotationLine_L."Line Type"::"Funnel Filter": BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_calculatedfield', '0');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_article', recCropRotationLine_L.Range);
          END;
          recCropRotationLine_L."Line Type"::Title: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_calculatedfield', '1');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_article', '');
          END;
        END;
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_linenumber', cduManCon_G.FormatIntToCrm(recCropRotationLine_L."Line No."));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recCropRotationLine_L.Description);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unit', '');
        // --> 20160224-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2740-BSI01
        {before
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_value', cduManCon_G.FormatIntToCrm(recCropRotationLine_L."Area (Ha)"));
        after}
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_value', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recCropRotationLine_L."Area (Ha)"));
        // <-- 20160224-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2740-BSI01

        IF recCropRotationLine_L."Line Type" = recCropRotationLine_L."Line Type"::Title THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_donotexecutecalcul', '1');
        END ELSE BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_donotexecutecalcul', '0');
        END;

        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_forceupdate', '0');
        // --> 20160224-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2740-BSI01
        {before
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recCropRotation_L."Contact Name");
        after}
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name',recCropRotationLine_L.Description);
        // <-- 20160224-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2740-BSI01
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_articletranscript_id', TxtTranscriptionArticleGuid_L);
        //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_elementcampaign_id', TxtElementCampaignGuid_L);
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053657, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
            //Mise .  jour r'ussie
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Mise . jour 'chou'e
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END;
      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression du d'tail 'l'ment de campagne
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053657, Code_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::elementcampaigndetail, 2, 2);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncTypeContratCadre@8053627(Code_P@8053610 : Text[110];TypeAction_P@8053609 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      Code_L@8053605 : Text[110];
      EntityName_L@8053604 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053600 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053608 : Boolean;
      recGuid_L@8053607 : Record 8053609;
      recTypeContract_L@8053611 : Record 8053198;
      FieldName_L@8053602 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      FieldValue_L@8053601 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      recSetup_L@8053000 : Record 8053602;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un type de contrat cadre (T8053198) (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_masteragreementtype';
      Code_L := Code_P;

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        IF NOT(recTypeContract_L.GET(Code_P)) THEN
          EXIT;

        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053670, Code_P);
        Modify_L := FALSE;

        FieldName_L[1] := 'cmc_code';
        FieldValue_L[1] := recTypeContract_L.Code;
        Modify_L := FindExist(EntityName_L, FieldName_L, FieldValue_L, 1, TxtCrmGuid_L);

        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recTypeContract_L.Code);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_denomination', recTypeContract_L.Description);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_cropingplanrequired', cduManCon_G.FormatBoolToCrm(
          recTypeContract_L."Crop Rotation Mandatory"));

        CASE recTypeContract_L.Type OF
          recTypeContract_L.Type::CCC: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490000');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_defaulttype', '0');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_category', '175490001');
          END;
          recTypeContract_L.Type::CCA: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490001');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_defaulttype', '1');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_category', '175490000');
          END;
          recTypeContract_L.Type::Bovin: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490004');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_defaulttype', '0');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_category', '175490002');
          END;
          recTypeContract_L.Type::ELAN: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490002');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_defaulttype', '0');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_category', '175490000');
          END;
          recTypeContract_L.Type::JA: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490003');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_defaulttype', '0');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_category', '175490000');
          END;
          recTypeContract_L.Type::Remere: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490005');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_defaulttype', '0');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_category', '175490002');
          END;
          recTypeContract_L.Type::TAF: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490006');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_defaulttype', '0');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_category', '175490002');
          END;
          recTypeContract_L.Type::Collect: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490007');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_defaulttype', '0');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_category', '175490000');
          END;
        END;

        //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053670, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //Mise .  jour r'ussie
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053670, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Mise . jour 'chou'e
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END;
      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression de l'enregistrement
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053670, Code_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::masteragreementtype, 2, 2);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncModeleContratCadreAppro@8053628(Code_P@8053610 : Text[110];TypeAction_P@8053609 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053608 : Text[50];
      Code_L@8053607 : Text[110];
      Doc1_L@8053603 : Text[30];
      Int1_L@8053604 : Integer;
      Campaign_L@8053000 : Code[10];
      EntityName_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053601 : Boolean;
      recGuid_L@8053600 : Record 8053609;
      recTemplateContratCadre_L@8053605 : Record 8053235;
      TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L@8053611 : Text[50];
      TxtFunnelGuid_L@8053612 : Text[50];
      TxtArticleTranscriptGuid_L@8053613 : Text[50];
      TxtCampaingGuid_L@8053614 : Text[50];
      recCampaign_L@8053615 : Record 8052991;
      Separator_L@8053616 : Text[1];
      FieldName_L@8053618 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      FieldValue_L@8053617 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un ModSle contrat cadre appro (T8053198) (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_supplyagreementtemplate';
      Code_L := Code_P;
      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        Doc1_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
        Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
        EVALUATE(Int1_L, COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1));
        Campaign_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));

        recCampaign_L.GET(Campaign_L);

        recTemplateContratCadre_L.GET(Doc1_L, Int1_L);
        TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053670, recTemplateContratCadre_L."Frame Contract Type");
        VerifGuidFinded(recTemplateContratCadre_L."Frame Contract Type", TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L,
          recTemplateContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Frame Contract Type"));

        TxtFunnelGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053673, recTemplateContratCadre_L."Item Funnel Code");
        VerifGuidFinded(recTemplateContratCadre_L."Item Funnel Code", TxtFunnelGuid_L,
          recTemplateContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Item Funnel Code"));

        TxtCampaingGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053622, recCampaign_L."No.");
        VerifGuidFinded(recCampaign_L."No.", TxtCampaingGuid_L, recCampaign_L.FIELDCAPTION("No."));

        IF recTemplateContratCadre_L."Area Crop Rotation Line No." <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          TxtArticleTranscriptGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053668, recCampaign_L."Crop Rotation Table Code" +
            Separator_L + FORMAT(recTemplateContratCadre_L."Area Crop Rotation Line No."));
          VerifGuidFinded(recCampaign_L."Crop Rotation Table Code" + Separator_L +
            FORMAT(recTemplateContratCadre_L."Area Crop Rotation Line No."), TxtArticleTranscriptGuid_L,
            recTemplateContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Area Crop Rotation Line No."));
        END;

        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053666, Code_P);
        Modify_L := FALSE;

        FieldName_L[1] := 'cmc_masteragreementtypeid';
        FieldValue_L[1] := cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L);
        FieldName_L[2] := 'cmc_linenumber';
        FieldValue_L[2] := cduManCon_G.FormatIntToCrm(recTemplateContratCadre_L."Line No.");
        FieldName_L[3] := 'cmc_productioncampaignid';
        FieldValue_L[3] := cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCampaingGuid_L);
        Modify_L := FindExist(EntityName_L, FieldName_L, FieldValue_L, 3, TxtCrmGuid_L);

        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour

      Modify_L@8053601 : Boolean;
      recGuid_L@8053600 : Record 8053609;
      recAbaque_L@8053613 : Record 8053238;

      TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L@8053611 : Text[50];

      FieldName_L@8053620 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      FieldValue_L@8053619 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un Coefficient abaque (T8053237) (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_abacuscoefficient';

        EVALUATE(Int1_L, COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1));
        Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
        Doc2_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
        Doc3_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));

        IF Doc1_L = '' THEN BEGIN
          EXIT;
        END;

        recAbaque_L.GET(Doc1_L, Int1_L, Doc2_L, Doc3_L);
        TxtCampaignGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053622, recAbaque_L."Campaign No.");
        VerifGuidFinded(recAbaque_L."Campaign No.", TxtCampaignGuid_L, recAbaque_L.FIELDCAPTION("Campaign No."));

        TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053670, recAbaque_L."Frame Contract Type");
        VerifGuidFinded(recAbaque_L."Frame Contract Type", TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L, recAbaque_L.FIELDCAPTION(
          "Frame Contract Type"));

        TxtAbacusType_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053669, Doc1_L + Separator_L + Doc2_L + Separator_L + Doc3_L);
        VerifGuidFinded(Doc1_L + Separator_L + Doc2_L + Separator_L + Doc3_L,
          TxtAbacusType_L, recAbaque_L.FIELDCAPTION("Frame Contract Tp Rate Code"));

        // --> 20150417-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2925-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //TxtSupplyAggrementTemplate_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053666, Doc1_L + Separator_L + FORMAT(Int1_L));
        //AFTER
        TxtSupplyAggrementTemplate_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053666, Doc1_L + Separator_L + FORMAT(Int1_L) +
          Separator_L + recAbaque_L."Campaign No.");
        // <-- 20150417-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2925-FHS01

          TxtSupplyAggrementTemplate_L, recAbaque_L.FIELDCAPTION("Frame Contract Templ. Line No."));
        recTemplateContratCadre_L.GET(Doc1_L, Int1_L);

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_value', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recAbaque_L.Value));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recTemplateContratCadre_L.Description);

        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_supplyagreementtemplateid', TxtSupplyAggrementTemplate_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid', TxtCampaignGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_masteragreementtypeid', TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L);

        //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN

    PROCEDURE SyncEngagement@8053632(Code_P@8053610 : Text[110];TypeAction_P@8053609 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053608 : Text[50];
      Code_L@8053607 : Text[110];
      EntityName_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      Doc1_L@8053605 : Text[30];
      Doc2_L@8053604 : Text[30];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053601 : Boolean;
      recGuid_L@8053600 : Record 8053609;
      recCommitment_L@8053603 : Record 8053199;
      TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L@8053611 : Text[50];
      recContrat_L@8053612 : Record 8053198;
      recSetup_L@8053613 : Record 8053602;
      Separator_L@8053614 : Text[1];
      FieldName_L@8053616 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      FieldValue_L@8053615 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un Engagement (T8053199) (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_commitment';
      Code_L := Code_P;
      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();

      Doc1_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
      Doc2_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));

      IF (Doc1_L = '') OR (Doc2_L = '') THEN BEGIN
        EXIT;
      END;

      recCommitment_L.GET(Doc1_L, Doc2_L);
      recContrat_L.GET(recCommitment_L."Frame Contract Type");

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053670, recCommitment_L."Frame Contract Type");
        VerifGuidFinded(recCommitment_L."Frame Contract Type", TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L,
          recCommitment_L.FIELDCAPTION("Frame Contract Type"));

        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053660, Code_P);
        Modify_L := FALSE;

        FieldName_L[1] := 'cmc_code';
        FieldValue_L[1] := recCommitment_L.Code;
        FieldName_L[2] := 'cmc_masteragreementtypeid';
        FieldValue_L[2] := cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L);
        Modify_L := FindExist(EntityName_L, FieldName_L, FieldValue_L, 2, TxtCrmGuid_L);

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recCommitment_L.Code);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_designation', recCommitment_L.Description);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_checklinefilter', recCommitment_L."Check Line Filter");
        IF (TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L <> '') AND (recCommitment_L."Frame Contract Type" <> '') THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_masteragreementtypeid', TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L);
        END;

        CASE recCommitment_L."Commitment Type" OF
          recCommitment_L."Commitment Type"::"Total Campaign Price": BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_commitmenttype', '175490000');
          END;
          recCommitment_L."Commitment Type"::"Partial Campaign Price": BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_commitmenttype', '175490001');
          END;
          recCommitment_L."Commitment Type"::Partial: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_commitmenttype', '175490002');
          END;
        END;

        //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053660, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //Mise .  jour r'ussie
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053660, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Mise . jour 'chou'e
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END;
      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression de l'enregistrement
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053660, Code_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::commitment, 2, 2);
      END;
    END;

      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053608 : Text[50];
      Code_L@8053607 : Text[110];
      EntityName_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      Doc1_L@8053605 : Text[30];
      Doc2_L@8053604 : Text[30];
      Doc3_L@8053603 : Text[30];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053601 : Boolean;
      recGuid_L@8053600 : Record 8053609;
      recFunnel_L@8053611 : Record 8053358;
      recFunnel_Line_L@8053612 : Record 8053359;
      recActivity_L@8053613 : Record 8052985;
      recFamily_L@8053614 : Record 8052986;
      recSubFamily_L@8053615 : Record 8052987;
      recSpecies_L@8053616 : Record 8052988;
      recItem_L@8053617 : Record 27;
      recVariant_L@8053618 : Record 5401;
      TxtGuid_L@8053619 : Text[50];
      ActivityFilter_L@8053620 : Text[250];
      SubFamilyFilter_L@8053621 : Text[250];
      FamilyFilter_L@8053622 : Text[250];
      SpeciesFilter_L@8053623 : Text[250];
      recSendedTmp_L@8053624 : TEMPORARY Record 8053601;
      recToDeleteTmp_L@8053625 : TEMPORARY Record 8053601;
      Count_L@8053627 : Integer;
      i_L@8053626 : Integer;
      LigneGuid_L@8053628 : Text[50];
      FieldName_L@8053630 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      FieldValue_L@8053629 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un Entonnoir article (T8053358) (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_funnelfilter';
      Code_L := Code_P;

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        IF Code_P = '' THEN
          EXIT;

        recFunnel_L.GET(Code_P);

        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053673, Code_P);
        Modify_L := FALSE;

        IF cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
          TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.SearchGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', Code_P, '', '', '', '');
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053673, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END;
        END;

        IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END;
        END;

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recFunnel_L.Code);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_designation', recFunnel_L.Description);

        //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053673, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
            //Mise .  jour r'ussie
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Mise . jour 'chou'e
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END;

        //Ajout des lignes
        recSendedTmp_L.DELETEALL;
        recToDeleteTmp_L.DELETEALL;
        //Activit'
        IF recFunnel_Line_L.GET(Code_P, recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Type"::Activity) THEN BEGIN
          recFunnel_L.GET(recFunnel_Line_L.Code);
          ActivityFilter_L := recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Line Filter";
          recActivity_L.RESET;
          recActivity_L.SETFILTER(Code, ActivityFilter_L);
          IF recActivity_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053648, recActivity_L.Code);
              IF TxtGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
                LigneGuid_L := SyncLigneEntonnoirArticle(TxtCrmGuid_L, recActivity_L.Name,
                  recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Type"::Activity, TxtGuid_L);
                //on remplit une table temporaire pour la phase de suppression
                recSendedTmp_L.INIT;
                recSendedTmp_L."Type Data" := recSendedTmp_L."Type Data"::Activity;
                recSendedTmp_L."Code Nav" := recActivity_L.Code;
                recSendedTmp_L.GUID := LigneGuid_L;
                recSendedTmp_L.INSERT;
              END;
            UNTIL recActivity_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

        //Famille
        IF recFunnel_Line_L.GET(Code_P, recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Type"::Family) THEN BEGIN
          recFunnel_L.GET(recFunnel_Line_L.Code);
          FamilyFilter_L := recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Line Filter";
          recFamily_L.RESET;
          recFamily_L.SETFILTER(Code, recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Line Filter");
          IF recFamily_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053649, recFamily_L.Code);
              IF TxtGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
                LigneGuid_L := SyncLigneEntonnoirArticle(TxtCrmGuid_L, recFamily_L.Name,
                  recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Type"::Family, TxtGuid_L);
                //on remplit une table temporaire pour la phase de suppression
                recSendedTmp_L.INIT;
                recSendedTmp_L."Type Data" := recSendedTmp_L."Type Data"::Family;
                recSendedTmp_L."Code Nav" := recFamily_L.Code;
                recSendedTmp_L.GUID := LigneGuid_L;
                recSendedTmp_L.INSERT;
              END;
            UNTIL recFamily_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

        //Sous-famille
        IF recFunnel_Line_L.GET(Code_P, recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Type"::SubFamily) THEN BEGIN
          recFunnel_L.GET(recFunnel_Line_L.Code);
          SubFamilyFilter_L := recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Line Filter";
          recSubFamily_L.RESET;
          recSubFamily_L.SETFILTER(Code, recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Line Filter");
          IF recSubFamily_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053650, recSubFamily_L.Code);
              IF TxtGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
                LigneGuid_L := SyncLigneEntonnoirArticle(TxtCrmGuid_L, recSubFamily_L.Name,
                  recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Type"::SubFamily, TxtGuid_L);
                //on remplit une table temporaire pour la phase de suppression
                recSendedTmp_L.INIT;
                recSendedTmp_L."Type Data" := recSendedTmp_L."Type Data"::"Sub Family";
                recSendedTmp_L."Code Nav" := recSubFamily_L.Code;
                recSendedTmp_L.GUID := LigneGuid_L;
                recSendedTmp_L.INSERT;
              END;
            UNTIL recSubFamily_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

        //EspSce
        IF recFunnel_Line_L.GET(Code_P, recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Type"::Species) THEN BEGIN
          recFunnel_L.GET(recFunnel_Line_L.Code);
          SpeciesFilter_L := recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Line Filter";
          recSpecies_L.RESET;
          recSpecies_L.SETFILTER(Code, recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Line Filter");
          IF recSpecies_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053651, recSpecies_L.Code);
              IF TxtGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
                LigneGuid_L := SyncLigneEntonnoirArticle(TxtCrmGuid_L, recSpecies_L.Name,
                  recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Type"::Species, TxtGuid_L);
                //on remplit une table temporaire pour la phase de suppression
                recSendedTmp_L.INIT;
                recSendedTmp_L."Type Data" := recSendedTmp_L."Type Data"::Species;
                recSendedTmp_L."Code Nav" := recSpecies_L.Code;
                recSendedTmp_L.GUID := LigneGuid_L;
                recSendedTmp_L.INSERT;
              END;
            UNTIL recSpecies_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

        //Article
        IF recFunnel_Line_L.GET(Code_P, recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Type"::Item) THEN BEGIN
          recFunnel_L.GET(recFunnel_Line_L.Code);
          recItem_L.RESET;
          recItem_L.SETFILTER("No.", recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Line Filter");
          IF recItem_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053648, recItem_L."No.");
              IF TxtGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
                LigneGuid_L := SyncLigneEntonnoirArticle(TxtCrmGuid_L, recItem_L.Description,
                  recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Type"::Item, TxtGuid_L);
                //on remplit une table temporaire pour la phase de suppression
                recSendedTmp_L.INIT;
                recSendedTmp_L."Type Data" := recSendedTmp_L."Type Data"::Item;
                recSendedTmp_L."Code Nav" := recItem_L."No.";
                recSendedTmp_L.GUID := LigneGuid_L;
                recSendedTmp_L.INSERT;
              END;
              //Variante
              recVariant_L.RESET;
              recVariant_L.SETRANGE("Item No.", recItem_L."No.");
              IF recVariant_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
                REPEAT
                  TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053648, recVariant_L."Item No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() +
                    recVariant_L.Code);
                  IF TxtGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    LigneGuid_L := SyncLigneEntonnoirArticle(TxtCrmGuid_L, recVariant_L.Description,
                      recFunnel_Line_L."Funnel Type"::Item, TxtGuid_L);
                    //on remplit une table temporaire pour la phase de suppression
                    recSendedTmp_L.INIT;
                    recSendedTmp_L."Type Data" := recSendedTmp_L."Type Data"::Item;
                    recSendedTmp_L."Code Nav" := recVariant_L."Item No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() + recVariant_L.Code;
                    recSendedTmp_L.GUID := LigneGuid_L;
                    recSendedTmp_L.INSERT;
                  END;
                UNTIL recVariant_L.NEXT = 0;
              END;
            UNTIL recItem_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END;

        //marquage des lignes en trop dans la GRC
        EntityName_L := 'cmc_funnelfilterline';
        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfiltertype', '');
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_activity_id', '');
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_family_id', '');
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_subfamily_id', '');
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_species_id', '');
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_product_id', '');
        cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
        cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfilter_id', '=', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCrmGuid_L));
        Count_L := cduManCon_G.SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
        IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
          i_L := 0;
          REPEAT
            i_L := i_L + 1;
            //R'cup'ration du GUID
            TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id');
            recToDeleteTmp_L.INIT;
            CASE cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfiltertype') OF
              '175490000': BEGIN
                  recToDeleteTmp_L."Type Data" := recToDeleteTmp_L."Type Data"::Activity;
              END;
              '175490001': BEGIN
                  recToDeleteTmp_L."Type Data" := recToDeleteTmp_L."Type Data"::Family;
              END;
              '175490002': BEGIN
                  recToDeleteTmp_L."Type Data" := recToDeleteTmp_L."Type Data"::"Sub Family";
              END;
              '175490003': BEGIN
                  recToDeleteTmp_L."Type Data" := recToDeleteTmp_L."Type Data"::Species;
              END;
              '175490004': BEGIN
                  recToDeleteTmp_L."Type Data" := recToDeleteTmp_L."Type Data"::Item;
              END;
            END;
            recToDeleteTmp_L."Code Nav" := FORMAT(i_L);
            recToDeleteTmp_L.GUID := cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id'));
            recToDeleteTmp_L.INSERT;
          UNTIL NOT(cduManCon_G.NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
        END;

        //suppression des lignes en trop dans la GRC
        recToDeleteTmp_L.RESET;
        IF recToDeleteTmp_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            recSendedTmp_L.RESET;
            recSendedTmp_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Data", GUID);
            recSendedTmp_L.SETRANGE("Type Data", recToDeleteTmp_L."Type Data");
            recSendedTmp_L.SETRANGE(GUID, recToDeleteTmp_L.GUID);
            IF recSendedTmp_L.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
              cduManCon_G.DeleteRecord(EntityName_L, cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(recToDeleteTmp_L.GUID));
            END;
          UNTIL recToDeleteTmp_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;

      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression de l'enregistrement
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053673, Code_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::funnelfilter, 2, 2);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncLigneEntonnoirArticle@8053638(FunnelGuid_P@8053602 : Text[50];Description_P@8053608 : Text[30];Type_P@8053600 : 'Activity,Family,SubFamily,Species,Item';EntityGuid_P@8053603 : Text[50]) Result_L : Text[50];
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053610 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053605 : Boolean;
      recGuid_L@8053604 : Record 8053609;
      Count_L@8053601 : Integer;
      Update_L@8053607 : Boolean;
      FieldName_L@8053611 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      FieldValue_L@8053609 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'une ligne entonnoir article (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - FunnelGuid_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - Description_P : Description
      //    - Type_P : Type de filtre
      //    - EntityGuid_P : GUID de l'entit' . inclure dans le filtre
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_funnelfilterline';
      Result_L := '';

      //on recherche si cette ligne existe d'j.
      Modify_L := FALSE;
      cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
      cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
      cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '');
      cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_mode', '');
      cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '=', '1');
      cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfilter_id', '=', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(FunnelGuid_P));
      CASE Type_P OF
        Type_P::Activity: BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_activity_id', '=', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(EntityGuid_P));
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfiltertype', '=', '175490000');
        END;
        Type_P::Family: BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_family_id', '=', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(EntityGuid_P));
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfiltertype', '=', '175490001');
        END;
        Type_P::SubFamily: BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_subfamily_id', '=', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(EntityGuid_P));
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfiltertype', '=', '175490002');
        END;
        Type_P::Species: BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_species_id', '=', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(EntityGuid_P));
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfiltertype', '=', '175490003');
        END;
        Type_P::Item: BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_product_id', '=', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(EntityGuid_P));
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfiltertype', '=', '175490004');
        END;
      END;
      Count_L := cduManCon_G.SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
      Update_L := TRUE;
      IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id');
        Modify_L := TRUE;
      END;

      IF Modify_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
        IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name') = Description_P THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_mode') = '1' THEN BEGIN
            Update_L := FALSE;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      IF (Modify_L = FALSE) OR (Update_L = TRUE) THEN BEGIN
        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfilter_id', FunnelGuid_P);
        CASE Type_P OF
          Type_P::Activity: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_activity_id', EntityGuid_P);
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfiltertype', '175490000');
          END;
          Type_P::Family: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_family_id', EntityGuid_P);
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfiltertype', '175490001');
          END;
          Type_P::SubFamily: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_subfamily_id', EntityGuid_P);
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfiltertype', '175490002');
          END;
          Type_P::Species: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_species_id', EntityGuid_P);
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfiltertype', '175490003');
          END;
          Type_P::Item: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_product_id', EntityGuid_P);
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfiltertype', '175490004');
          END;
        END;
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', Description_P);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_mode', '1');

        //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //Mise .  jour r'ussie
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Mise . jour 'chou'e
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END;
      END;
      Result_L := cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(TxtCrmGuid_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncTypeContratCadreCollecte@8053634(Code_P@8053610 : Text[110];TypeAction_P@8053609 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053608 : Text[50];
      Code_L@8053607 : Text[110];
      EntityName_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      Doc1_L@8053605 : Text[30];
      Doc2_L@8053604 : Text[30];
      Doc3_L@8053603 : Text[30];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053601 : Boolean;
      recGuid_L@8053600 : Record 8053609;
      recTypeContract_L@8053611 : Record 8053096;
      FieldName_L@8053613 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      FieldValue_L@8053612 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un Type contrat cadre collecte (T8053198) (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_collectmasteragreementtype';
      Code_L := Code_P;

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        recTypeContract_L.GET(Code_P);

        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053671, Code_P);
        Modify_L := FALSE;

        FieldName_L[1] := 'cmc_code';
        FieldValue_L[1] := recTypeContract_L."Contract Type";
        Modify_L := FindExist(EntityName_L, FieldName_L, FieldValue_L, 1, TxtCrmGuid_L);

        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_allowedinCollectagreement', cduManCon_G.FormatBoolToCrm(
                                                                            recTypeContract_L."Allow In Frame Contract"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recTypeContract_L.Description);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_code', recTypeContract_L."Contract Type");

        //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053671, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //Mise .  jour r'ussie
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053671, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Mise . jour 'chou'e
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END;
      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression de l'enregistrement
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053671, Code_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::collectmasteragreementtype, 2, 2);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncEntonnoirContratCollecte@8053635(Code_P@8053610 : Text[110];TypeAction_P@8053609 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053608 : Text[50];
      Code_L@8053607 : Text[110];
      EntityName_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      Doc1_L@8053605 : Text[30];
      Doc2_L@8053604 : Text[30];
      Doc3_L@8053603 : Text[30];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053601 : Boolean;
      recGuid_L@8053600 : Record 8053609;
      recFunnel_L@8053611 : Record 8053196;
      TxtCampaingGuid_L@8053612 : Text[50];
      TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L@8053613 : Text[50];
      TxtProductGuid_L@8053614 : Text[50];
      TxtSpeciesGuid_L@8053615 : Text[50];
      TxtContractGuid_L@8053616 : Text[50];
      recFunnelDesc_L@8053617 : Record 8053358;
      Separator_L@8053618 : Text[1];
      TxtContractDefaultGuid_L@8053619 : Text[50];
      FieldName_L@8053621 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      FieldValue_L@8053620 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un Entonnoir contrat˜collecte (T8053196) (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_collectagreementfunnelfilter';
      Code_L := Code_P;
      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        Doc1_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
        Doc2_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));

        recFunnel_L.GET(Doc1_L, Doc2_L);
        recFunnelDesc_L.GET(recFunnel_L."Item Funnel Code");

        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053663, Code_P);
        TxtCampaingGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053622, recFunnel_L."Campaign No.");
        VerifGuidFinded(recFunnel_L."Campaign No.", TxtCampaingGuid_L, recFunnel_L.FIELDCAPTION("Campaign No."));

        TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053673, recFunnel_L."Item Funnel Code");
        VerifGuidFinded(recFunnel_L."Item Funnel Code", TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L, recFunnel_L.FIELDCAPTION("Item Funnel Code"));

        TxtContractGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053671, recFunnel_L."Default Complet Contract Type");
        VerifGuidFinded(recFunnel_L."Default Complet Contract Type", TxtContractGuid_L,
                        recFunnel_L.FIELDCAPTION("Default Complet Contract Type"));

        TxtContractDefaultGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053671, recFunnel_L."Default Partiel Contract Type");
        VerifGuidFinded(recFunnel_L."Default Partiel Contract Type", TxtContractDefaultGuid_L,
                        recFunnel_L.FIELDCAPTION(recFunnel_L."Default Partiel Contract Type"));

        TxtSpeciesGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053651, recFunnel_L."Default Species Code");
        VerifGuidFinded(recFunnel_L."Default Species Code", TxtSpeciesGuid_L, recFunnel_L.FIELDCAPTION("Default Species Code"));

        TxtProductGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, recFunnel_L."Default Item No.");
        VerifGuidFinded(recFunnel_L."Default Item No.", TxtProductGuid_L, recFunnel_L.FIELDCAPTION("Default Item No."));

        Modify_L := FALSE;

        FieldName_L[1] := 'cmc_funnelfilterid';
        FieldValue_L[1] := cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L);
        FieldName_L[2] := 'cmc_productioncampaignid';
        FieldValue_L[2] := cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCampaingGuid_L);
        Modify_L := FindExist(EntityName_L, FieldName_L, FieldValue_L, 2, TxtCrmGuid_L);

        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recFunnelDesc_L.Description);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_includedincollectagreement', cduManCon_G.FormatBoolToCrm(
          recFunnel_L."Include in Frame Contract"));
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_fullratedefaultagreementtype_id', TxtContractGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectmasteragreementtypeid', TxtContractDefaultGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productid', TxtProductGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_speciesid', TxtSpeciesGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfilterid', TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid', TxtCampaingGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_requiredfullrate', cduManCon_G.FormatBoolToCrm(
          recFunnel_L."Rate 100pc Contract Mandatory"));

        //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053663, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //Mise .  jour r'ussie
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053663, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Mise . jour 'chou'e
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END;
      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression de l'enregistrement
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053663, Code_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::collectagreementfunnelfilter, 2, 2);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncEngContratCadreCollecte@8053636(Code_P@8053610 : Text[110];TypeAction_P@8053609 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053608 : Text[50];
      Code_L@8053607 : Text[110];
      EntityName_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      Doc1_L@8053605 : Text[30];
      Doc2_L@8053604 : Text[30];
      Doc3_L@8053615 : Text[30];
      Date1_L@8053619 : Date;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053601 : Boolean;
      recGuid_L@8053600 : Record 8053609;
      recCommitment_L@8053603 : Record 8053214;
      recContrat_L@8053612 : Record 8053198;
      TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L@8053613 : Text[50];
      TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L@8053614 : Text[50];
      TxtCampaingGuid_L@8053616 : Text[50];
      recFunnel_L@8053617 : Record 8053358;
      Separator_L@8053618 : Text[1];
      FieldName_L@8053620 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      FieldValue_L@8053611 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un Engagement contrat cadre collecte (T8053199) (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_collectagreementcommitment';
      Code_L := Code_P;
      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();

      Doc1_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
      Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
      Doc2_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
      Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
      Doc3_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
      EVALUATE(Date1_L, COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L)));

      // --> 20151126-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2463-BS01
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) <> TextDelete THEN BEGIN
      // <-- 20151126-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2463-BS01
      IF NOT(recCommitment_L.GET(Doc1_L, Doc2_L, Doc3_L, Date1_L)) THEN
        EXIT;
      recFunnel_L.GET(recCommitment_L."Item Funnel Code");
      // --> 20151126-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2463-BS01
      END;
      // <-- 20151126-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2463-BS01

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        // --> 20151126-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2463-BS01
        {before
        TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053671, recCommitment_L."Contract Type");
        after}
        TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053672, recCommitment_L."Contract Type");
        // <-- 20151126-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-2463-BS01

        // <-- 20150420-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2929-FHS01
        VerifGuidFinded(recCommitment_L."Contract Type", TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L, recCommitment_L.FIELDCAPTION("Contract Type"));

        TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053663, recCommitment_L."Campaign No." + Separator_L +
          recCommitment_L."Item Funnel Code");
        VerifGuidFinded(recCommitment_L."Campaign No." + Separator_L + recCommitment_L."Item Funnel Code",
          TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L, recCommitment_L.FIELDCAPTION("Item Funnel Code"));

        TxtCampaingGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053622, recCommitment_L."Campaign No.");
        VerifGuidFinded(recCommitment_L."Campaign No.", TxtCampaingGuid_L, recCommitment_L.FIELDCAPTION("Campaign No."));

        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053662, Code_P);
        Modify_L := FALSE;

        FieldName_L[1] := 'cmc_collectagreementfunnelfilterid';
        FieldValue_L[1] := cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L);
        FieldName_L[2] := 'cmc_collectmasteragreementtypeid';
        FieldValue_L[2] := cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L);
        FieldName_L[3] := 'cmc_productioncampaign_id';
        FieldValue_L[3] := cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCampaingGuid_L);
        FieldName_L[4] := 'cmc_maxcommitmentdate';
        FieldValue_L[4] := cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recCommitment_L."Modify Ending Date");
        Modify_L := FindExist(EntityName_L, FieldName_L, FieldValue_L, 4, TxtCrmGuid_L);

        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_maxcommitmentdate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recCommitment_L."Modify Ending Date"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_maxcommitmentpercent', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recCommitment_L."Max Commitment %"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recFunnel_L.Description);

        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectagreementfunnelfilterid', TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectmasteragreementtypeid', TxtMasterAgreementTypeGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaign_id', TxtCampaingGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_price', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recCommitment_L."Informative Price"));

        // --> 20161025-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22601-BSI01
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_mincommitmentdate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recCommitment_L."Modify Starting Date"))
      ;
        // <-- 20161025-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22601-BSI01

        //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053662, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //Mise .  jour r'ussie
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053662, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Mise . jour 'chou'e
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END;
      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression de l'enregistrement
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053662, Code_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::collectagreementcommitment, 2, 2);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncUpdateNbContratPF@8053005(Code_P@8053002 : Text[110];TypeAction_P@8053001 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053000 : Text[50];
      recContrat_L@8053004 : Record 8052983;
      TxtAccountGuid_L@8053009 : Text[50];
      TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L@8053010 : Text[50];
      TxtProductionCampaignGuid_L@8053011 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053012 : Text[50];
      recType_L@8053005 : Record 8053096;
      ContactNo_L@8053003 : Code[20];
      cduCVMan_L@8053006 : Codeunit 8053377;
      recCamp_L@8053007 : Code[10];
      recCampaign_L@8053008 : Record 8052991;
      recLigneModele_L@8053013 : Record 8053235;
      recLigneEnt_L@8053014 : Record 8053359;
      EntFound_L@8053015 : Code[10];
      recItem_L@8053016 : Record 27;
      RecContact_L@1000000000 : Record 5050;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Mise . jour de la liste des contrats PF (NAV -> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'agriculteur
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // --> 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2862-FHS01
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_contractfirmpricesubscribed';

      //Supression
      cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
      cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '=', Code_P);
      cduManCon_G.DeleteMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        IF recContrat_L.GET(Code_P) THEN
          // --> 20150416-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2862-FHS01
          IF recContrat_L.Type <> recContrat_L.Type::Template THEN BEGIN
          // <-- 20150416-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2862-FHS01
          //Suppression si existante
            IF recType_L.GET(recContrat_L."Contract Type") THEN
              IF recType_L."Qte PF on CCC" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
                //on envoie uniquement ceux qui sont en Qte PF sur ccc
                ContactNo_L := cduCVMan_L.GetContNoFromCustVentNo(recContrat_L."Vendor No.", 1);

                // --> 20180216-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33316-BSI
                IF NOT RecContact_L.GET(ContactNo_L) THEN
                  CLEAR(RecContact_L);
                IF NOT RecContact_L."Contact CRM" THEN
                  EXIT;
                // <-- 20180216-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33316-BSI

                TxtAccountGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053626, ContactNo_L);
                VerifGuidFinded(ContactNo_L, TxtAccountGuid_L, recContrat_L.FIELDCAPTION("Vendor No."));

                recCampaign_L.GET(recContrat_L."Campaign No.");
                recCampaign_L.RESET;
                recCampaign_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Code","Starting Date");
                recCampaign_L.SETFILTER("Ending Date", '<%1', recCampaign_L."Starting Date");
                recCampaign_L.FINDLAST;
                recCamp_L := recCampaign_L."No.";

                TxtProductionCampaignGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053622, recCamp_L);
                VerifGuidFinded(recCamp_L, TxtProductionCampaignGuid_L,
                  recContrat_L.FIELDCAPTION("Campaign No."));

                // --> 20150416-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2862-FHS01
                //BEFORE
                {
                recLigneModele_L.RESET;
                //recLigneModele_L.SETRANGE("Frame Contract Type", recContrat_L."Contract Type");
                recLigneModele_L.SETRANGE("Collect Contract Template", recContrat_L."Template No.");
                recLigneModele_L.FINDFIRST;

                TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053673, recLigneModele_L."Item Funnel Code");
                VerifGuidFinded(recLigneModele_L."Item Funnel Code", TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L,
                  recLigneModele_L.FIELDCAPTION("Item Funnel Code"));
                }
                //AFTER
                recItem_L.RESET;
                recItem_L.SETRANGE("No.", recContrat_L."Item No.");

                // --> 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                //BEFORE
                //EntFound_L := FALSE;
                //AFTER
                EntFound_L := '';
                // <-- 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01

                // --> 20150429-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                recLigneEnt_L.RESET;
                recLigneEnt_L.SETRANGE("Funnel Type", recLigneEnt_L."Funnel Type"::Item);
                IF recLigneEnt_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
                  REPEAT
                    recItem_L.FILTERGROUP(10);
                    recItem_L.SETFILTER("No.", recLigneEnt_L."Funnel Line Filter");
                    recItem_L.FILTERGROUP(0);
                    IF NOT(recItem_L.ISEMPTY) THEN
                  // --> 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                  //BEFORE
                  //    EntFound_L := TRUE;
                  //UNTIL (recLigneEnt_L.NEXT = 0) OR (EntFound_L = TRUE);
                  //AFTER
                      EntFound_L := recLigneEnt_L.Code;
                  UNTIL (recLigneEnt_L.NEXT = 0) OR (EntFound_L <> '');
                  // <-- 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01

                // --> 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                //BEFORE
                //IF EntFound_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
                //AFTER
                IF EntFound_L = '' THEN BEGIN
                // <-- 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                  recLigneEnt_L.SETRANGE("Funnel Type", recLigneEnt_L."Funnel Type"::Species);
                  recItem_L.RESET;
                  recItem_L.SETRANGE("No.", recContrat_L."Item No.");
                  IF recLigneEnt_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
                    REPEAT
                      recItem_L.SETFILTER("Species Code", recLigneEnt_L."Funnel Line Filter");
                      IF NOT(recItem_L.ISEMPTY) THEN
                  // --> 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                  //BEFORE
                  //    EntFound_L := TRUE;
                  //UNTIL (recLigneEnt_L.NEXT = 0) OR (EntFound_L = TRUE);
                  //AFTER
                      EntFound_L := recLigneEnt_L.Code;
                  UNTIL (recLigneEnt_L.NEXT = 0) OR (EntFound_L <> '');
                  // <-- 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                END;

                // --> 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                //BEFORE
                //IF EntFound_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
                //AFTER
                IF EntFound_L = '' THEN BEGIN
                // <-- 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                  recLigneEnt_L.SETRANGE("Funnel Type", recLigneEnt_L."Funnel Type"::SubFamily);
                  recItem_L.RESET;
                  recItem_L.SETRANGE("No.", recContrat_L."Item No.");
                  IF recLigneEnt_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
                    REPEAT
                      recItem_L.SETFILTER("Sub-Family Code", recLigneEnt_L."Funnel Line Filter");
                      IF NOT(recItem_L.ISEMPTY) THEN
                  // --> 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                  //BEFORE
                  //    EntFound_L := TRUE;
                  //UNTIL (recLigneEnt_L.NEXT = 0) OR (EntFound_L = TRUE);
                  //AFTER
                      EntFound_L := recLigneEnt_L.Code;
                  UNTIL (recLigneEnt_L.NEXT = 0) OR (EntFound_L <> '');
                  // <-- 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                END;

                // --> 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                //BEFORE
                //IF EntFound_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
                //AFTER
                IF EntFound_L = '' THEN BEGIN
                // <-- 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                  recLigneEnt_L.SETRANGE("Funnel Type", recLigneEnt_L."Funnel Type"::Family);
                  recItem_L.RESET;
                  recItem_L.SETRANGE("No.", recContrat_L."Item No.");
                  IF recLigneEnt_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
                    REPEAT
                      recItem_L.SETFILTER("Family Code", recLigneEnt_L."Funnel Line Filter");
                      IF NOT(recItem_L.ISEMPTY) THEN
                  // --> 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                  //BEFORE
                  //    EntFound_L := TRUE;
                  //UNTIL (recLigneEnt_L.NEXT = 0) OR (EntFound_L = TRUE);
                  //AFTER
                      EntFound_L := recLigneEnt_L.Code;
                  UNTIL (recLigneEnt_L.NEXT = 0) OR (EntFound_L <> '');
                  // <-- 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                END;

                // --> 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                //BEFORE
                //IF EntFound_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
                //AFTER
                IF EntFound_L = '' THEN BEGIN
                // <-- 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                  recLigneEnt_L.SETRANGE("Funnel Type", recLigneEnt_L."Funnel Type"::Activity);
                  recItem_L.RESET;
                  recItem_L.SETRANGE("No.", recContrat_L."Item No.");
                  IF recLigneEnt_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
                    REPEAT
                      recItem_L.SETFILTER(recItem_L."Activity Code", recLigneEnt_L."Funnel Line Filter");
                      IF NOT(recItem_L.ISEMPTY) THEN
                  // --> 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                  //BEFORE
                  //    EntFound_L := TRUE;
                  //UNTIL (recLigneEnt_L.NEXT = 0) OR (EntFound_L = TRUE);
                  //AFTER
                      EntFound_L := recLigneEnt_L.Code;
                  UNTIL (recLigneEnt_L.NEXT = 0) OR (EntFound_L <> '');
                  // <-- 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                END;
                // <-- 20150429-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01

                // --> 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                //BEFORE
                //IF EntFound_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
                //AFTER
                IF EntFound_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
                // <-- 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                  // --> 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                  //BEFORE
                  //TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053673, recLigneEnt_L.Code);
                  //AFTER
                  TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053673, EntFound_L);
                  // <-- 20150604-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2846-FHS01
                  VerifGuidFinded(recLigneEnt_L.Code, TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L,
                    recLigneEnt_L.FIELDCAPTION(Code));
                // <-- 20150416-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2862-FHS01
                  //Cr'ation de la ligne
                  cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
                  cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', Code_P);
                  cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_accountid', TxtAccountGuid_L);
                  cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfilterid', TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L);
                  cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid', TxtProductionCampaignGuid_L);
                  IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                  END;
              // --> 20150416-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2862-FHS01
                END;
              END;
              // <-- 20150416-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2862-FHS01
          END;
      END;
      // <-- 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2862-FHS01
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncContratCadreCollecte@8053736(Code_P@8053609 : Text[110];TypeAction_P@8053602 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      recContratCadre_L@8053618 : Record 8053208;
      Modify_L@8053614 : Boolean;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053610 : Text[50];
      Code_L@8053600 : Text[110];
      TxtAccountGuid_L@8053601 : Text[50];
      TxtProductionCampaignGuid_L@8053619 : Text[50];
      TxtCommitmentGuid_L@8053620 : Text[50];
      TxtCropRotationGuid_L@8053622 : Text[50];
      TxtContractTypeGuid_L@8053621 : Text[50];
      recGuid_L@8053607 : Record 8053609;
      Separator_L@8053604 : Text[1];
      recLigneContrat_L@8053605 : Record 8053209;
      AlreadyUpdated_L@8053606 : Boolean;
      FieldName_L@8053611 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      FieldValue_L@8053608 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      recContact_L@8053000 : Record 5050;
      recTypeContratCadre_L@8053001 : Record 8053198;
      recContractCamp_L@8053002 : Record 8053324;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Importation du contrat cadre collecte dans Nav depuis le CRM (NAV -> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_collectmasteragreement';
      Code_L := Code_P;
      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        recContratCadre_L.GET(Code_P);

        IF recContact_L.GET(recContratCadre_L."Contact No.") THEN BEGIN
          IF recContact_L."Contact CRM" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;
        END;

        IF recContratCadre_L."Synchro Status" <> recContratCadre_L."Synchro Status"::"ERP validated" THEN BEGIN
          TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053656, Code_P);
          IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_erpagreementnumber', '');
            IF cduManCon_G.GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
              IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_erpagreementnumber') <> recContratCadre_L."Contract No." THEN BEGIN
                cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_erpagreementnumber', recContratCadre_L."Contract No.");
                IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
                END;
              END;
            END;
          END;
          EXIT;
        END;

        IF (recContratCadre_L."Synchro Status" <> recContratCadre_L."Synchro Status"::"ERP validated") OR
          (recContratCadre_L."Last Synchro Date" > recContratCadre_L."Last Modification Date")
        THEN BEGIN
          EXIT;
        END;

        IF recContractCamp_L.GET(recContratCadre_L."Campaign No.", recContratCadre_L."Contract Type") THEN BEGIN
          IF recContractCamp_L."Modify Ending Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT(WORKDATE > recContractCamp_L."Modify Ending Date") OR
               (recContratCadre_L."Synchro Status" <> recContratCadre_L."Synchro Status"::"ERP validated")
            THEN BEGIN
              TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053656, Code_P);
              cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
              cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_erpagreementnumber', recContratCadre_L."Contract No.");
              IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
              END;
              EXIT;
            END;
          END;
        END;

        recLigneContrat_L.RESET;
        recLigneContrat_L.SETRANGE("Contract No.", recContratCadre_L."Contract No.");
        IF recLigneContrat_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            cduManCon_G.AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::LigneContratCadreCollect, Code_P + Separator_L +
              FORMAT(recLigneContrat_L."Line No."), TextModify, '');
          UNTIL recLigneContrat_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;

        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        TxtAccountGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053626, recContratCadre_L."Contact No.");
        VerifGuidFinded(recContratCadre_L."Contact No.", TxtAccountGuid_L, recContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Contact No."));

        TxtProductionCampaignGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053622, recContratCadre_L."Crop Rotation Campaign No.");
        VerifGuidFinded(recContratCadre_L."Crop Rotation Campaign No.", TxtProductionCampaignGuid_L,
          recContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Crop Rotation Campaign No."));

        TxtCommitmentGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053660, recContratCadre_L."Contract Type" + Separator_L +
                                                        recContratCadre_L."Commitment Code");
        VerifGuidFinded(recContratCadre_L."Commitment Code", TxtCommitmentGuid_L, recContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Commitment Code"));

        TxtCropRotationGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053659, recContratCadre_L."Contact No." + Separator_L +
          recContratCadre_L."Crop Rotation Campaign No.");
        VerifGuidFinded(recContratCadre_L."Contact No." + Separator_L + recContratCadre_L."Crop Rotation Campaign No.",
          TxtCropRotationGuid_L, recContratCadre_L.FIELDCAPTION("Crop Rotation Campaign No."));

        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053656, Code_P);
        FieldName_L[1] := 'cmc_erpagreementnumber';
        FieldValue_L[1] := recContratCadre_L."Contract No.";
        Modify_L := FindExist(EntityName_L, FieldName_L, FieldValue_L, 1, TxtCrmGuid_L);
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053656, Code_P);
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
            IF cduManCon_G.ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
              Modify_L := TRUE;
            END;
          END;
        END;
      {
        IF Modify_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          AlreadyUpdated_L := TRUE;
          cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_accountid', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_commitment_id', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_elementcampaignid', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_erpagreementnumber', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_confirmationdate', '');
          // --> 20150715-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1943-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_signaturedate', '');
          //AFTER
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_datesignature', '');
          // <-- 20150715-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1943-FHS01
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_answer', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_paymentcompliance', '');
          IF cduManCon_G.GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT(cduManCon_G.CompareGuid(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_accountid'), TxtAccountGuid_L)) THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            IF NOT(cduManCon_G.CompareGuid(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid'),
               TxtProductionCampaignGuid_L))
            THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            IF NOT(cduManCon_G.CompareGuid(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_commitment_id'), TxtCommitmentGuid_L)) THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            IF NOT(cduManCon_G.CompareGuid(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_elementcampaignid'), TxtCropRotationGuid_L)) THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_erpagreementnumber') <> recContratCadre_L."Contract No." THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name') <> recContratCadre_L.Description THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_confirmationdate') <> cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(
               recContratCadre_L."Confirmation Date")
            THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            // --> 20150715-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1943-FHS01
            //BEFORE
            //IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_signaturedate') <> cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(
            //AFTER
            IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_datesignature') <> cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(
            // <-- 20150715-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1943-FHS01
               recContratCadre_L."Date Signed")
            THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
