            CASE recContratCadre_L.Answer OF
              recContratCadre_L.Answer::"To Revise": BEGIN
                IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_answer') <> '175490002' THEN
                  AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
              END;
              recContratCadre_L.Answer::Accepted: BEGIN
                IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_answer') <> '175490000' THEN
                  AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
              END;
              recContratCadre_L.Answer::Refused: BEGIN
                IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_answer') <> '175490001' THEN
                  AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
              END;
            END;

            CASE recContratCadre_L."Conformity For Payment" OF
              recContratCadre_L."Conformity For Payment"::Yes: BEGIN
                IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_answer') <> '175490001' THEN
                  AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
              END;
              recContratCadre_L."Conformity For Payment"::No: BEGIN
                IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_answer') <> '175490000' THEN
                  AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
              END;
              recContratCadre_L."Conformity For Payment"::"Waiting for explanation": BEGIN
                IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_answer') <> '175490002' THEN
                  AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
              END;
            END;
          END;
        END;

        IF AlreadyUpdated_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          EXIT;
        END;
      }
        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_accountid', TxtAccountGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid', TxtProductionCampaignGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_commitment_id', TxtCommitmentGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_elementcampaignid', TxtCropRotationGuid_L);

        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_erpagreementnumber', recContratCadre_L."Contract No.");
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recContratCadre_L.Description);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_confirmationdate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recContratCadre_L."Confirmation Date"));
        // --> 20150715-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1943-FHS01
        //BEFORE
        //cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_signaturedate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recContratCadre_L."Date Signed"));
        //AFTER
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_datesignature', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recContratCadre_L."Date Signed"));
        // <-- 20150715-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1943-FHS01

        CASE recContratCadre_L.Answer OF
          recContratCadre_L.Answer::"To Revise": BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_answer', '175490002');
          END;
          recContratCadre_L.Answer::Accepted: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_answer', '175490000');
          END;
          recContratCadre_L.Answer::Refused: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_answer', '175490001');
          END;
        END;

        CASE recContratCadre_L."Conformity For Payment" OF
          recContratCadre_L."Conformity For Payment"::Yes: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_paymentcompliance', '175490001');
          END;
          recContratCadre_L."Conformity For Payment"::No: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_paymentcompliance', '175490000');
          END;
          recContratCadre_L."Conformity For Payment"::"Waiting for explanation": BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_paymentcompliance', '175490002');
          END;
        END;

        recContratCadre_L."Last Synchro Date" := TODAY;
        recContratCadre_L.MODIFY;

        //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053656, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //Mise .  jour r'ussie
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053656, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Mise . jour 'chou'e
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END;

        // --> 20170414-WI00-ADV01.01.00-26681-BSI01
        {before
        //Mise . jour du statut
        CASE recContratCadre_L.Status OF
          recContratCadre_L.Status::Open: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1);
          END;
          recContratCadre_L.Status::Released: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 175490000);
          END;
        END;
        after}
        // <-- 20170414-WI00-ADV01.01.00-26681-BSI01

      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression de l'enregistrement
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053656, Code_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::collectmasteragreement, 2, 2);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncLigneContratCadreCollecte@8053737(Code_P@8053601 : Text[110];TypeAction_P@8053600 : Code[10]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      recLigneContrat_L@8053606 : Record 8053209;
      Doc1_L@8053610 : Text[30];
      LineNo_L@8053611 : Integer;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053612 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053613 : Boolean;
      Code_L@8053602 : Text[110];
      TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L@8053614 : Text[50];
      TxtCollectMasterAgrGuid_L@8053615 : Text[50];
      recGuid_L@8053616 : Record 8053609;
      Separator_L@8053604 : Text[1];
      recContratCadre_L@8053605 : Record 8053208;
      AlreadyUpdated_L@8053607 : Boolean;
      FieldName_L@8053609 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      FieldValue_L@8053608 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      recContact_L@8053000 : Record 5050;
      recContractCamp_L@8053001 : Record 8053324;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Importation de la ligne de contrat cadre collecte dans Nav depuis le CRM (CRM -> NAV)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_collectagreementline';
      Code_L := Code_P;
      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();
      Result_L := FALSE;

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        Doc1_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
        EVALUATE(LineNo_L, COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L)));
        IF NOT(recLigneContrat_L.GET(Doc1_L, LineNo_L)) THEN BEGIN
          Result_L := TRUE;
          EXIT(Result_L);
        END;

        IF recContratCadre_L.GET(Doc1_L) THEN BEGIN
          // C'est le statut de synchro qu'il faut v'rifier
          {
          IF recContratCadre_L.Status <> recContratCadre_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
            Result_L := TRUE;
            EXIT(Result_L);
          END;

          IF recContratCadre_L."Synchro Status" <> recContratCadre_L."Synchro Status"::"ERP validated" THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;
          IF recContractCamp_L.GET(recContratCadre_L."Campaign No.", recContratCadre_L."Contract Type") THEN BEGIN
            IF recContractCamp_L."Modify Ending Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
              IF NOT(WORKDATE > recContractCamp_L."Modify Ending Date") OR
                 (recContratCadre_L."Synchro Status" <> recContratCadre_L."Synchro Status"::"ERP validated")
              THEN BEGIN
                EXIT;
              END;
            END;
          END;
          }

          IF (recContratCadre_L."Synchro Status" <> recContratCadre_L."Synchro Status"::"ERP validated") OR
            (recLigneContrat_L."Last Synchro Date" > recContratCadre_L."Last Modification Date")
          THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;

          IF recContact_L.GET(recContratCadre_L."Contact No.") THEN BEGIN
            IF recContact_L."Contact CRM" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
              Result_L := TRUE;
              EXIT(Result_L);
            END;
          END;
        END;

        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053665, Code_P);
        Modify_L := FALSE;

        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        TxtCollectMasterAgrGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053656, recLigneContrat_L."Contract No.");
        VerifGuidFinded(recLigneContrat_L."Contract No.", TxtCollectMasterAgrGuid_L, recLigneContrat_L.FIELDCAPTION("Contract No."));

        TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053673, recLigneContrat_L."Item Funnel Code");
        VerifGuidFinded(recLigneContrat_L."Item Funnel Code", TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L, recLigneContrat_L.FIELDCAPTION("Item Funnel Code"))
      ;

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END;
        END;
      {
        IF Modify_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          AlreadyUpdated_L := TRUE;
          cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectmasteragreementid', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfilterid', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_surface', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_homeconsumptionsurface', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_rate', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_quantity', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_potential', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pfpotential', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_amendment', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_assignedquantity', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_plannedpfquantity', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_computedpfquantity', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_isfullrate', '');
          IF cduManCon_G.GetRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT(cduManCon_G.CompareGuid(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectmasteragreementid'),
              TxtCollectMasterAgrGuid_L))
            THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            IF NOT(cduManCon_G.CompareGuid(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfilterid'),
              TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L))
            THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name') <> recLigneContrat_L.Description THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_surface') <> cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recLigneContrat_L."Area (Ha)") THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_homeconsumptionsurface') <> cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(
              recLigneContrat_L."Autoconsumed Surface (Ha)")
            THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_rate') <> cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recLigneContrat_L."% Rate") THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_quantity') <> cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recLigneContrat_L."Quantity (T)")
            THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_potential') <> cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recLigneContrat_L.Potential) THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pfpotential') <> cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(
               recLigneContrat_L."Potential PF")
            THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_amendment') <> cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recLigneContrat_L.Amendment) THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            recLigneContrat_L.CALCFIELDS("Qty. Assigned (T)");
            IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_assignedquantity') <> cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(
               recLigneContrat_L."Qty. Assigned (T)")
            THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_plannedpfquantity') <> cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(
               recLigneContrat_L."Qty. PF (Expected)")
            THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_computedpfquantity') <> cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(
               recLigneContrat_L.UpdateQtyPF(recLigneContrat_L))
            THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
            IF cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_isfullrate') <> cduManCon_G.FormatBoolToCrm(
              recLigneContrat_L."% Rate" = 100)
            THEN
              AlreadyUpdated_L := FALSE;
          END;
        END;

        IF AlreadyUpdated_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          EXIT;
        END;
      }
        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectmasteragreementid', TxtCollectMasterAgrGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_funnelfilterid', TxtFunnelFilterGuid_L);

        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recLigneContrat_L.Description);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_surface', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recLigneContrat_L."Area (Ha)"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_homeconsumptionsurface', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(
          recLigneContrat_L."Autoconsumed Surface (Ha)"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_rate', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recLigneContrat_L."% Rate"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_quantity', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recLigneContrat_L."Quantity (T)"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_potential', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recLigneContrat_L.Potential));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pfpotential', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recLigneContrat_L."Potential PF"));
        recLigneContrat_L.CALCFIELDS("Qty. Assigned (T)", Amendment);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_amendment', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recLigneContrat_L.Amendment));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_assignedquantity', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recLigneContrat_L."Qty. Assigned (T)"));

        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_plannedpfquantity', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recLigneContrat_L."Qty. PF (Expected)"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_computedpfquantity', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recLigneContrat_L.UpdateQtyPF(
          recLigneContrat_L)));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_isfullrate', cduManCon_G.FormatBoolToCrm(recLigneContrat_L."% Rate" = 100));

        recLigneContrat_L."Last Synchro Date" := TODAY;
        recLigneContrat_L.MODIFY;

        //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053665, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
            //Mise .  jour r'ussie
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Mise . jour 'chou'e
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END;
        Result_L := TRUE;

      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression de l'enregistrement
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053665, Code_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::collectagreementline, 2, 2);
        Result_L := TRUE;
      END;
      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncDetailLigneContratCadreCol@8053738(Code_P@8053619 : Text[110];TypeAction_P@8053618 : Code[10]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      recDetailLigneContrat_L@8053606 : Record 8053210;
      Doc1_L@8053609 : Text[30];
      LineNo_L@8053608 : Integer;
      DetailLineNo_L@8053602 : Integer;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053611 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053610 : Boolean;
      Code_L@8053600 : Text[110];
      recGuid_L@8053601 : Record 8053609;
      TxtContractGuid_L@8053620 : Text[50];
      TxtContractLineGuid_L@8053621 : Text[50];
      TxtProductGuid_L@8053622 : Text[50];
      TxtSpeciesGuid_L@8053623 : Text[50];
      Separator_L@8053604 : Text[1];
      FieldName_L@8053607 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      FieldValue_L@8053605 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      recContract_L@1000000000 : Record 8053208;
      recRef_L@1000000001 : RecordRef;
      recContact_L@8053000 : Record 5050;
      recContractCamp_L@8053001 : Record 8053324;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Importation des d'tails de ligne de contrat cadre collecte dans Nav depuis le CRM (CRM -> NAV)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique si l'op'ration a pu ^tre r'alis'e
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_collectagreementlinedetail';
      Code_L := Code_P;
      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        Doc1_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
        Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
        EVALUATE(LineNo_L, COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1));
        EVALUATE(DetailLineNo_L, COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L)));
        IF NOT(recDetailLigneContrat_L.GET(Doc1_L, LineNo_L, DetailLineNo_L)) THEN BEGIN
          EXIT;
        END;

        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053658, Code_P);
        Modify_L := FALSE;

        IF recContract_L.GET(Doc1_L) THEN BEGIN
          {
          IF recContract_L.Status <> recContract_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;

          IF recContract_L."Synchro Status" <> recContract_L."Synchro Status"::"ERP validated" THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;
          IF recContractCamp_L.GET(recContract_L."Campaign No.", recContract_L."Contract Type") THEN BEGIN
            IF recContractCamp_L."Modify Ending Date" <> 0D THEN BEGIN
              IF NOT(WORKDATE > recContractCamp_L."Modify Ending Date") OR
                 (recContract_L."Synchro Status" <> recContract_L."Synchro Status"::"ERP validated")
              THEN BEGIN
                EXIT;
              END;
            END;
          END;
          }

          IF (recContract_L."Synchro Status" <> recContract_L."Synchro Status"::"ERP validated") OR
            (recDetailLigneContrat_L."Last Synchro Date" > recContract_L."Last Modification Date")
          THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;

          IF recContact_L.GET(recContract_L."Contact No.") THEN BEGIN
            IF recContact_L."Contact CRM" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
              EXIT;
            END;
          END;
        END;
        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        TxtContractGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053671, recDetailLigneContrat_L."Contract Type");
        IF TxtContractGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
          recRef_L.GETTABLE(recContract_L);
          recRef_L.SETPOSITION(recContract_L.GETPOSITION);
          cduManCon_G.SyncFromTrigger(recRef_L, recRef_L, TextModify);
          COMMIT;
        END;
        VerifGuidFinded(recDetailLigneContrat_L."Contract No.", TxtContractGuid_L, recDetailLigneContrat_L.FIELDCAPTION("Contract No."))
      ;

        TxtContractLineGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053665, recDetailLigneContrat_L."Contract No." +
          Separator_L + cduManCon_G.FormatIntToCrm(recDetailLigneContrat_L."Contract Line No."));
        IF TxtContractLineGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
          IF recContract_L.GET(Doc1_L) THEN BEGIN
            IF recContract_L.Status <> recContract_L.Status::Released THEN BEGIN
              EXIT;
            END;
            recRef_L.GETTABLE(recContract_L);
            recRef_L.SETPOSITION(recContract_L.GETPOSITION);
            cduManCon_G.SyncFromTrigger(recRef_L, recRef_L, TextModify);
            COMMIT;
          END;
        END;
        VerifGuidFinded(recDetailLigneContrat_L."Contract No." + Separator_L + cduManCon_G.FormatIntToCrm(
          recDetailLigneContrat_L."Contract Line No."), TxtContractLineGuid_L,
          recDetailLigneContrat_L.FIELDCAPTION("Contract Line No."));

        TxtProductGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, recDetailLigneContrat_L."Item No.");
        VerifGuidFinded(recDetailLigneContrat_L."Item No.", TxtProductGuid_L, recDetailLigneContrat_L.FIELDCAPTION("Item No."));

        TxtSpeciesGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053651, recDetailLigneContrat_L."Species Code");
        VerifGuidFinded(recDetailLigneContrat_L."Species Code", TxtSpeciesGuid_L, recDetailLigneContrat_L.FIELDCAPTION("Species Code"));

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.ExistRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            Modify_L := TRUE;
          END;
        END;

        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectmasteragreementtypeid', TxtContractGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectagreementlineid', TxtContractLineGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productid', TxtProductGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_speciesid', TxtSpeciesGuid_L);

        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_duedate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recDetailLigneContrat_L."Due Date"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recDetailLigneContrat_L.Description);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_quantity', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recDetailLigneContrat_L."Quantity (T)"));

        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_duedatepremiumcode',recDetailLigneContrat_L."Bonus Due Code");
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_collectcontractnumber', recDetailLigneContrat_L."Collection Contract No.");

        recDetailLigneContrat_L.CALCFIELDS("Quantity On Coll Contract");
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_assignedquantity',
          cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recDetailLigneContrat_L."Quantity On Coll Contract"));

        CASE recDetailLigneContrat_L.Parity OF
          recDetailLigneContrat_L.Parity::" ": BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_parity', '#NULL#');
          END;
          recDetailLigneContrat_L.Parity::Departure: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_parity', '175490000');
          END;
          recDetailLigneContrat_L.Parity::Made: BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_parity', '175490001');
          END;
        END;
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_unitprice', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recDetailLigneContrat_L."Unit Price"));

        recDetailLigneContrat_L."Last Synchro Date" := TODAY;
        recDetailLigneContrat_L.MODIFY;

        //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053658, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
            //Mise .  jour r'ussie
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Mise . jour 'chou'e
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END;
      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression de l'enregistrement
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053658, Code_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::collectagreementlinedetail, 2, 2);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncContratTPG@8053649(Code_P@8053601 : Text[110];TypeAction_P@8053600 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      recJrnlLine_L@8053602 : Record 8053607;
      LastGuid_L@8053603 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un contrat TPG (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      LastGuid_L := '';
      recJrnlLine_L.RESET;
      recJrnlLine_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJrnlLine_L.Type::TPGHeader);
      recJrnlLine_L.SETFILTER("Document No.", Code_P + '*');
      IF recJrnlLine_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(recJrnlLine_L.Entity);
        recJrnlLine_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE);
        REPEAT
          //envoie des donn'es de l'ent^te
          cduManCon_G.SetField(recJrnlLine_L.Entity, recJrnlLine_L.Field, recJrnlLine_L.Data);
        UNTIL recJrnlLine_L.NEXT = 0;
        //Mise . jour de l'ent^te
        IF NOT(cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(recJrnlLine_L.Entity, recJrnlLine_L.Code)) THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
        END;
        recJrnlLine_L.RESET;
        recJrnlLine_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJrnlLine_L.Type::TPGLine);
        recJrnlLine_L.SETFILTER("Document No.", Code_P + '*');
        IF recJrnlLine_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
          //Envoie des donn'es des lignes
          REPEAT
            //Recherche si ligne suivante
            IF LastGuid_L <> recJrnlLine_L.Code THEN BEGIN
              IF LastGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
                IF NOT(cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(recJrnlLine_L.Entity, LastGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
                  cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
                END;
              END;
              LastGuid_L := recJrnlLine_L.Code;
              cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(recJrnlLine_L.Entity);
            END;
            //Envoyer des donn'es de la ligne
            cduManCon_G.SetField(recJrnlLine_L.Entity, recJrnlLine_L.Field, recJrnlLine_L.Data);
          UNTIL recJrnlLine_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;
        //Mise . jour de la derniSre ligne si elle existe
        IF LastGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(recJrnlLine_L.Entity, LastGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
          END;
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncAllTarifTPG@8053644();
    VAR
      recSalesPrice_L@8053600 : Record 7002;
      recSetup_L@8053601 : Record 8053602;
      EntityName_L@8053602 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Ajoute le tarif TPG . la CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
        EntityName_L := 'cmc_tpgprice';
        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        cduManCon_G.DeleteMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);

        recSetup_L.GET();
        recSalesPrice_L.RESET;
        recSalesPrice_L.SETRANGE("Sales Type", recSalesPrice_L."Sales Type"::"Customer Price Group");
        recSalesPrice_L.SETRANGE("Sales Code", recSetup_L."Sales Code TPG");
        IF recSalesPrice_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            SyncTarifTPG(recSalesPrice_L);
          UNTIL recSalesPrice_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
      cduManCon_G.ClearConnector();
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncTarifTPG@8053643(recSalesPrice_P@8053600 : Record 7002);
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053601 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      txtItemGuid_L@8053603 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Ajoute le tarif TPG . la CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recSalesPrice_P : Prix de vente
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF recSalesPrice_P."Variant Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
        txtItemGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, recSalesPrice_P."Item No.");
      END ELSE BEGIN
        txtItemGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, recSalesPrice_P."Item No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() +
          recSalesPrice_P."Variant Code");
      END;

      IF cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recSalesPrice_P."Item No.", txtItemGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
        EntityName_L := 'cmc_tpgprice';
        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productid', txtItemGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recSalesPrice_P."Item Description" + recSalesPrice_P."Item Description 2");
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_price', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recSalesPrice_P."Unit Price"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_price_base', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recSalesPrice_P."Unit Price"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_month', FORMAT(recSalesPrice_P."Starting Date", 2, '<Month,2>'));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_year', FORMAT(recSalesPrice_P."Starting Date", 4, '<Year4>'));

        cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncAllSeason@8053646();
    VAR
      recSeason_L@8053600 : Record 8053154;
      recSetup_L@8053601 : Record 8053602;
      EntityName_L@8053602 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Ajoute la saison . la CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
        EntityName_L := 'cmc_season';
        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        cduManCon_G.DeleteMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);

        recSeason_L.RESET;
        // on ne synchronise que les saisons actives
        recSeason_L.SETRANGE(Unactive, FALSE);
        IF recSeason_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            SyncSeason(recSeason_L);
          UNTIL recSeason_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
      cduManCon_G.ClearConnector();
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncSeason@8053645(recSeason_P@8053600 : Record 8053154);
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053601 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      txtItemGuid_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      recVariant_L@8053000 : Record 5401;
      TxtCampaignGuid_L@8053001 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Ajoute la saison . la CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recSeason_P : Saison
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_season';
      cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      TxtCampaignGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053622, recSeason_P."Campaign No.");
      cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recSeason_P."Campaign No.", TxtCampaignGuid_L, recSeason_P.FIELDCAPTION("Campaign No."));
      CASE recSeason_P.Type OF
        recSeason_P.Type::Activity: BEGIN
          TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053648, recSeason_P."No.");
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recSeason_P."No.", TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490000');
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_activityid', TxtCrmGuid_L);
        END;
        recSeason_P.Type::Family: BEGIN
          TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053649, recSeason_P."No.");
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recSeason_P."No.", TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490001');
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_familyid', TxtCrmGuid_L);
        END;
        recSeason_P.Type::Subfamily: BEGIN
          TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053650, recSeason_P."No.");
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recSeason_P."No.", TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490002');
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_subfamilyid', TxtCrmGuid_L);
        END;
        recSeason_P.Type::Species: BEGIN
          TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053651, recSeason_P."No.");
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recSeason_P."No.", TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490003');
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_speciesid', TxtCrmGuid_L);
        END;
        recSeason_P.Type::Item: BEGIN
          IF recSeason_P."Variant Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
            txtItemGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, recSeason_P."No.");
          END ELSE BEGIN
            txtItemGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, recSeason_P."No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() +
               recSeason_P."Variant Code");
          END;
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490004');
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productid', txtItemGuid_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid', TxtCampaignGuid_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliverybegindate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Delivery Starting Date"))
      ;
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliveryenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Delivery Ending Date"));
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_duebegindate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Due Date Starting Date"));
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_dueenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Due Date Ending Date"));
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recSeason_P.Description);
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_orderbegindate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Order Starting Date"));
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_orderenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Order Ending Date"));
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pivotdate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Reference Date"));
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_id_no', cduManCon_G.FormatIntToCrm(recSeason_P."ID No."));

          cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recSeason_P."No.", txtItemGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
            recVariant_L.RESET;
            recVariant_L.SETRANGE("Item No.", recSeason_P."No.");
            IF recVariant_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
              REPEAT
                cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
                txtItemGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, recVariant_L."Item No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() +
                  recVariant_L.Code);
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490004');
                cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productid', txtItemGuid_L);
                cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid', TxtCampaignGuid_L);
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliverybegindate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(
                  recSeason_P."Delivery Starting Date"));
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliveryenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(
                  recSeason_P."Delivery Ending Date"));
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_duebegindate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Due Date Starting Date")
      );
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_dueenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Due Date Ending Date"));
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recSeason_P.Description);
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_orderbegindate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Order Starting Date"))
      ;
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_orderenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Order Ending Date"));
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pivotdate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Reference Date"));
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_id_no', cduManCon_G.FormatIntToCrm(recSeason_P."ID No."));
                cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
              UNTIL recVariant_L.NEXT = 0;
            EXIT;
          END;
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490004');
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productid', txtItemGuid_L);
        END;
      END;

      IF recSeason_P.Type <> recSeason_P.Type::Item THEN BEGIN
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid', TxtCampaignGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliverybegindate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Delivery Starting Date"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliveryenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Delivery Ending Date"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_duebegindate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Due Date Starting Date"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_dueenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Due Date Ending Date"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recSeason_P.Description);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_orderbegindate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Order Starting Date"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_orderenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Order Ending Date"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pivotdate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_P."Reference Date"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_id_no', cduManCon_G.FormatIntToCrm(recSeason_P."ID No."));

        cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncSeasonByID@8053003(Code_P@8053002 : Text[110]);
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053601 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      txtItemGuid_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      recVariant_L@8053000 : Record 5401;
      TxtCampaignGuid_L@8053001 : Text[50];
      recSeason_L@8053003 : Record 8053154;
      idSeason_L@8053004 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Ajoute la saison . la CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recSeason_P : Saison
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF Code_P = '' THEN BEGIN
        EntityName_L := 'cmc_season';
        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        cduManCon_G.DeleteMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);

        recSeason_L.RESET;
        // on ne synchronise que les saisons actives
        recSeason_L.SETRANGE(Unactive, FALSE);
        IF recSeason_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            SyncSeason(recSeason_L);
          UNTIL recSeason_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;
        EXIT;
      END;

      EntityName_L := 'cmc_season';
      cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
      cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, 'cmc_id_no', '=', Code_P);
      cduManCon_G.DeleteMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);

      IF NOT(EVALUATE(idSeason_L, Code_P)) THEN
        EXIT;

      IF NOT(recSeason_L.GET(idSeason_L)) THEN
        EXIT;

      IF recSeason_L.Unactive = TRUE THEN
        EXIT;

      cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

      TxtCampaignGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053622, recSeason_L."Campaign No.");
      cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recSeason_L."Campaign No.", TxtCampaignGuid_L, recSeason_L.FIELDCAPTION("Campaign No."));
      CASE recSeason_L.Type OF
        recSeason_L.Type::Activity: BEGIN
          TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053648, recSeason_L."No.");
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recSeason_L."No.", TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490000');
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_activityid', TxtCrmGuid_L);
        END;
        recSeason_L.Type::Family: BEGIN
          TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053649, recSeason_L."No.");
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recSeason_L."No.", TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490001');
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_familyid', TxtCrmGuid_L);
        END;
        recSeason_L.Type::Subfamily: BEGIN
          TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053650, recSeason_L."No.");
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recSeason_L."No.", TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490002');
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_subfamilyid', TxtCrmGuid_L);
        END;
        recSeason_L.Type::Species: BEGIN
          TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053651, recSeason_L."No.");
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recSeason_L."No.", TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490003');
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_speciesid', TxtCrmGuid_L);
        END;
        recSeason_L.Type::Item: BEGIN
          IF recSeason_L."Variant Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
            txtItemGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, recSeason_L."No.");
          END ELSE BEGIN
            txtItemGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, recSeason_L."No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() +
               recSeason_L."Variant Code");
          END;
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490004');
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productid', txtItemGuid_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid', TxtCampaignGuid_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliverybegindate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Delivery Starting Date"))
      ;
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliveryenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Delivery Ending Date"));
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_duebegindate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Due Date Starting Date"));
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_dueenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Due Date Ending Date"));
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recSeason_L.Description);
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_orderbegindate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Order Starting Date"));
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_orderenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Order Ending Date"));
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pivotdate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Reference Date"));
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_id_no', Code_P);

          cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recSeason_L."No.", txtItemGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
            recVariant_L.RESET;
            recVariant_L.SETRANGE("Item No.", recSeason_L."No.");
            IF recVariant_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
              REPEAT
                cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
                txtItemGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, recVariant_L."Item No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() +
                  recVariant_L.Code);
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type', '175490004');
                cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productid', txtItemGuid_L);
                cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid', TxtCampaignGuid_L);
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliverybegindate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(
                  recSeason_L."Delivery Starting Date"));
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliveryenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(
                  recSeason_L."Delivery Ending Date"));
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_duebegindate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Due Date Starting Date")
      );
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_dueenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Due Date Ending Date"));
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recSeason_L.Description);
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_orderbegindate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Order Starting Date"))
      ;
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_orderenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Order Ending Date"));
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pivotdate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Reference Date"));
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_id_no', Code_P);
                cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
              UNTIL recVariant_L.NEXT = 0;
            EXIT;
          END;
        END;
      END;

      IF recSeason_L.Type <> recSeason_L.Type::Item THEN BEGIN
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid', TxtCampaignGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliverybegindate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Delivery Starting Date"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_deliveryenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Delivery Ending Date"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_duebegindate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Due Date Starting Date"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_dueenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Due Date Ending Date"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recSeason_L.Description);
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_orderbegindate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Order Starting Date"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_orderenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Order Ending Date"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pivotdate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(recSeason_L."Reference Date"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_id_no', Code_P);

        cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncTypeContratParCampagne@8053650(Code_P@8053610 : Text[110];TypeAction_P@8053609 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053608 : Text[50];
      Code_L@8053607 : Text[110];
      EntityName_L@8053606 : Text[50];
      Doc1_L@8053605 : Text[30];
      Doc2_L@8053604 : Text[30];
      Doc3_L@8053002 : Text[30];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      Modify_L@8053601 : Boolean;
      recGuid_L@8053600 : Record 8053609;
      Separator_L@8053614 : Text[1];
      FieldName_L@8053616 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      FieldValue_L@8053615 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];
      recTypeContactByCamp_L@8053617 : Record 8053324;
      TxtTypeContractGuid_L@8053618 : Text[50];
      TxtCampGuid_L@8053619 : Text[50];
      recType_L@8053603 : Record 8053198;
      recTypeContactByCamp2_L@8053000 : Record 8053324;
      TxtCommitmentGuid_L@8053001 : Text[50];
      Size_L@1000000000 : Integer;
      TxtBuffer_L@1000000001 : Text[200];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un type de contract par campagne (T8053324) (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_agreementopeningperiod';
      Code_L := Code_P;
      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();

      Doc1_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
      Doc2_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
      // --> 20150421-CER16-CMC02.02.00-2934-FHS01
      //BEFORE
      //Doc2_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
      //AFTER
      Code_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
      Doc2_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, 1, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) - 1);
      Doc3_L := COPYSTR(Code_L, STRPOS(Code_L, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(Code_L));
      // <-- 20150421-CER16-CMC02.02.00-2934-FHS01

      IF (Doc1_L = '') OR (Doc2_L = '') THEN BEGIN
        EXIT;
      END;

      // --> 20150630-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1847-FHS01
      //BEFORE
      {
      // --> 20150421-CER16-CMC02.02.00-2934-FHS01
      //BEFORE
      //recTypeContactByCamp_L.GET(Doc1_L, Doc2_L);
      //AFTER
      recTypeContactByCamp_L.GetRec(Doc1_L, Doc2_L, Doc3_L);
      // <-- 20150421-CER16-CMC02.02.00-2934-FHS01
      recType_L.GET(recTypeContactByCamp_L."Frame Contract Type Code");
      }
      // <-- 20150630-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1847-FHS01

      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        // --> 20150630-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1847-FHS01
        IF recTypeContactByCamp_L.GetRec(Doc1_L, Doc2_L, Doc3_L) THEN BEGIN
          recType_L.GET(recTypeContactByCamp_L."Frame Contract Type Code");
        // <-- 20150630-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1847-FHS01
          TxtTypeContractGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053670, recTypeContactByCamp_L."Frame Contract Type Code");
          VerifGuidFinded(recTypeContactByCamp_L."Frame Contract Type Code", TxtTypeContractGuid_L,
            recTypeContactByCamp_L.FIELDCAPTION("Frame Contract Type Code"));

          TxtCampGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053622, recTypeContactByCamp_L."Campaign Code");
          VerifGuidFinded(recTypeContactByCamp_L."Campaign Code", TxtCampGuid_L, recTypeContactByCamp_L.FIELDCAPTION("Campaign Code"));

          // --> 20150421-CER16-CMC02.02.00-2934-FHS01
          IF recTypeContactByCamp_L."Commitment Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            TxtCommitmentGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053660, recTypeContactByCamp_L."Frame Contract Type Code" + Separator_L +
              recTypeContactByCamp_L."Commitment Code");
            VerifGuidFinded(recTypeContactByCamp_L."Frame Contract Type Code" + Separator_L + recTypeContactByCamp_L."Commitment Code",
              TxtCommitmentGuid_L, recTypeContactByCamp_L.FIELDCAPTION("Commitment Code"));
          END ELSE
            TxtCommitmentGuid_L := '#NULL#';
          // <-- 20150421-CER16-CMC02.02.00-2934-FHS01

          //recherche de la campagne
          IF recType_L.Type = recType_L.Type::CCC THEN BEGIN
            //Si type contrat CCC alors campagne N+1
            recTypeContactByCamp2_L.RESET;
            recTypeContactByCamp2_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Modify Ending Date", recTypeContactByCamp2_L."Frame Contract Type Code");
            recTypeContactByCamp2_L.SETFILTER("Modify Ending Date", '>%1', recTypeContactByCamp_L."Modify Ending Date");
            recTypeContactByCamp2_L.SETRANGE("Frame Contract Type Code", recTypeContactByCamp_L."Frame Contract Type Code");
            // --> 20150421-CER16-CMC02.02.00-2934-FHS01
            recTypeContactByCamp2_L.SETFILTER("Commitment Code", '%1|%2', recTypeContactByCamp_L."Commitment Code", '');
            // <-- 20150421-CER16-CMC02.02.00-2934-FHS01
            IF NOT recTypeContactByCamp2_L.FINDFIRST THEN
              //Il n'y a pas de campagne trouv'e avant celle-ci. Donc pas de synchro possible
              EXIT;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            // --> 20150421-CER16-CMC02.02.00-2934-FHS01
            //BEFORE
            //recTypeContactByCamp2_L.GET(recTypeContactByCamp_L."Frame Contract Type Code", recTypeContactByCamp_L."Campaign Code");
            //AFTER
            recTypeContactByCamp2_L.GetRec(recTypeContactByCamp_L."Frame Contract Type Code", recTypeContactByCamp_L."Campaign Code",
              recTypeContactByCamp_L."Commitment Code");
            // <-- 20150421-CER16-CMC02.02.00-2934-FHS01
          END;

          //R'cup'ration du GUID
          TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053689, Code_P);
          Modify_L := FALSE;

          FieldName_L[1] := 'cmc_masteragreementtypeid';
          FieldValue_L[1] := cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtTypeContractGuid_L);
          FieldName_L[2] := 'cmc_productioncampaignid';
          FieldValue_L[2] := cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCampGuid_L);
          // --> 20150421-CER16-CMC02.02.00-2934-FHS01
          //BEFORE
          //Modify_L := FindExist(EntityName_L, FieldName_L, FieldValue_L, 2, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          //AFTER
          FieldName_L[3] := 'cmc_commitmentid';
          FieldValue_L[3] := cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCommitmentGuid_L);
          Modify_L := FindExist(EntityName_L, FieldName_L, FieldValue_L, 3, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          // <-- 20150421-CER16-CMC02.02.00-2934-FHS01

          cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
          //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_masteragreementtypeid', TxtTypeContractGuid_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid', TxtCampGuid_L);
          // --> 20150421-CER16-CMC02.02.00-2934-FHS01
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_commitmentid', TxtCommitmentGuid_L);
          // <-- 20150421-CER16-CMC02.02.00-2934-FHS01
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_createstartdate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(
            recTypeContactByCamp2_L."Creating Starting Date"));
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_createenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(
            recTypeContactByCamp2_L."Creating Ending Date"));
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_updatestartdate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(
            recTypeContactByCamp2_L."Modify Starting Date"));
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_updateenddate', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(
            recTypeContactByCamp2_L."Modify Ending Date"));
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', recType_L.Description);

          //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
          IF Modify_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
            IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
              //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
              cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053689, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
            END ELSE BEGIN
              cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
              //Mise .  jour r'ussie
              //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
              cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053689, Code_P, TxtCrmGuid_L);
              cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
            END ELSE BEGIN
              //Mise . jour 'chou'e
              cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression de l'enregistrement
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid2(Text8053689, Code_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::ContractTypeByCampaign, 2, 2);
        // --> 20150630-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1847-FHS01
        //Recherche de tous les cas de suppression
        recGuid_L.RESET;
        recGuid_L.SETRANGE("Type Data", recGuid_L."Type Data"::ContractTypeByCampaign);
        recGuid_L.SETFILTER("Code Nav", Doc1_L + Separator_L + Doc2_L + Separator_L + '*');
        IF recGuid_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
          REPEAT
            IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
              Size_L := STRLEN(Doc1_L + Separator_L + Doc2_L + Separator_L);
              IF Size_L > STRLEN(recGuid_L."Code Nav") THEN
                TxtBuffer_L := COPYSTR(recGuid_L."Code Nav", Size_L + 1, STRLEN(recGuid_L."Code Nav"))
              ELSE
                TxtBuffer_L := '';
              IF NOT(recTypeContactByCamp_L.GET(Doc1_L, Doc2_L, TxtBuffer_L)) THEN
                cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav(EntityName_L, recGuid_L.GUID, recGuid_L."Type Data"::ContractTypeByCampaign, 2, 2);
            END;
          UNTIL recGuid_L.NEXT = 0;
        // <-- 20150630-GEN10-ADV01.01.00-1847-FHS01
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncCloseAllContract@8053647();
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053601 : Text[50];
      i_L@8053600 : Integer;
      Count_L@8053602 : Integer;
      recTypeContactByCamp_L@8053603 : Record 8053324;
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053604 : Text[50];
      Campaign_L@8053605 : Text[54];
      recContractType_L@8053607 : Record 8053198;
      MasterAgreementType_L@8053606 : Code[20];
      recCampaign_L@8053001 : Record 8052991;
      recCampaign2_L@8053000 : Record 8052991;
      LastCode_L@8053002 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Ferme toous les contrats ayant une date ne permettant plus leur modification dans la CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
        EntityName_L := 'cmc_supplyagreement';
        LastCode_L := '';
        REPEAT
          cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

          //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_masteragreementtypeid', '');

          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '!=', '175490002');
          IF LastCode_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '>', LastCode_L);
          END;
          cduManCon_G.SetOrderBy(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id');
          Count_L := cduManCon_G.SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
          IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
            i_L := 0;
            REPEAT
              i_L := i_L + 1;
              TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid'); //Campagne
              Campaign_L := cduManCon_G.GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053622, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);

              TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_masteragreementtypeid');
              MasterAgreementType_L := cduManCon_G.GetCodeFromGuid2(Text8053670, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
              TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id');

              IF (MasterAgreementType_L <> '') AND (Campaign_L <> '') THEN BEGIN
                IF recTypeContactByCamp_L.GET(MasterAgreementType_L, Campaign_L) THEN BEGIN
                  IF recTypeContactByCamp_L."Modify Ending Date" < WORKDATE THEN BEGIN
                    //R'cup'ration du GUID
                    cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 175490002); //D'sactiver
                  END;
                END;
              END;
              LastCode_L := TxtCrmGuid_L;
            UNTIL NOT(cduManCon_G.NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
          END;
        UNTIL Count_L = 0;

        recContractType_L.RESET;
        recContractType_L.SETRANGE(Type, recContractType_L.Type::CCC);
        IF recContractType_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          EntityName_L := 'cmc_collectmasteragreement';
          LastCode_L := '';
          REPEAT
            cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);

            //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid', '');

            cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '!=', '175490002');
            IF LastCode_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
              cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '>', LastCode_L);
            END;
            cduManCon_G.SetOrderBy(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id');
            Count_L := cduManCon_G.SelectMultipleRecords(EntityName_L);
            IF Count_L > 0 THEN BEGIN
              i_L := 0;
              REPEAT
                i_L := i_L + 1;
                TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaignid'); //Campagne
                Campaign_L := cduManCon_G.GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053622, TxtCrmGuid_L, FALSE);
                TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id');
                recCampaign_L.GET(Campaign_L);
                recCampaign2_L.RESET;
                recCampaign2_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Code", "Starting Date");
                recCampaign2_L.SETFILTER("Starting Date", '>%1', recCampaign_L."Ending Date");
                IF recCampaign2_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                  IF (MasterAgreementType_L <> '') AND (Campaign_L <> '') THEN BEGIN
                    IF recTypeContactByCamp_L.GET(recContractType_L.Code, recCampaign2_L."No.") THEN BEGIN
                      IF recTypeContactByCamp_L."Modify Ending Date" < WORKDATE THEN BEGIN
                        //R'cup'ration du GUID
                        cduManCon_G.SetState(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 175490002); //D'sactiver
                      END;
                    END;
                  END;
                END;
                LastCode_L := TxtCrmGuid_L;
              UNTIL NOT(cduManCon_G.NextEntity(EntityName_L, 1));
            END;
          UNTIL Count_L = 0;
        END;
      END;
      cduManCon_G.ClearConnector();
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllBalance@8053000();
    VAR
      recContact_L@8053000 : Record 5050;
      recCampaign_L@8053600 : Record 8052991;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Mise . jour de la balance des clients
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion() THEN BEGIN
        recCampaign_L.RESET;
        recCampaign_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Code", "Starting Date", "Ending Date");
        recCampaign_L.SETFILTER("Starting Date", '<%1', WORKDATE);
        recCampaign_L.SETFILTER("Ending Date", '>%1', WORKDATE);
        IF recCampaign_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        END;

        recContact_L.RESET;
        recContact_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Contact CRM");
        recContact_L.SETRANGE("Contact CRM", TRUE);
        IF recContact_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            UpdateBalance(recContact_L, recCampaign_L."No.");
          UNTIL recContact_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
      cduManCon_G.ClearConnector();
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateBalance@8053001(recContact_P@8053000 : Record 5050;Campaign_P@8053600 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053001 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053002 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Mise . jour de la balance d'un client
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recContact_P : Journal . filtrer
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'account';

      //R'cup'ration du GUID
      TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053626, recContact_P."No.");
      IF TxtCrmGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
        IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(recContact_P."Dynamics CRM Id")) THEN
          TxtCrmGuid_L := recContact_P."Dynamics CRM Id";
      END;

      IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
        recContact_P.CALCFIELDS("Balance (LCY)"); //Balance (LCY)
        recContact_P.SETFILTER("Date Filter", '..%1', WORKDATE);
        recContact_P.CALCFIELDS("Balance Due (LCY)"); //Balance (LCY)
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_balancing', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recContact_P."Balance (LCY)"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_amoutoutstandingbalancetodate',
          cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recContact_P."Balance Due (LCY)"));
        cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_young', cduManCon_G.FormatBoolToCrm(recContact_P.IsYoungFarmerCtt(Campaign_P)));
        cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
      END;
    END;

        28: recJournal_P.SETRANGE(Type, recJournal_P.Type::EngContratCadreCollecte);
        29: recJournal_P.SETRANGE(Type, recJournal_P.Type::ElementCampaign);

      //  31: recJournal_P.SETRANGE(Type, recJournal_P.Type::ContratCadreCollect);

        33: recJournal_P.SETRANGE(Type, recJournal_P.Type::TPGHeader);

        // <-- 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2862-FHS01
            // --> 20170407-WI00-TER01.01.00-3254-BSI01
        36 : recJournal_P.SETRANGE(Type, recJournal_P.Type::Contact);

    PROCEDURE VerifGuidFinded@8053639(Code_P@8053600 : Text[110];Guid_P@8053601 : Text[50];Caption_P@8053602 : Text[250]);
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // V'rifie si le GUID ou le code ont 't' trouv' et lance l'erreur correspondante
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code
      //    - Guid_P : GUID
      //    - Caption_P : Caption du champ
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(Code_P, Guid_P)) THEN BEGIN
        cduManCon_G.ClearConnector();
        ERROR(Text8053680, Caption_P, Code_P);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SearchUnactiveProduct@1000000001(Item_P@1000000000 : Code[31]) Result_L : Text[50];
    VAR
      EntityName_L@1000000001 : Text[50];
    BEGIN

    END;

    PROCEDURE ReplaceString@8053640(String@8053600 : Text[250];FindWhat@8053601 : Text[250];ReplaceWith@8053602 : Text[250]) NewString : Text[250];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de remplacer une chaime par une autre ATTENTION, il ne faut pas que la chaine a remplac' soit dans la chaine remplaÎante
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - String : chaine initiale
      //    - FindWhat : Chaine . remplacer
      //    - ReplaceWith : Chaine remplaÎante
      //  Sorties
      //    - NewString : chaine r'sultat
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      WHILE STRPOS(String,FindWhat) > 0 DO
        String := DELSTR(String,STRPOS(String,FindWhat)) + ReplaceWith + COPYSTR(String,STRPOS(String,FindWhat) + STRLEN(FindWhat));
      NewString := String;
    END;

    PROCEDURE FindExist@8053642(Entity_P@8053604 : Text[50];FieldName_P@8053603 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];FieldValue_P@8053602 : ARRAY [8] OF Text[250];NbField_P@8053601 : Integer;VAR GuidFinded_Var_P@8053600 : Text[54]) Result_L : Boolean;
    VAR
      i_L@8053605 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de trouv' un GUID existant dans la CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Entity_P : Nom de l'entit'
      //    - FieldName_P : Nom du champ (tableau dim8)
      //    - FieldValue_P : Valeur du champ . rechercher (tableau dim8)
      //    - NbField_P : Nombre de champs dans le tableau
      //    - GuidFinded_Var_P : GUID trouv'
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : Trouv' ou non
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := FALSE;

      cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(Entity_P);
      IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(GuidFinded_Var_P)) THEN BEGIN
        IF cduManCon_G.ExistRecord(Entity_P, GuidFinded_Var_P) THEN BEGIN
          Result_L := TRUE;
        END;
      END;

      IF Result_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        IF NbField_P = 0 THEN BEGIN
          EXIT(Result_L);
        END;

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(Entity_P);

        cduManCon_G.SetField(Entity_P, Entity_P + 'id', '');

        i_L := 0;
        REPEAT
          i_L += 1;
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity(Entity_P, FieldName_P[i_L], '=', FieldValue_P[i_L]);
        UNTIL i_L >= NbField_P;

        IF cduManCon_G.SelectMultipleRecords(Entity_P) > 0 THEN BEGIN
          GuidFinded_Var_P := cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute(Entity_P, Entity_P + 'id'));
          Result_L := TRUE;
        END;
      END;

      cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(Entity_P);

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncSalesLineHistory@1000000000(recSalesLine_P@1000000000 : Record 37);
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      TxtGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      recContactRelation_L@8053600 : Record 5054;
      Amount_L@8053605 : Decimal;
      recContact_L@8053608 : Record 5050;
      recItem_L@8053610 : Record 27;
      recVariant_L@8053609 : Record 5401;
      RecSalesHead_L@1000000001 : Record 36;
      Code_L@1000000002 : Code[50];
      RecGUID2_L@1000000003 : Record 8053609;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20160603-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2786-BSI01
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'une ligne d'historique de ventes et achats (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recEntry_P : 'criture valeur . envoyer
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'cmc_saleshistory';

      RecSalesHead_L.GET(recSalesLine_P."Document Type",recSalesLine_P."Document No.");
      Code_L := '';

      //recherche du compte
      recContactRelation_L.RESET;

      recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("No.", recSalesLine_P."Sell-to Customer No.");//recEntry_P."Source No.");
      recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Customer);

      IF recContactRelation_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        recContact_L.GET(recContactRelation_L."Contact No.");
        IF recContact_L."Contact CRM" = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053626, recContact_L."No.");
          IF cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recContact_L."No.", TxtGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.ResetAuto(EntityName_L);
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_account', TxtGuid_L);
            //recherche du produit
            IF recSalesLine_P."Variant Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
              TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, recSalesLine_P."No.");
              IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recSalesLine_P."No.", TxtGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
                TxtGuid_L := SearchUnactiveProduct(recSalesLine_P."No.");
                cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recSalesLine_P."No.", TxtGuid_L, recSalesLine_P.FIELDCAPTION("No."));
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053627, recSalesLine_P."No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() +
                recSalesLine_P."Variant Code");
              IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidFindedWithoutError(recSalesLine_P."Variant Code", TxtGuid_L)) THEN BEGIN
                TxtGuid_L := SearchUnactiveProduct(recSalesLine_P."No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() +
                                                   recSalesLine_P."Variant Code");

                cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recSalesLine_P."No." + cduManCon_G.GetItemSeparator() +
                                       recSalesLine_P."Variant Code", TxtGuid_L,recSalesLine_P.FIELDCAPTION("Variant Code"));
              END;
            END;
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_product', TxtGuid_L);
            IF recSalesLine_P."Variant Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
              IF recItem_L.GET(recSalesLine_P."No.") THEN
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name', COPYSTR(recItem_L.Description + recItem_L."Description 2", 1, 250));
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF recVariant_L.GET(recSalesLine_P."No.", recSalesLine_P."Variant Code") THEN
                cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_name',  COPYSTR(recVariant_L.Description + recVariant_L."Description 2",
                1, 250));
            END;

            //recherche de l'activit'
            TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053648, recSalesLine_P."Activity Code");
            cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recSalesLine_P."Activity Code", TxtGuid_L, recSalesLine_P.FIELDCAPTION("Activity Code"));
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_activity', TxtGuid_L);

            //recherche de la famille
            TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053649, recSalesLine_P."Family Code");
            cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recSalesLine_P."Family Code", TxtGuid_L, recSalesLine_P.FIELDCAPTION("Family Code"));
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_family', TxtGuid_L);

            //recherche de la campagne
            TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053622, recSalesLine_P."CMC Campaign No.");
            cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recSalesLine_P."CMC Campaign No.", TxtGuid_L, recSalesLine_P.FIELDCAPTION("CMC Campaign No."));
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_productioncampaign', TxtGuid_L);

            //recherche de la sous famille
            TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053650, recSalesLine_P."Sub-Family Code");
