            cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recSalesLine_P."Sub-Family Code", TxtGuid_L, recSalesLine_P.FIELDCAPTION("Sub-Family Code"));
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_subfamily', TxtGuid_L);

            //recherche de l'espSce
            TxtGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053651, recSalesLine_P."Species Code");
            cduManCon_G.GuidFinded(recSalesLine_P."Species Code", TxtGuid_L, recSalesLine_P.FIELDCAPTION("Species Code"));
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid(EntityName_L, 'cmc_species', TxtGuid_L);

            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_date', cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(RecSalesHead_L."Posting Date"));
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_qutity', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recSalesLine_P.Quantity));

            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_qtytoship',
                                             cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recSalesLine_P.Quantity - recSalesLine_P."Quantity Shipped"));
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_qtyshippednotinvoiced',
                                             cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recSalesLine_P."Qty. Shipped Not Invoiced"));

            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_amount', cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recSalesLine_P.Amount));
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type_ecriture', 'ELV'); //Ecritures lignes vente

            // --> 20170720-WI00-ADV01.01.00-25019-BSI01
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_ncommandevente',recSalesLine_P."Document No.");
            cduManCon_G.SetField(EntityName_L, 'cmc_nlignecommande',cduManCon_G.FormatIntToCrm(recSalesLine_P."Line No."));
            // <-- 20170720-WI00-ADV01.01.00-25019-BSI01

            Code_L := recSalesLine_P."Document No." + cduManCon_G.GetSeparator + FORMAT(recSalesLine_P."Line No.");

            CLEAR(RecGUID2_L);
            IF NOT RecGUID2_L.GET(RecGUID2_L."Type Data"::HistorySalesLine,Code_L) THEN BEGIN
              IF (cduManCon_G.CreateRecord(EntityName_L, TxtGuid_L)) THEN
                cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid2(Text8053699, Code_L, TxtGuid_L)
              ELSE
                cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF RecGUID2_L."Sale Line Modified" THEN
                 IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord(EntityName_L, RecGUID2_L.GUID) THEN BEGIN
                  RecGUID2_L."Sale Line Modified" := FALSE;
                  RecGUID2_L.MODIFY;
                  // --> 20161013-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22362-BSI01
                  COMMIT;
                  // <-- 20161013-WI00-ADV01.01.00-22362-BSI01
                 END;
            END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
      // <-- 20160603-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2786-BSI01
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateSalesLinesHistory@1000000002(RecSalesLine_P@1000000000 : Record 37);
    VAR
      RecGuid2_L@1000000001 : Record 8053609;
      Code_L@1000000002 : Code[50];
    BEGIN
      // --> 20160603-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2786-BSI01
      IF cduManCon_G.InterfaceIsActiveOnCompany(COMPANYNAME) THEN BEGIN
        Code_L := '';
        Code_L := RecSalesLine_P."Document No." + cduManCon_G.GetSeparator + FORMAT(RecSalesLine_P."Line No.");
        IF RecGuid2_L.GET(RecGuid2_L."Type Data"::HistorySalesLine,Code_L)
        // --> 20161130-WI00-ADV01.01.00-23009-BSI01
        AND (NOT RecGuid2_L."Sale Line Modified") THEN BEGIN
        // <-- 20161130-WI00-ADV01.01.00-23009-BSI01
          RecGuid2_L."Sale Line Modified" := TRUE;
          RecGuid2_L.MODIFY;
        END;
      END;
      // <-- 20160603-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2786-BSI01
    END;

    PROCEDURE SynchDeletedSalesOrderLines@60010();
    VAR
      RecCRMGUID_L@60000 : Record 8053609;
      RecSalesLine_L@60001 : Record 37;
      DocumentNo_L@60002 : Code[20];
      LineNo_L@60003 : Integer;
      Separator_L@1000000000 : Text[1];
    BEGIN
      // --> 20170612-WI00-ADV01.01.00-25019-BSI01
      Separator_L := '';
      DocumentNo_L := '';
      LineNo_L := 0;
      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator;
      CLEAR(RecCRMGUID_L);
      RecCRMGUID_L.SETRANGE("Type Data",RecCRMGUID_L."Type Data"::HistorySalesLine);
      RecCRMGUID_L.SETRANGE("Sale Line Modified",TRUE);
      IF RecCRMGUID_L.FINDSET THEN
      REPEAT
        DocumentNo_L := COPYSTR(RecCRMGUID_L."Code Nav", 1, STRPOS(RecCRMGUID_L."Code Nav", Separator_L) - 1);
        EVALUATE(LineNo_L,COPYSTR(RecCRMGUID_L."Code Nav", STRPOS(RecCRMGUID_L."Code Nav", Separator_L) + 1,
                            MAXSTRLEN(RecCRMGUID_L."Code Nav")));

        IF (DocumentNo_L = '') OR (LineNo_L = 0) THEN
          EXIT;

        IF NOT RecSalesLine_L.GET(RecSalesLine_L."Document Type"::Order,DocumentNo_L,LineNo_L) THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav('cmc_saleshistory', RecCRMGUID_L.GUID, RecCRMGUID_L."Type Data"::HistorySalesLine, 2, 1);
            cduManCon_G.ClearConnector;
          END;
          RecCRMGUID_L.DELETE;
        END;
        COMMIT;
      UNTIL RecCRMGUID_L.NEXT = 0;
      // <-- 20170612-WI00-ADV01.01.00-25019-BSI01
    END;

    PROCEDURE "<--Fonction Dynamics365-->"@60000();
    BEGIN
    END;

    PROCEDURE RunPendingEvent_D365@60005();
    VAR
      recJournal_L@8053600 : Record 8053606;
      recJournal2_L@1000000000 : Record 8053606;
      recJournalUpdate_L@8053601 : Record 8053606;
      recCrmOrganisation_L@8053602 : Record 8053075;
      cduSynchroError_L@8053603 : Codeunit 8053605;
      recJournalSearch_L@8053604 : Record 8053606;
      i_L@8053605 : Integer;
      recJournalLine_L@8053606 : Record 8053607;
      recAutomation_L@8053607 : Automation "{18033653-DC3D-4EE4-A298-FD5AF3571A9A} 1.0:{E3A42FF0-3633-4846-9C11-20D1AB63FFC9}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class";
      recSetup_L@8053000 : Record 8053602;
      Skip_L@8053001 : Boolean;
      recAutomation2_L@8053002 : Automation "{18033653-DC3D-4EE4-A298-FD5AF3571A9A} 1.0:{E3A42FF0-3633-4846-9C11-20D1AB63FFC9}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class";
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Traite toutes les demandes qui ne sont pas encore trait'es
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      CodeCompany_G := cduManCon_G.GetCompanyMcCode();

      //Recherche du code de la soci't' actuelle
      recCrmOrganisation_L.RESET;
      recCrmOrganisation_L.SETRANGE("Company Name", COMPANYNAME);
      recCrmOrganisation_L.FINDFIRST;

      i_L := 0;

      //Parcours de toutes les lignes du journal n'ayant pas fini d'^tre trait'es
      recJournal2_L.RESET;
      recJournal2_L.SETCURRENTKEY(Closed, Type);
      recJournal2_L.SETRANGE(Closed, FALSE);
      IF NOT recJournal2_L.ISEMPTY THEN
        IF cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion_D365 AND cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion2_D365 THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.GetAutomation_D365(recAutomation_L);
          cduManCon_G.GetAutomation2_D365(recAutomation2_L);
          //REPEAT
            //i_L += 1;
              //recJournal_L.SETRANGE(Type,recJournal_L.Type::Contact);  //to delete d365
              //SetFilterOnJournalByType(recJournal_L, i_L);
              CLEAR(recJournal_L);
              recJournal_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Priority Order",Closed);
              recJournal_L.SETRANGE(Closed,FALSE);
              IF recJournal_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
                REPEAT
                  recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L.Type, recJournal_L."Document No.");
                  //Lancement de l'ordre (RunEvent via codeunit externe)
                  CLEAR(cduSynchroError_L);
                  cduSynchroError_L.SetCrmConnexion_D365(recAutomation_L, recAutomation2_L);
                  IF NOT(cduSynchroError_L.RUN(recJournalUpdate_L)) THEN BEGIN
                    recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L.Type, recJournal_L."Document No.");
                    recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text"));
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L.Type, recJournal_L."Document No.");
                    recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" := '';
                  END;

                  IF recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" <> Text8053698 THEN BEGIN
                    //Mise . jour du journal
                    IF recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" = '' THEN BEGIN
                      recJournalUpdate_L.Closed := TRUE;
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      IF STRPOS(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text", Text8053640) = 1 THEN BEGIN
                        recJournalUpdate_L.Closed := TRUE;
                        recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" := COPYSTR(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text", STRLEN(Text8053640) + 1,
                          STRLEN(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text"));
                      END ELSE BEGIN
                        recJournalUpdate_L.Closed := FALSE;
                      END;
                    END;
                    recJournalUpdate_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
                  END;
                  COMMIT;
                UNTIL recJournal_L.NEXT = 0;
              END;
          //UNTIL i_L = 1;//37;
        END;

      cduManCon_G.ClearConnector();

      PurgeJournal();
    END;

    PROCEDURE RunPendingEventWithFilter_D365@60004(VAR recJournalExport_P@8053606 : Record 8053606);
    VAR
      recJournal_L@8053600 : Record 8053606;
      recJournalUpdate_L@8053601 : Record 8053606;
      recCrmOrganisation_L@8053602 : Record 8053075;
      cduSynchroError_L@8053603 : Codeunit 8053605;
      recJournalSearch_L@8053604 : Record 8053606;
      i_L@8053605 : Integer;
      Count_L@8053608 : Integer;
      ValBuffer_L@8053607 : Integer;
      j_L@8053609 : Integer;
      recAutomation_L@8053610 : Automation "{18033653-DC3D-4EE4-A298-FD5AF3571A9A} 1.0:{E3A42FF0-3633-4846-9C11-20D1AB63FFC9}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class";
      recSetup_L@8053001 : Record 8053602;
      Skip_L@8053000 : Boolean;
      recAutomation2_L@8053002 : Automation "{18033653-DC3D-4EE4-A298-FD5AF3571A9A} 1.0:{E3A42FF0-3633-4846-9C11-20D1AB63FFC9}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class";
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Traite toutes les demandes qui ne sont pas encore trait'es
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      CodeCompany_G := cduManCon_G.GetCompanyMcCode();

      //Recherche du code de la soci't' actuelle
      recCrmOrganisation_L.RESET;
      recCrmOrganisation_L.SETRANGE("Company Name", COMPANYNAME);
      recCrmOrganisation_L.FINDFIRST;

      i_L := 0;

      cduManCon_G.OpenWindow();

      //Parcours de toutes les lignes du journal n'ayant pas fini d'^tre trait'es
      recJournal_L.RESET;
      recJournal_L.SETCURRENTKEY(Closed, Type);
      recJournal_L.COPYFILTERS(recJournalExport_P);
      recJournal_L.SETRANGE(Closed, FALSE);
      IF recJournal_L.ISEMPTY = FALSE THEN
        IF cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion_D365 AND cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion2_D365 THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.GetAutomation_D365(recAutomation_L);
          cduManCon_G.GetAutomation2_D365(recAutomation2_L);
          REPEAT
            i_L += 1;
            j_L := 0;
            cduManCon_G.NewStepWindow(i_L);
            SetFilterOnJournalByType(recJournal_L, i_L);
            Count_L := recJournal_L.COUNT;
            IF recJournal_L.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
              REPEAT
                j_L += 1;
                ValBuffer_L := ROUND(j_L/Count_L * 9999, 1, '=');
                cduManCon_G.UpdateWindow(2, ValBuffer_L);
                recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L.Type, recJournal_L."Document No.");
                //Lancement de l'ordre (RunEvent via codeunit externe)
                CLEAR(cduSynchroError_L);
                cduSynchroError_L.SetCrmConnexion_D365(recAutomation_L, recAutomation2_L);
                IF NOT(cduSynchroError_L.RUN(recJournalUpdate_L)) THEN BEGIN
                  recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L.Type, recJournal_L."Document No.");
                  recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text"));
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L.Type, recJournal_L."Document No.");
                  recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" := '';
                END;

                IF recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" <> Text8053698 THEN BEGIN
                  //Mise . jour du journal
                  IF recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" = '' THEN BEGIN
                    recJournalUpdate_L.Closed := TRUE;
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    IF STRPOS(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text", Text8053640) = 1 THEN BEGIN
                      recJournalUpdate_L.Closed := TRUE;
                      recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" := COPYSTR(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text", STRLEN(Text8053640) + 1,
                        STRLEN(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text"));
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      recJournalUpdate_L.Closed := FALSE;
                    END;
                  END;
                  recJournalUpdate_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
                END;
                COMMIT;
              UNTIL recJournal_L.NEXT = 0;
            END;
          UNTIL i_L = 37;
        END;

      cduManCon_G.ClearConnector_D365;
      PurgeJournal;
      cduManCon_G.CloseWindow;
    END;

    PROCEDURE RunPendingCCCEvent_D365@60003();
    VAR
      recJournal_L@8053600 : Record 8053606;
      recJournalUpdate_L@8053601 : Record 8053606;
      recCrmOrganisation_L@8053602 : Record 8053075;
      cduSynchroError_L@8053603 : Codeunit 8053605;
      recJournalSearch_L@8053604 : Record 8053606;
      i_L@8053605 : Integer;
      recJournalLine_L@8053606 : Record 8053607;
      recAutomation_L@8053607 : Automation "{18033653-DC3D-4EE4-A298-FD5AF3571A9A} 1.0:{E3A42FF0-3633-4846-9C11-20D1AB63FFC9}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class";
      recAutomation2_L@8053000 : Automation "{18033653-DC3D-4EE4-A298-FD5AF3571A9A} 1.0:{E3A42FF0-3633-4846-9C11-20D1AB63FFC9}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class";
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Traite toutes les demandes qui ne sont pas encore trait'es
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      CodeCompany_G := cduManCon_G.GetCompanyMcCode();

      //Recherche du code de la soci't' actuelle
      recCrmOrganisation_L.RESET;
      recCrmOrganisation_L.SETRANGE("Company Name", COMPANYNAME);
      recCrmOrganisation_L.FINDFIRST;

      i_L := 0;

      //Parcours de toutes les lignes du journal n'ayant pas fini d'^tre trait'es
      recJournal_L.RESET;
      recJournal_L.SETCURRENTKEY(Closed, Type);
      recJournal_L.SETRANGE(Closed, FALSE);
      recJournal_L.SETFILTER(Type, '%1|%2|%3', recJournal_L.Type::ContratCadreCollect, recJournal_L.Type::LigneContratCadreCollect,
        recJournal_L.Type::DetailLigneContratCadreCollect);
      IF recJournal_L.ISEMPTY = FALSE THEN
        IF cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion() AND cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion2() THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.GetAutomation(recAutomation_L);
          cduManCon_G.GetAutomation2(recAutomation2_L);
          REPEAT
            i_L += 1;
            CASE i_L OF
              1: recJournal_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournal_L.Type::ContratCadreCollect);
              2: recJournal_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournal_L.Type::LigneContratCadreCollect);
              3: recJournal_L.SETRANGE(Type, recJournal_L.Type::DetailLigneContratCadreCollect);
            END;
            IF recJournal_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
              REPEAT
                recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L.Type, recJournal_L."Document No.");

                //Lancement de l'ordre (RunEvent via codeunit externe)
                CLEAR(cduSynchroError_L);
                cduSynchroError_L.SetCrmConnexion(recAutomation_L, recAutomation2_L);
                IF NOT(cduSynchroError_L.RUN(recJournalUpdate_L)) THEN BEGIN
                  recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L.Type, recJournal_L."Document No.");
                  recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text"));
                END ELSE BEGIN
                  recJournalUpdate_L.GET(recJournal_L.Type, recJournal_L."Document No.");
                  recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" := '';
                END;

                IF recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" <> Text8053698 THEN BEGIN
                  //Mise . jour du journal
                  IF recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" = '' THEN BEGIN
                    recJournalUpdate_L.Closed := TRUE;
                  END ELSE BEGIN
                    IF STRPOS(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text", Text8053640) = 1 THEN BEGIN
                      recJournalUpdate_L.Closed := TRUE;
                      recJournalUpdate_L."Error text" := COPYSTR(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text", STRLEN(Text8053640) + 1,
                        STRLEN(recJournalUpdate_L."Error text"));
                    END ELSE BEGIN
                      recJournalUpdate_L.Closed := FALSE;
                    END;
                  END;
                  recJournalUpdate_L.MODIFY(TRUE);
                END;
                COMMIT;
              UNTIL recJournal_L.NEXT = 0;
            END;
          UNTIL i_L = 3;
        END;

      cduManCon_G.ClearConnector();

      PurgeJournal();
    END;

    PROCEDURE RunEvent_D365@60008(recJournal_P@8053601 : Record 8053606) Result_L : Text[250];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Gestion des demandes reÎues du CRM
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recJournal_P : enregistrement du journal . traiter
      //  Sorties
      //    - Result_L : indique la r'ussite de l'op'ration
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Result_L := Text8053616;
      CASE recJournal_P.Type OF
        recJournal_P.Type::Account : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncAccount_D365(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;

        //****D365
        recJournal_P.Type::Contact : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncContact_D365(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;

        recJournal_P.Type::"Ship-to Address" : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncAddress_D365(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;

        recJournal_P.Type::City : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          cduManCon_G.SyncVille(recJournal_P."Document No.");
        END;

        {
        recJournal_P.Type::Campaign : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          cduManCon_G.SyncEntity('Campaign');
        END;

        recJournal_P.Type::Order : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncOrderHeader(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::Item : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncItem(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::"Unloading Place" : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncUnloadingPlace(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::"Unloading Method" : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncUnloadingMethod(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::"Unloading Size" : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncUnloadingSize(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::"Unloading Time" : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncUnloadingTime(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::Activity : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncActivity(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::Family : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncFamily(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::SubFamily : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncSubFamily(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::Specie : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncSpecies(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::Quota : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          // --> 20151013-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01
          //SyncQuota(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
          // <-- 20151013-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2211-BSI01
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::"Transcript." : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncTranscription(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::"ArticleTranscript." : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncArticleTranscription(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::ElementCampaign : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncElementCampaign(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::DetailsElementCampaign : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncDetailsElementCampaign(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::TypeContratCadre : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncTypeContratCadre(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::ModeleContratCadreAppro : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncModeleContratCadreAppro(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::Abaque : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncAbaque(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::CoeffAbaque : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncCoeffAbaque(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::TypeEngagement : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncTypeEngagement(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::Engagement : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncEngagement(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::EntonnoirArticle : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncEntonnoirArticle(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::TypeContratCadreCollecte : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncTypeContratCadreCollecte(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::EntonnoirContratCollecte : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncEntonnoirContratCollecte(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::EngContratCadreCollecte : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncEngContratCadreCollecte(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::ContratCadreCollect : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncContratCadreCollecte(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::LigneContratCadreCollect : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncLigneContratCadreCollecte(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::DetailLigneContratCadreCollect : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncDetailLigneContratCadreCol(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::Season : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncSeasonByID(recJournal_P."Document No.");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::TPG : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncAllTarifTPG();
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::TPGHeader : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncContratTPG(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::ContractTypeCamp : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncTypeContratParCampagne(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        recJournal_P.Type::History : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncHistoryFromAccount(recJournal_P."Document No.");
        END;
        // --> 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2862-FHS01
        recJournal_P.Type::NbPf : BEGIN
          Result_L := '';
          SyncUpdateNbContratPF(recJournal_P."Document No.", recJournal_P."Type Action");
        END;
        // <-- 20150327-GEN10-CMC02.02.00-2862-FHS01
        }
        ELSE BEGIN
          ERROR(Text8053698);
        END;
      END;

      EXIT(Result_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncHistory_D365@60002();
    VAR
      recEntry_L@8053600 : Record 5802;
      recConnectorSetup_L@8053601 : Record 8053602;
      cduCatchError_L@8053602 : Codeunit 8053608;
      recAutomation_L@8053000 : Automation "{18033653-DC3D-4EE4-A298-FD5AF3571A9A} 1.0:{E3A42FF0-3633-4846-9C11-20D1AB63FFC9}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class";
      RecSalesLine_L@1000000000 : Record 37;
      RecGUID2_L@1000000001 : Record 8053609;
      Code_L@1000000002 : Code[50];
      Separator_L@1000000003 : Code[10];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation des historiques de ventes et achats (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      recConnectorSetup_L.GET();

      IF NOT(cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion()) THEN BEGIN
        cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
      END;
      cduManCon_G.GetAutomation(recAutomation_L);

      //Recherche s'il y a de nouvelle 'criture . envoyer de type appro
      recEntry_L.RESET;
      recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Entry No.", '>%1', recConnectorSetup_L.LastEntryApproHistorySended);
      recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Source Type", '%1|%2', recEntry_L."Source Type"::Customer, recEntry_L."Source Type"::Vendor);
      recEntry_L.SETRANGE("Item Ledger Entry Type", recEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry Type"::Sale);
      recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Document Type", '%1|%2', recEntry_L."Document Type"::"Sales Invoice",
        recEntry_L."Document Type"::"Sales Credit Memo");
      IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryActivity <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Activity Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryActivity);
      END;

      IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryFamily <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryFamily);
      END;

      IF recConnectorSetup_L."EntryApproHistorySub-Family" <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Sub-Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L."EntryApproHistorySub-Family");
      END;

      recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorApproHistory := '';
      IF recEntry_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          IF (recEntry_L."Invoiced Quantity" <> 0) AND (recEntry_L."Sales Amount (Actual)" <> 0) THEN BEGIN
            cduCatchError_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::HistoryAppro, recAutomation_L, recAutomation_L);
            cduCatchError_L.SetParameterHistory(recEntry_L);
            IF cduCatchError_L.RUN() THEN BEGIN
              recConnectorSetup_L.LastEntryApproHistorySended := recEntry_L."Entry No.";
            END ELSE BEGIN
              //erreur : on sauvegarde ce que l'on a pu faire
              recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorApproHistory := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorApproHistory));
              recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
              COMMIT;
              recEntry_L.FINDLAST;
            END;
          END;
        UNTIL recEntry_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
      recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
      COMMIT;

      Code_L := '';
      Separator_L := '';
      RecSalesLine_L.RESET;
      RecSalesLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type",RecSalesLine_L."Document Type"::Order);
      RecSalesLine_L.SETRANGE(Type,RecSalesLine_L.Type::Item);
      IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryActivity <> '' THEN BEGIN
        RecSalesLine_L.SETFILTER("Activity Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryActivity);
      END;
      IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryFamily <> '' THEN BEGIN
        RecSalesLine_L.SETFILTER("Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryFamily);
      END;
      IF recConnectorSetup_L."EntryApproHistorySub-Family" <> '' THEN BEGIN
        RecSalesLine_L.SETFILTER("Sub-Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L."EntryApproHistorySub-Family");
      END;
      recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorHistorySalesLine := '';
      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator;
      IF RecSalesLine_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
        REPEAT
          Code_L := RecSalesLine_L."Document No." + Separator_L + FORMAT(RecSalesLine_L."Line No.");
          IF RecSalesLine_L.Quantity <> RecSalesLine_L."Quantity Invoiced" THEN BEGIN
            IF (NOT RecGUID2_L.GET(RecGUID2_L."Type Data"::HistorySalesLine,Code_L)) OR
               (RecGUID2_L.GET(RecGUID2_L."Type Data"::HistorySalesLine,Code_L) AND RecGUID2_L."Sale Line Modified")
            THEN BEGIN
              cduCatchError_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::HistorySalesLine, recAutomation_L, recAutomation_L);
              cduCatchError_L.SetParameterHistorySalesLine(RecSalesLine_L);
              COMMIT;
              IF cduCatchError_L.RUN() THEN BEGIN
                recConnectorSetup_L."Last Sales Order Sended" := RecSalesLine_L."Document No.";
                recConnectorSetup_L."Last Sales Order Line Sended" := RecSalesLine_L."Line No.";
              END ELSE BEGIN
                //erreur : on sauvegarde ce que l'on a pu faire
                recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorHistorySalesLine := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT,1,
                                                             MAXSTRLEN(recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorHistorySalesLine));
                recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
                COMMIT;
                RecSalesLine_L.FINDLAST;
              END;
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN
            IF (RecGUID2_L.GET(RecGUID2_L."Type Data"::HistorySalesLine,Code_L)) AND RecGUID2_L."Sale Line Modified" THEN BEGIN
              RecGUID2_L."Sale Line Modified" := FALSE;
              RecGUID2_L.MODIFY;
              COMMIT;
            END;
          END;
        UNTIL RecSalesLine_L.NEXT = 0;
      recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
      COMMIT;

      //Recherche s'il y a de nouvelle 'criture . envoyer de type collect
      recEntry_L.RESET;
      recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Entry No.", '>%1', recConnectorSetup_L.LastEntryCollectHistorySended);
      recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Source Type", '%1|%2', recEntry_L."Source Type"::Customer, recEntry_L."Source Type"::Vendor);
      recEntry_L.SETRANGE("Item Ledger Entry Type", recEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry Type"::Purchase);
      recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Movement Type", recConnectorSetup_L."EntryColl Movement Type");
      recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Document Type", '');

      IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryCollectHistoryActivity <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Activity Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryCollectHistoryActivity);
      END;

      IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryCollectHistoryFamily <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryCollectHistoryFamily);
      END;

      IF recConnectorSetup_L."EntryCollectHistorySub-Family" <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Sub-Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L."EntryCollectHistorySub-Family");
      END;

      recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorCollectHistory := '';
      IF recEntry_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          cduCatchError_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::HistoryCollect, recAutomation_L, recAutomation_L);
          cduCatchError_L.SetParameterHistory(recEntry_L);
          IF cduCatchError_L.RUN() THEN BEGIN
            recConnectorSetup_L.LastEntryCollectHistorySended := recEntry_L."Entry No.";
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //erreur : on sauvegarde ce que l'on a pu faire
            recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorCollectHistory := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorCollectHistory));
            recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
            COMMIT;
            recEntry_L.FINDLAST;
          END;
        UNTIL recEntry_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
      cduManCon_G.ClearConnector();
      recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
      COMMIT;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncHistoryFromAccount_D365@60001(Contact_P@8053004 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      recEntry_L@8053003 : Record 5802;
      recConnectorSetup_L@8053002 : Record 8053602;
      cduCatchError_L@8053001 : Codeunit 8053608;
      recAutomation_L@8053000 : Automation "{18033653-DC3D-4EE4-A298-FD5AF3571A9A} 1.0:{E3A42FF0-3633-4846-9C11-20D1AB63FFC9}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class";
      recContactRelation_L@8053005 : Record 5054;
      recItemLedEntry_L@8053006 : Record 32;
      recEntry2_L@8053007 : Record 5802;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation l'historique d'un nouveau contact dans la CRM (compte) (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Contact_P : Contact dont on veut envoyer les 'critures
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      recConnectorSetup_L.GET();
      cduManCon_G.GetAutomation(recAutomation_L);

      recContactRelation_L.RESET;
      recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("MC Company Code", cduManCon_G.GetCompanyMcCode());
      recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("Contact No.", Contact_P);
      recContactRelation_L.SETFILTER("Link to Table", '%1|%2', recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Customer,
        recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Vendor);
      IF recContactRelation_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          //Recherche des 'critures articles
          recItemLedEntry_L.RESET;
          recItemLedEntry_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Source Type", "Source No.");
          IF recContactRelation_L."Link to Table" = recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Customer THEN
            recItemLedEntry_L.SETRANGE("Source Type", recItemLedEntry_L."Source Type"::Customer)
          ELSE
            recItemLedEntry_L.SETRANGE("Source Type", recItemLedEntry_L."Source Type"::Vendor);
          recItemLedEntry_L.SETRANGE("Source No.", recContactRelation_L."No.");
          recItemLedEntry_L.SETFILTER("Entry Type", '%1|%2', recItemLedEntry_L."Entry Type"::Sale,
            recItemLedEntry_L."Entry Type"::Purchase);
          IF recItemLedEntry_L.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              //Recherche des 'critures valeurs
              recEntry_L.RESET;
              recEntry_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Item Ledger Entry No.");
              recEntry_L.SETRANGE("Item Ledger Entry No.", recItemLedEntry_L."Entry No.");
              recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Source Type", '%1|%2', recEntry_L."Source Type"::Customer, recEntry_L."Source Type"::Vendor);
              recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Item Ledger Entry Type", '%1|%2', recEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry Type"::Sale,
                recEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry Type"::Purchase);
              recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Document Type", '%1|%2|%3|%4', recEntry_L."Document Type"::"Sales Invoice",
                recEntry_L."Document Type"::"Sales Credit Memo", recEntry_L."Document Type"::"Purchase Invoice",
                recEntry_L."Document Type"::"Purchase Credit Memo");

              IF recEntry_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
                REPEAT
                  //Recherche s'il y a de nouvelle 'criture . envoyer de type appro
                  recEntry2_L.RESET;
                  recEntry2_L.SETRANGE("Entry No.", recEntry_L."Entry No.");
                  IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryActivity <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    recEntry2_L.SETFILTER("Activity Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryActivity);
                  END;
                  IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryFamily <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    recEntry2_L.SETFILTER("Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryFamily);
                  END;
                  IF recConnectorSetup_L."EntryApproHistorySub-Family" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    recEntry2_L.SETFILTER("Sub-Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L."EntryApproHistorySub-Family");
                  END;
                  recEntry2_L.SETRANGE("Item Ledger Entry Type", recEntry2_L."Item Ledger Entry Type"::Sale);
                  recEntry2_L.SETFILTER("Document Type", '%1|%2', recEntry2_L."Document Type"::"Sales Invoice",
                   recEntry2_L."Document Type"::"Sales Credit Memo");

                  recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorApproHistory := '';
                  IF NOT recEntry2_L.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
                    cduCatchError_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::HistoryAppro, recAutomation_L, recAutomation_L);
                    cduCatchError_L.SetParameterHistory(recEntry_L);
                    IF NOT cduCatchError_L.RUN() THEN BEGIN
                      //erreur : on sauvegarde ce que l'on a pu faire
                      recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorApproHistory := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1,
                        MAXSTRLEN(recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorApproHistory));
                      recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
                      COMMIT;
                    END;
                  END;

                  //Recherche s'il y a de nouvelle 'criture . envoyer de type collect
                  recEntry2_L.RESET;
                  recEntry2_L.SETRANGE("Entry No.", recEntry_L."Entry No.");
                  IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryCollectHistoryActivity <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    recEntry2_L.SETFILTER("Activity Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryCollectHistoryActivity);
                  END;
                  IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryCollectHistoryFamily <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    recEntry2_L.SETFILTER("Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryCollectHistoryFamily);
                  END;
                  IF recConnectorSetup_L."EntryCollectHistorySub-Family" <> '' THEN BEGIN
                    recEntry2_L.SETFILTER("Sub-Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L."EntryCollectHistorySub-Family");
                  END;
                  recEntry2_L.SETRANGE("Item Ledger Entry Type", recEntry2_L."Item Ledger Entry Type"::Purchase);
                  recEntry2_L.SETFILTER("Document Type", '%1|%2', recEntry2_L."Document Type"::"Purchase Invoice",
                    recEntry2_L."Document Type"::"Purchase Credit Memo");

                  recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorCollectHistory := '';
                  IF NOT recEntry2_L.ISEMPTY THEN BEGIN
                    cduCatchError_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::HistoryCollect, recAutomation_L, recAutomation_L);
                    cduCatchError_L.SetParameterHistory(recEntry_L);
                    IF NOT cduCatchError_L.RUN() THEN BEGIN
                      //erreur : on sauvegarde ce que l'on a pu faire
                      recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorCollectHistory := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1,
                        MAXSTRLEN(recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorCollectHistory));
                      recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
                      COMMIT;
                    END;
                  END;
                UNTIL recEntry_L.NEXT = 0;
              END;
            UNTIL recItemLedEntry_L.NEXT = 0;
          END;
        UNTIL recContactRelation_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;

      cduManCon_G.ClearConnector_D365;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetCrmConnexion_D365@60007(VAR recAutomation_P@8053600 : Automation "{18033653-DC3D-4EE4-A298-FD5AF3571A9A} 1.0:{E3A42FF0-3633-4846-9C11-20D1AB63FFC9}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class");
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de n'utiliser qu'une seule initialisation de la connexion GRC
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recAutomation_P : Codeunit de gestion du connecteur
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      cduManCon_G.SetAutomation_D365(recAutomation_P);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetCrmConnexion2_D365@60006(VAR recAutomation_P@8053600 : Automation "{18033653-DC3D-4EE4-A298-FD5AF3571A9A} 1.0:{E3A42FF0-3633-4846-9C11-20D1AB63FFC9}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class");
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Permet de n'utiliser qu'une seule initialisation de la connexion GRC
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - recAutomation_P : Codeunit de gestion du connecteur
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      cduManCon_G.SetAutomation2_D365(recAutomation_P);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncAccount_D365@60009(Code_P@8053601 : Text[50];TypeAction_P@8053600 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      recContact_L@8053602 : Record 5050;
      recCustomer_L@8053603 : Record 18;
      recVendor_L@8053604 : Record 23;
      recContBusRel_L@8053605 : Record 5054;
      SyncCustomer_L@8053608 : Boolean;
      SyncVendor_L@8053606 : Boolean;
      EntityName_L@8053607 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053609 : Text[50];
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053610 : Text[50];
      Finded_L@8053611 : Boolean;
      UserGuid_L@8053612 : Text[50];
      recMasterRelation_L@8053613 : Record 8053076;
      recThirdPartyToCreate_L@8053614 : Record 8053600;
      recShipToAddress_L@8053615 : Record 222;
      recUnloading_L@8053616 : Record 8053169;
      recGuid_L@8053617 : Record 8053601;
      UnloadingPlaceCode_L@8053618 : Text[54];
      recGuidUpdate_L@8053619 : Record 8053601;
      GetGuidFromContact_L@8053620 : Boolean;
      Separator_L@8053621 : Text[1];
      CityGuid_L@8053622 : Text[50];
      PostalGuid_L@8053623 : Text[50];
      CountryGuid_L@8053624 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid2_L@8053625 : Text[50];
      ContactGuid_L@8053626 : Text[50];
      recSetup_L@8053627 : Record 8053602;
      recCampaign_L@8053628 : Record 8052991;
      StatusCode_L@1000000000 : Text[50];
      JRLEXPORT_L@1000000001 : Record 8053606;
      RecContactSalesperson_L@1000000002 : Record 8053160;
      RecCRMSalesperson_L@1000000003 : Record 8053608;
      OwnerGUID_L@1000000004 : Text[50];
      PayMethGUID_L@1000000005 : Text[50];
      PayTermsGUID_L@1000000006 : Text[50];
      LocationGUID_L@1000000007 : Text[50];
      ShipMethGUID_L@1000000008 : Text[50];
      ComModeGUID_L@1000000009 : Text[50];
      NewCityGUID_L@1000000010 : Text[50];
      ParentAccount_L@1000000011 : Text[50];
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un compte (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'account';
      SyncCustomer_L := FALSE;
      SyncVendor_L := FALSE;
      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        recSetup_L.GET;
        Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();
        recContact_L.GET(Code_P);
        IF recContact_L."Contact CRM" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          EXIT;
        END;
        //Si contact de type personne alors pas de synchronisation
        IF recContact_L.Type <> recContact_L.Type::Company THEN
          EXIT;

        IF CodeCompany_G = '' THEN
          CodeCompany_G := cduManCon_G.GetCompanyMcCode();

        recCampaign_L.RESET;
        recCampaign_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Type Code", "Starting Date", "Ending Date");
        recCampaign_L.SETFILTER("Starting Date", '<=%1', WORKDATE);
        recCampaign_L.SETFILTER("Ending Date", '>=%1', WORKDATE);
        IF recCampaign_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        END;

        recContBusRel_L.RESET;
        recContBusRel_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Link to Table", "Contact No.", "MC Company Code");
        recContBusRel_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recContBusRel_L."Link to Table"::Customer);
        recContBusRel_L.SETRANGE("Contact No.", Code_P);
        recContBusRel_L.SETRANGE("MC Company Code", CodeCompany_G);
        IF recContBusRel_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          recCustomer_L.GET(recContBusRel_L."No.");
          SyncCustomer_L := TRUE;
        END;

        recContBusRel_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recContBusRel_L."Link to Table"::Vendor);
        IF recContBusRel_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          recVendor_L.GET(recContBusRel_L."No.");
          SyncVendor_L := TRUE;
        END;

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);

        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053626, recContact_L."No.");
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(recContact_L."Dynamics CRM Id")) THEN
            TxtCrmGuid_L := recContact_L."Dynamics CRM Id";
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053626, recContact_L."No.", recContact_L."Dynamics CRM Id");
        END;

        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            TxtCrmGuid_L := '';
          END;
        END;

        TxtCrmGuid2_L := TxtCrmGuid_L;

        //Recherche du Tiers MS . Cr'' pour retrouver un 'ventuel GUID
        IF cduManCon_G.SearchThirdPartyToCreate(recContact_L."No.", recThirdPartyToCreate_L, CodeCompany_G, '') THEN BEGIN
          //Contact existant
          TxtCrmGuid2_L := recThirdPartyToCreate_L."Dynamics CRM id";
          cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053626, recContact_L."No.", recThirdPartyToCreate_L."Dynamics CRM id");
        END;

        StatusCode_L := '';
        PostalGuid_L := '';
        CityGuid_L := '';
        CountryGuid_L := '';
        IF TxtCrmGuid2_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
          //cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_villeid', '');
          //cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_country_id', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode, '');
          IF cduManCon_G.GetRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid2_L) THEN BEGIN
            //PostalGuid_L := cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id'));
            CityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_villeid'));
            //CountryGuid_L := cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(cduManCon_G.GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_country_id'));
            StatusCode_L := cduManCon_G.GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, TextStatusCode);
          END;
        END;

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
        //On ne r'cupSre que les valeurs qui sont utiles pour la mise . jour
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'name', recContact_L.Name); //Name

        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_statutsynchronisation', '990260002'); // mettre Synchronis'
        {cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_young', cduManCon_G.FormatBoolToCrm(recContact_L.IsYoungFarmerCtt(
          recCampaign_L."No."))); //Young Farmer} //to add ?
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'accountnumber', recContact_L."No."); //No.
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'telephone1', recContact_L."Phone No."); //Phone No.
      //cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_shortname', COPYSTR(recContact_L."Search Name", 1, 10)); //Search Name   //to add ?

        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'fax', recContact_L."Fax No."); //Fax No.
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'emailaddress1', recContact_L."E-Mail"); //E-Mail
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'websiteurl', recContact_L."Home Page"); //Home Page
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'address1_line1', recContact_L.Address); //Address
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'address1_line2', recContact_L."Address 2"); //Address 2
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'address1_line3', recContact_L.County); //County
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_type','175490000'); //Type Client

        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_dateencours',cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(WORKDATE)); //Date En cours
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_numtva', recContact_L."VAT Registration No."); //N› TVA

        //Mode de communication
        IF recContact_L."Farmer List 3" <> '' THEN BEGIN
          ComModeGUID_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text60004, recContact_L."Farmer List 3");
          cduManCon_G.GuidFinded_D365(recContact_L."Farmer List 3", ComModeGUID_L,
                                      recContact_L.FIELDCAPTION("Farmer List 3"));
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_modecommunication', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(ComModeGUID_L));
        END;

        // Add20170419
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_situationdecompte', recContact_L."Farmer List 2"); //Situation du compte
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_telmobile', recContact_L."Mobile Phone No."); //T'l'phone mobile
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_appartenancegroupe',recContact_L."Farmer List 4"); //Appartenance . un groupe

        //Propri'taire
        OwnerGUID_L := GetOwnerID(recContact_L."No.");
        {
        OwnerGUID_L := '';
        CLEAR(RecContactSalesperson_L);
        RecContactSalesperson_L.SETRANGE("Contact Code",recContact_L."No.");
        IF RecContactSalesperson_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          CLEAR(RecCRMSalesperson_L);
          RecCRMSalesperson_L.SETRANGE(Code,RecContactSalesperson_L."Salesperson Code");
          IF RecCRMSalesperson_L.FINDFIRST THEN
            OwnerGUID_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053631, RecCRMSalesperson_L."User ID");
        END;
        }

        //Il faut faire le lien avec la table de relation
        {
        // Les codes postaux sont d'sormais inclus dans l'entit' Ville
        IF recContact_L."Post Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Post Code
          TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053613, recContact_L."Post Code");
          cduManCon_G.GuidFinded_D365(recContact_L."Post Code", TxtEntityGuid_L, recContact_L.FIELDCAPTION("Post Code"));
          IF PostalGuid_L <> TxtEntityGuid_L THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
          END;
        END;
        }

        IF recContact_L.City <> '' THEN BEGIN //City
          TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053612, recContact_L."Post Code" + Separator_L + recContact_L.City);
          cduManCon_G.GuidFinded_D365(recContact_L.City, TxtEntityGuid_L, recContact_L.FIELDCAPTION(City));
          IF CityGuid_L <> TxtEntityGuid_L THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_villeid', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
          END;
        END;

        IF recContact_L."Country/Region Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Country/Region Code
          TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053611, recContact_L."Country/Region Code");
          cduManCon_G.GuidFinded_D365(recContact_L."Country/Region Code", TxtEntityGuid_L,
                                      recContact_L.FIELDCAPTION("Country/Region Code"));
          IF CountryGuid_L <> TxtEntityGuid_L THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pays', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
          END;
        END;

       { IF recContact_L."Customer Blocked" = recContact_L."Customer Blocked"::" " THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_frozenbalance', '0'); //Customer Not Blocked
        END ELSE BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_frozenbalance', '1'); //Customer Blocked
        END;
        } // to add ?


        //Magasin par d'faut
        IF recContact_L."Location Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
          LocationGUID_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053623, recContact_L."Location Code");
          cduManCon_G.GuidFinded_D365(recContact_L."Location Code", LocationGUID_L,
                                      recContact_L.FIELDCAPTION("Location Code"));
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_magasinid', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(LocationGUID_L));
        END;


        //Les donn'es suivantes viennent du client s'il existe
        IF SyncCustomer_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN

          //Code mode de rSglement
          IF recCustomer_L."Payment Method Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            PayMethGUID_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text60001, recCustomer_L."Payment Method Code");
            cduManCon_G.GuidFinded_D365(recCustomer_L."Payment Method Code", PayMethGUID_L,
                                        recCustomer_L.FIELDCAPTION("Payment Method Code"));
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_modereglementid', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(PayMethGUID_L));
          END;

          //Code condition de paiement
          IF recCustomer_L."Payment Terms Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            PayTermsGUID_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text60002, recCustomer_L."Payment Terms Code");
            cduManCon_G.GuidFinded_D365(recCustomer_L."Payment Terms Code", PayTermsGUID_L,
                                        recCustomer_L.FIELDCAPTION("Payment Terms Code"));
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_conditionpaiementid', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(PayTermsGUID_L));
          END;

          {
          //Magasin par d'faut
          IF recCustomer_L."Location Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            LocationGUID_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053623, recCustomer_L."Location Code");
            cduManCon_G.GuidFinded_D365(recCustomer_L."Location Code", LocationGUID_L,
                                        recCustomer_L.FIELDCAPTION("Location Code"));
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_magasinid', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(LocationGUID_L));
          END;
          }

          //Mode de livraison pr'f'r'
          IF recCustomer_L."Shipment Method Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
            ShipMethGUID_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text60003, recCustomer_L."Shipment Method Code");
            cduManCon_G.GuidFinded_D365(recCustomer_L."Shipment Method Code", ShipMethGUID_L,
                                        recCustomer_L.FIELDCAPTION("Shipment Method Code"));
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_modelivraisonid', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(ShipMethGUID_L));
          END;

          recContact_L.CALCFIELDS("Balance (LCY)"); //Balance (LCY)
          recContact_L.SETFILTER("Date Filter", '..%1', WORKDATE);
          recContact_L.CALCFIELDS("Balance Due (LCY)"); //Balance Due (LCY)
          //En cours Non 'chu
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_encoursechu',cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm(recContact_L."Balance Due (LCY)"));
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_encoursnonechu',
          cduManCon_G.FormatDecToCrm( recContact_L."Balance (LCY)" -recContact_L."Balance Due (LCY)"));  //En cours Non 'chu

          //Compte parent
          IF recCustomer_L."Third Grouping" <> recCustomer_L."No." THEN BEGIN
          ParentAccount_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053626, recCustomer_L."Third Grouping");

          VerifGuidFinded(recCustomer_L."Third Grouping", ParentAccount_L, recCustomer_L.FIELDCAPTION("Third Grouping"));
            //IF cduManCon_G.GetCodeFromGuid(Text8053626, ParentAccount_L, FALSE) <> recContact_L."No." THEN
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'parentaccountid', ParentAccount_L);
          END;

          //N› SIRET
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_siret',recCustomer_L."Registration No.");
          // <-- 20170503-WI00-TER01.01.00-BSI01
        END;

        //Les donn'es suivantes viennent du fournisseur s'il existe
        IF SyncVendor_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
          //Setting-off Allowed
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_compensable', cduManCon_G.FormatBoolToCrm(
            recVendor_L."Setting-off Allowed"));
        END;
        //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
          UserGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053631, USERID);
          Finded_L := FALSE;
          //Recherche du Tiers MS . Cr'' pour retrouver un 'ventuel GUID
          IF cduManCon_G.SearchThirdPartyToCreate(recContact_L."No.", recThirdPartyToCreate_L, CodeCompany_G, '') THEN BEGIN
            //Contact existant
            TxtCrmGuid_L := recThirdPartyToCreate_L."Dynamics CRM id";
            Finded_L := cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END;

          IF Finded_L = FALSE THEN BEGIN
           {
           cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_potentialpig', '175490002');      //Ne sait pas
           cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_potentialplant', '175490002');    //Ne sait pas
           cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_potentialpoultry', '175490002');  //Ne sait pas
           cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_potentialrabbit', '175490002');   //Ne sait pas
           cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_potentialruminant', '175490002'); //Ne sait pas
           } // to add ?
           Finded_L := cduManCon_G.CreateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END;
          IF Finded_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
            //on remplit le GUID sur le contact
            //"Dynamics CRM Id"
            recContact_L."Dynamics CRM Id" := cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(TxtCrmGuid_L);
            recContact_L.MODIFY;

            //on remplit le GUID sur le client
            IF SyncCustomer_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN //"Dynamics CRM Id"
              IF ISNULLGUID(recCustomer_L."Dynamics CRM Id") THEN BEGIN
                recCustomer_L."Dynamics CRM Id" := cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(TxtCrmGuid_L);
                recCustomer_L.MODIFY;
              END;
            END;

            //on remplit le GUID sur le fournisseur
            IF SyncVendor_L = TRUE THEN BEGIN
              IF ISNULLGUID(recVendor_L."Dynamics CRM Id") THEN BEGIN //"Dynamics CRM Id"
                recVendor_L."Dynamics CRM Id" := cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(TxtCrmGuid_L);
                recVendor_L.MODIFY;
              END;
            END;

            IF OwnerGUID_L <> '' THEN
              cduManCon_G.AssignOwner_D365(OwnerGUID_L,EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);

            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053626, recContact_L."No.", TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            IF OwnerGUID_L <> '' THEN
              cduManCon_G.AssignOwner_D365(OwnerGUID_L,EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
            //Mise .  jour r'ussie
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid(Text8053626, recContact_L."No.", TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Mise . jour 'chou'e
          END;
        END;

        IF StatusCode_L <> '2' THEN // On ne synchronise pas le statut si le compte est d'sactiv' dans la CRM
        CASE recContact_L."Activity State" OF
          recContact_L."Activity State"::Active: BEGIN //Actif
            cduManCon_G.SetState_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 1); //Active - Actif
          END;

          recContact_L."Activity State"::"Active Without Activity": BEGIN //Actif sans activit'
            cduManCon_G.SetState_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 175490000); //Active - Actif sans activit'
          END;

          recContact_L."Activity State"::"Not Active": BEGIN //Non actif
            cduManCon_G.SetState_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, 2); // Inactive - Inactive
          END;
        END;

        IF recCustomer_L."No." <> '' THEN BEGIN
          //Mise . jour de l'adresse du contact/client
          cduManCon_G.AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::"Ship-to Address", recCustomer_L."No.", TextModify, COMPANYNAME);
          //cduManCon_G.AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::"Unloading Place", recCustomer_L."No.", TextModify, COMPANYNAME);

          //Synchronisation des adresses de livraison du client
          recShipToAddress_L.RESET;
          recShipToAddress_L.SETRANGE("Customer No.", recCustomer_L."No.");
          IF recShipToAddress_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
            REPEAT
              cduManCon_G.AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::"Ship-to Address", recCustomer_L."No." + Separator_L +
                recShipToAddress_L.Code, TextModify, COMPANYNAME);
              {
              //Synchronisation des lieux de d'chargement
              recUnloading_L.RESET;
              recUnloading_L.SETRANGE("Customer No.", recShipToAddress_L."Customer No.");
              recUnloading_L.SETRANGE("Ship-to Code", recShipToAddress_L.Code);
              IF recUnloading_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
                REPEAT
                  cduManCon_G.AddJournalExport(recJrnExp_G.Type::"Unloading Place", recCustomer_L."No." + Separator_L +
                    recShipToAddress_L.Code + Separator_L + recUnloading_L.Code, TextModify, COMPANYNAME);
                UNTIL recUnloading_L.NEXT = 0;

              recGuid_L.RESET;
              recGuid_L.SETRANGE("Type Data", recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Unloading Place");
              recGuid_L.SETFILTER("Code Nav", recShipToAddress_L."Customer No." + Separator_L + recShipToAddress_L.Code +
                Separator_L + '*');
              IF recGuid_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN
                REPEAT
                  UnloadingPlaceCode_L := COPYSTR(recGuid_L."Code Nav", STRPOS(recGuid_L."Code Nav", Separator_L) + 1,
                    STRLEN(recGuid_L."Code Nav"));
                  UnloadingPlaceCode_L := COPYSTR(UnloadingPlaceCode_L, STRPOS(UnloadingPlaceCode_L, Separator_L) + 1,
                    STRLEN(UnloadingPlaceCode_L));
                  IF NOT(recUnloading_L.GET(recShipToAddress_L."Customer No.", recShipToAddress_L.Code, UnloadingPlaceCode_L))
                  THEN BEGIN
                    cduManCon_G.SetState_D365('cmc_locationunloading', recGuid_L.GUID, 2);
                    recGuidUpdate_L.GET(recGuid_L."Type Data", recGuid_L."Code Nav");
                    recGuidUpdate_L.DELETE;
                  END;
                UNTIL recGuid_L.NEXT = 0;
            }
            UNTIL recShipToAddress_L.NEXT = 0;
        END;
        // --> import contact et adresses de livraison
        cduManCon_G.InsertMQContact_D365(TxtCrmGuid_L);
        cduManCon_G.InsertMQAdresses_D365(TxtCrmGuid_L);
      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        ContactGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053626, Code_P);
        //supression du contact(Nav)/Compte(CRM) ?
        IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(ContactGuid_L)) THEN
          cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav_D365(EntityName_L, ContactGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::Account, 2, 1);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncAddress_D365@60012(Code_P@8053604 : Text[50];TypeAction_P@8053601 : Code[10]);
    VAR
      EntityName_L@8053603 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053602 : Text[50];
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053600 : Text[50];
      CustomerCode_L@8053606 : Code[20];
      AddressCode_L@8053605 : Code[10];
      recCustomer_L@8053607 : Record 18;
      recAddress_L@8053608 : Record 222;
      TypeAddress_L@8053609 : Integer;
      GuidAddress_L@8053610 : Text[50];
      recGuid_L@8053611 : Record 8053601;
      Separator_L@8053612 : Text[1];
      recContactRelation_L@1000000002 : Record 5054;
      recContact_L@1000000000 : Record 5050;
      ContactFinded_L@1000000001 : Boolean;
      TxtCrmAccountGuid_L@1000000003 : Text[50];
      RecPays_L@1000000004 : Record 9;
      OwnerGUID_L@1000000007 : Text[50];
      RecCRMSalesperson_L@1000000006 : Record 8053608;
      RecContactSalesperson_L@1000000005 : Record 8053160;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'une adresse (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'plus_adresse';
      Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();
      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);

        //R'cup'ration du code client et du code de l'adresse
        IF STRPOS(Code_P, Separator_L) > 0 THEN BEGIN
          CustomerCode_L := COPYSTR(COPYSTR(Code_P, 1, STRPOS(Code_P, Separator_L) - 1), 1, MAXSTRLEN(CustomerCode_L));
          AddressCode_L := COPYSTR(Code_P, STRPOS(Code_P, Separator_L) + 1, MAXSTRLEN(AddressCode_L));
          IF NOT(recAddress_L.GET(CustomerCode_L, AddressCode_L)) THEN
            EXIT;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          CustomerCode_L := COPYSTR(Code_P, 1, MAXSTRLEN(CustomerCode_L));
          AddressCode_L := '';
        END;

        IF NOT(recCustomer_L.GET(CustomerCode_L)) THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.ClearConnector_D365;
          ERROR(TextError8053600, CustomerCode_L, recCustomer_L.TABLECAPTION);
        END;

        //Recherche du contact li' au client
        ContactFinded_L := FALSE;
        recContactRelation_L.RESET;
        recContactRelation_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Link to Table", "No.", "MC Company Code");
        recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("Link to Table", recContactRelation_L."Link to Table"::Customer);
        recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("No.", recCustomer_L."No.");
        recContactRelation_L.SETRANGE("MC Company Code", cduManCon_G.GetCompanyMcCode());
        IF recContactRelation_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          recContact_L.GET(recContactRelation_L."Contact No.");
          IF (recContact_L."Contact CRM" = FALSE) OR (recContact_L.Type <> recContact_L.Type::Company) THEN BEGIN
            EXIT;
          END;

          //R'cup'ration du GUID
          TxtCrmAccountGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053626, recContact_L."No.");
          IF TxtCrmAccountGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmAccountGuid_L)) THEN
              ContactFinded_L := TRUE;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          // pas de relation trouv' = pas de synchro
          EXIT;
        END;

        TypeAddress_L := 1;

        IF AddressCode_L = '' THEN BEGIN
          EXIT;

          cduManCon_G.ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
          IF ContactFinded_L = TRUE THEN
            cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_compteid', '=', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtCrmAccountGuid_L))
          ELSE
            cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_compteid', '=',
                                        cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(recCustomer_L."Dynamics CRM Id"));
          //cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '=', '175490003'); //adresse principale

          IF cduManCon_G.SelectMultipleRecords_D365(EntityName_L) > 0 THEN BEGIN
            GuidAddress_L := cduManCon_G.GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id');
            cduManCon_G.UpdateGuid(Text8053635, recCustomer_L."No.", cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(GuidAddress_L));
          END;

        END ELSE BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id', '');
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_nom', '=', recAddress_L.Name); //Name
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_rue1', '=', recAddress_L.Address); //Address
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_rue2', '=', recAddress_L."Address 2"); //Address 2
          cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_rue3', '=', recAddress_L.County); //County

          //Il faut faire le lien avec la table de relation
          {IF recAddress_L."Post Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Post Code
            TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053613, recAddress_L."Post Code");
            cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id', '=',
                                            cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
          END;
          }

          IF recAddress_L.City <> '' THEN BEGIN //City
            TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053612, recAddress_L."Post Code" + Separator_L +
                                                   recAddress_L.City);
            cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_villeid', '=', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
          END;

          IF recAddress_L."Country/Region Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Country/Region Code
            TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053611, recAddress_L."Country/Region Code");
            cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pays', '=', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
          END;


          {CASE TypeAddress_L OF
            1:  cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '=', '175490000'); //livraison
            2:  cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '=', '175490001'); //facturation
            3:  cduManCon_G.SetFilterEntity_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '=', '175490002'); //exp'dition
          END;}

          IF cduManCon_G.SelectMultipleRecords_D365(EntityName_L) > 0 THEN BEGIN
            GuidAddress_L := cduManCon_G.GetAttribute_D365(EntityName_L, EntityName_L + 'id');
            cduManCon_G.UpdateGuid(Text8053635, recCustomer_L."No.", cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(GuidAddress_L));
          END;
        END;

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
        IF ContactFinded_L = TRUE THEN
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_compteid', TxtCrmAccountGuid_L)
        ELSE
          cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_compteid', recCustomer_L."Dynamics CRM Id");

        IF AddressCode_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_nom', recAddress_L.Name); //Name
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_rue1', recAddress_L.Address); //Address
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_rue2', recAddress_L."Address 2"); //Address 2
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_rue3', recAddress_L.County); //County
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_code', recAddress_L.Code);

          IF NOT RecPays_L.GET(recAddress_L."Country/Region Code") THEN
            CLEAR(RecPays_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_pays',RecPays_L.Name);


          //Il faut faire le lien avec la table de relation
          {IF recAddress_L."Post Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Post Code
            TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053613, recAddress_L."Post Code");
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id', TxtEntityGuid_L);
          END;
          }
          IF recAddress_L.City <> '' THEN BEGIN //City
            TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053612, recAddress_L."Post Code" + Separator_L +
              recAddress_L.City);
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_villeid', TxtEntityGuid_L);
          END;

          {IF recAddress_L."Country/Region Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Country/Region Code
            TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053611, recAddress_L."Country/Region Code");
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_country_id', TxtEntityGuid_L);
          END;}
        END ELSE BEGIN
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_nom', recCustomer_L.Name); //Name
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_rue1', recCustomer_L.Address); //Address
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_rue2', recCustomer_L."Address 2"); //Address 2
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_rue3', recCustomer_L.County); //County

          IF NOT RecPays_L.GET(recCustomer_L."Country/Region Code") THEN
            CLEAR(RecPays_L);
          cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L,'plus_pays',RecPays_L.Name);

          //Il faut faire le lien avec la table de relation
          {IF recCustomer_L."Post Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Post Code
            TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053613, recCustomer_L."Post Code");
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_postalcode_id', TxtEntityGuid_L);
          END;
          }
          IF recCustomer_L.City <> '' THEN BEGIN //City
            TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053612, recCustomer_L."Post Code" + Separator_L +
              recCustomer_L.City);
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'plus_villeid', TxtEntityGuid_L);
          END;

          {IF recCustomer_L."Country/Region Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Country
            TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053611, recCustomer_L."Country/Region Code");
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_address1_country_id', TxtEntityGuid_L);
          END;}
        END;

       {CASE TypeAddress_L OF
          0:  cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '175490003'); //adresse principale
          1:  cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '175490000'); //livraison
          2:  cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '175490001'); //facturation
          3:  cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_additionaladdresstype', '175490002'); //exp'dition
        END;
        }
        //Recherche du GUID de l'adresse de livraison
        IF TypeAddress_L = 0 THEN
          GuidAddress_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053635, Code_P)
        ELSE
          GuidAddress_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053636, Code_P);

        //mise . jour ou cr'ation de l'adresse
        IF GuidAddress_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF NOT(cduManCon_G.ExistRecord_D365(EntityName_L, GuidAddress_L)) THEN
            GuidAddress_L := '';
        END;


        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_statutdesynchronisation', '990260002'); //Synchronis'
        // Propri'taire
        OwnerGUID_L := GetOwnerID(CustomerCode_L);
        {
        OwnerGUID_L := '';
        CLEAR(RecContactSalesperson_L);
        RecContactSalesperson_L.SETRANGE("Contact Code", CustomerCode_L);
        IF RecContactSalesperson_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          CLEAR(RecCRMSalesperson_L);
          RecCRMSalesperson_L.SETRANGE(Code,RecContactSalesperson_L."Salesperson Code");
          IF RecCRMSalesperson_L.FINDFIRST THEN
            OwnerGUID_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053631, RecCRMSalesperson_L."User ID");
        END;
        }

        IF GuidAddress_L = '' THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, GuidAddress_L) THEN BEGIN
            IF OwnerGUID_L <> '' THEN
              cduManCon_G.AssignOwner_D365(OwnerGUID_L,EntityName_L, GuidAddress_L);

            IF TypeAddress_L = 0 THEN BEGIN
              cduManCon_G.UpdateGuid(Text8053635, recCustomer_L."No.", GuidAddress_L);
            END ELSE BEGIN
              cduManCon_G.UpdateGuid(Text8053636, recCustomer_L."No." + Separator_L + recAddress_L.Code, GuidAddress_L);
            END;
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, GuidAddress_L) THEN BEGIN
            IF OwnerGUID_L <> '' THEN
              cduManCon_G.AssignOwner_D365(OwnerGUID_L,EntityName_L, GuidAddress_L);
          END;
        END;
      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        //supression de l'article
        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        IF STRPOS(Code_P, Separator_L) > 0 THEN
          TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053636, Code_P)
        ELSE
          TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053635, Code_P);
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L)) THEN
            IF STRPOS(Code_P, Separator_L) > 0 THEN
              cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::"Address Ship-to", 2, 1)
            ELSE
              cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::Address, 2, 1);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncContact_D365@1000000004(Code_P@8053601 : Text[50];TypeAction_P@8053600 : Code[10]);
    VAR
