      recContact_L@8053602 : Record 5050;
      recCustomer_L@8053603 : Record 18;
      recVendor_L@8053604 : Record 23;
      recContBusRel_L@8053605 : Record 5054;
      EntityName_L@8053607 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid_L@8053609 : Text[50];
      TxtEntityGuid_L@8053610 : Text[50];
      Finded_L@8053611 : Boolean;
      recMasterRelation_L@8053613 : Record 8053076;
      recThirdPartyToCreate_L@8053614 : Record 8053600;
      recShipToAddress_L@8053615 : Record 222;
      recUnloading_L@8053616 : Record 8053169;
      recGuid_L@8053617 : Record 8053601;
      UnloadingPlaceCode_L@8053618 : Text[54];
      recGuidUpdate_L@8053619 : Record 8053601;
      GetGuidFromContact_L@8053620 : Boolean;
      Separator_L@8053621 : Text[1];
      CityGuid_L@8053622 : Text[50];
      PostalGuid_L@8053623 : Text[50];
      CountryGuid_L@8053624 : Text[50];
      TxtCrmGuid2_L@8053625 : Text[50];
      ContactGuid_L@8053626 : Text[50];
      recSetup_L@8053627 : Record 8053602;
      StatusCode_L@1000000000 : Text[50];
      JRLEXPORT_L@1000000001 : Record 8053606;
      SocieteGuid_L@1000000002 : Text[50];
      OwnerGUID_L@1000000003 : Text[50];
      RecCRMSalesperson_L@1000000004 : Record 8053608;
      RecContactSalesperson_L@1000000005 : Record 8053160;
      NewCityGUID_L@1000000006 : Text[50];
      RecContactAccreditation_L@1000000007 : Record 8053314;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation d'un compte (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //    - Code_P : Code de l'entit' . synchroniser
      //    - TypeAction_P : Type d'action
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      EntityName_L := 'contact';
      IF (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextModify) OR (UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextRename) THEN BEGIN
        recSetup_L.GET;
        Separator_L := cduManCon_G.GetSeparator();
        recContact_L.GET(Code_P);

        SocieteGuid_L := '';

        IF recContact_L."Contact CRM" = FALSE THEN BEGIN
          EXIT;
        END;

        IF recContact_L.Type <> recContact_L.Type::Person THEN
          EXIT;

      {  IF (recContact_L.City <> '') AND (recContact_L."Post Code" <> '') THEN
          cduManCon_G.SyncVille(recContact_L."Post Code",recContact_L.City);
       }

        IF CodeCompany_G = '' THEN
          CodeCompany_G := cduManCon_G.GetCompanyMcCode();

        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);

        //R'cup'ration du GUID
        TxtCrmGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text60000, recContact_L."No.");
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
          IF NOT(ISNULLGUID(recContact_L."Dynamics CRM Id")) THEN
            TxtCrmGuid_L := recContact_L."Dynamics CRM Id";
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid(Text60000, recContact_L."No.", recContact_L."Dynamics CRM Id");
        END;

        IF TxtCrmGuid_L <> '' THEN BEGIN
          IF ISNULLGUID(TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            TxtCrmGuid_L := '';
          END;
        END;

        SocieteGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053626, recContact_L."Company No.");
        VerifGuidFinded(recContact_L."Company No.", SocieteGuid_L, recContact_L.FIELDCAPTION("Company No."));

        //TxtCrmGuid2_L := TxtCrmGuid_L;
        cduManCon_G.ResetAuto_D365(EntityName_L);
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_contactnumber', recContact_L."No.");
        cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'parentcustomerid', SocieteGuid_L); // nom de la soci't' parente
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'lastname', recContact_L.Name);
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'address1_line1', recContact_L.Address);
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'address1_line2', recContact_L."Address 2");
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'telephone1', recContact_L."Phone No.");
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'emailaddress1', recContact_L."E-Mail"); //E-Mail
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'websiteurl', recContact_L."Home Page"); //Home Page
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L,'address1_postalcode',recContact_L."Post Code");
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_statutdesynchronisation', '990260002'); // Synchronis'
        cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L, 'mobilephone', recContact_L."Mobile Phone No."); //T'l'phone mobile

        // Code agr'ment CertiPhyto
        IF recSetup_L."Certiphyto Accreditation Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN
          RecContactAccreditation_L.SETRANGE("Contact Type",RecContactAccreditation_L."Contact Type"::Person);
          RecContactAccreditation_L.SETRANGE("Contact No.",recContact_L."No.");
          RecContactAccreditation_L.SETRANGE("Accreditation Code",recSetup_L."Certiphyto Accreditation Code");
          IF RecContactAccreditation_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L,'cmc_numerophytolicence',RecContactAccreditation_L."Accreditation No.");
            cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L,'cmc_datevaliditephytolicence',
                                      cduManCon_G.FormatDateToCrm(RecContactAccreditation_L."Ending Date"));
          END;
        END;
        //cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L,'address1_city',recContact_L.City);
        //cduManCon_G.SetField_D365(EntityName_L,'address1_country',recContact_L."Country/Region Code");
        IF recContact_L.City <> '' THEN BEGIN //City
          TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053612, recContact_L."Post Code" + Separator_L + recContact_L.City);
          cduManCon_G.GuidFinded_D365(recContact_L.City, TxtEntityGuid_L, recContact_L.FIELDCAPTION(City));
          IF CityGuid_L <> TxtEntityGuid_L THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_plus_villeid', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
          END;
        END;

        IF recContact_L."Country/Region Code" <> '' THEN BEGIN //Country/Region Code
          TxtEntityGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053611, recContact_L."Country/Region Code");
          cduManCon_G.GuidFinded_D365(recContact_L."Country/Region Code", TxtEntityGuid_L,
                                      recContact_L.FIELDCAPTION("Country/Region Code"));
          IF CountryGuid_L <> TxtEntityGuid_L THEN BEGIN
            cduManCon_G.SetFieldGuid_D365(EntityName_L, 'cmc_pays', cduManCon_G.CleanGuidToCRM(TxtEntityGuid_L));
          END;
        END;

        //Propri'taire
        OwnerGUID_L := GetOwnerID(recContact_L."Company No.");
        {
        OwnerGUID_L := '';
        CLEAR(RecContactSalesperson_L);
        RecContactSalesperson_L.SETRANGE("Contact Code", recContact_L."Company No.");
        IF RecContactSalesperson_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          CLEAR(RecCRMSalesperson_L);
          RecCRMSalesperson_L.SETRANGE(Code,RecContactSalesperson_L."Salesperson Code");
          IF RecCRMSalesperson_L.FINDFIRST THEN
            OwnerGUID_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053631, RecCRMSalesperson_L."User ID");
        END;
        }
        //Si la donn'e n'existe pas, on la cr''e.
        IF TxtCrmGuid_L = '' THEN BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.CreateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            //on remplit le GUID sur le contact
            //"Dynamics CRM Id"
            IF OwnerGUID_L <> '' THEN
              cduManCon_G.AssignOwner_D365(OwnerGUID_L,EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
            recContact_L."Dynamics CRM Id" := cduManCon_G.FormatGuid(TxtCrmGuid_L);
            recContact_L.MODIFY;
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid(Text60000, recContact_L."No.", TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END;
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF cduManCon_G.UpdateRecord_D365(EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L) THEN BEGIN
            IF OwnerGUID_L <> '' THEN
              cduManCon_G.AssignOwner_D365(OwnerGUID_L,EntityName_L, TxtCrmGuid_L);
            //Mise .  jour r'ussie
            //on remplit le GUID dans la liste des GUID
            cduManCon_G.CreateLinkedGuid(Text60000, recContact_L."No.", TxtCrmGuid_L);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            //Mise . jour 'chou'e
          END;
        END;

      END;
      IF UPPERCASE(TypeAction_P) = TextDelete THEN BEGIN
        ContactGuid_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text60000, Code_P);
        //supression du contact(Nav)/Compte(CRM) ?
        IF NOT(cduManCon_G.GuidIsEmpty(ContactGuid_L)) THEN
          cduManCon_G.DeleteEntityFromNav_D365(EntityName_L, ContactGuid_L, recGuid_L."Type Data"::Contact, 2, 1);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetOwnerID@1000000003(ContactNo_P@1000000001 : Code[20]) OwnerGUIDRes_L : Text[50];
    VAR
      RecContactSalesperson_L@1000000003 : Record 8053160;
      RecCRMSalesperson_L@1000000002 : Record 8053608;
    BEGIN
      // Propri'taire
      OwnerGUIDRes_L := '';
      CLEAR(RecContactSalesperson_L);
      RecContactSalesperson_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Contact Code",Owner);
      RecContactSalesperson_L.SETRANGE("Contact Code", ContactNo_P);
      RecContactSalesperson_L.SETRANGE(Owner,TRUE);
      IF RecContactSalesperson_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        CLEAR(RecCRMSalesperson_L);
        RecCRMSalesperson_L.SETRANGE(Code,RecContactSalesperson_L."Salesperson Code");
        IF RecCRMSalesperson_L.FINDFIRST THEN
          OwnerGUIDRes_L := cduManCon_G.GetGuid(Text8053631, RecCRMSalesperson_L."User ID");
      END;
      EXIT(OwnerGUIDRes_L);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SyncHistoryCharge@1000000007(Start_P@1000000006 : Integer;End_P@1000000007 : Integer);
    VAR
      recEntry_L@8053600 : Record 5802;
      recConnectorSetup_L@8053601 : Record 8053602;
      cduCatchError_L@8053602 : Codeunit 8053608;
      recAutomation_L@8053000 : Automation "{90B5DB41-ED3C-488E-84F5-6647F85F95A2} 1.0:{F313095A-9ADF-3065-9FD4-FD3674235D4D}:Unknown Automation Server.Unknown Class";
      RecSalesLine_L@1000000000 : Record 37;
      RecGUID2_L@1000000001 : Record 8053609;
      Code_L@1000000002 : Code[50];
      Separator_L@1000000003 : Code[10];
      cduCatchError2_L@1000000004 : Codeunit 8053608;
      Total_L@1000000005 : Integer;
      Start_L@60000 : DateTime;
      End_L@60001 : DateTime;
      Table33165HistoCRM_L@1000000008 : Record 60108;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Synchronisation des historiques de ventes et achats (Nav --> CRM)
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      //  Entr'es
      //  Sorties
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

      // --> 20180220-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-CJY01
      // --> 20190212-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-CJY01
      Start_L := CREATEDATETIME(TODAY,TIME);
      // <-- 20190212-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-CJY01
      // duplication de la fonction SyncHistory pour g'rer probleme historique 33316
      recConnectorSetup_L.GET();

      IF NOT(cduManCon_G.InitializeCrmConnexion()) THEN BEGIN
        cduManCon_G.GetLastConnectorError();
      END;
      cduManCon_G.GetAutomation(recAutomation_L);

      //Recherche s'il y a de nouvelle 'criture . envoyer de type appro
      recEntry_L.RESET;
      // --> 20190215-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-CJY01
      {BEFORE
      recEntry_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Item Charge No.","Inventory Posting Group","Item No.");
      AFTER}
      // --> 20190222-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-CJY01
      //retrait de ce SETCURRENTKEY finalement non id'ale
      //recEntry_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Item Charge No.","Entry No.","Source Type","Item Ledger Entry Type","Document Type","Activity Code");
      // <-- 20190222-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-CJY01
      // <-- 20190215-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-CJY01
      //recEntry_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Item No.","Posting Date","Item Ledger Entry Type","Entry Type",
      //"Variance Type","Item Charge No.","Location Code","Variant Code","Campaign No.");

      // --> 20190222-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-CJY01
      //retrait de ce filtre, finalement non n'cessaire
      //recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Item Charge No.",'<>%1','');
      // <-- 20190222-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-CJY01
      //recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Entry No.", '>%1', recConnectorSetup_L.LastEntryApproHistorySended);
      // --> 20190215-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-CJY01
      {BEFORE
      recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Entry No.", '>%1', 1020043);
      AFTER}
      recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Entry No.", '%1..%2', Start_P,End_P);
      // <-- 20190215-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-CJY01

      recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Source Type", '%1|%2', recEntry_L."Source Type"::Customer, recEntry_L."Source Type"::Vendor);
      recEntry_L.SETRANGE("Item Ledger Entry Type", recEntry_L."Item Ledger Entry Type"::Sale);
      recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Document Type", '%1|%2', recEntry_L."Document Type"::"Sales Invoice",
        recEntry_L."Document Type"::"Sales Credit Memo");
      IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryActivity <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Activity Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryActivity);
      END;

      IF recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryFamily <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L.EntryApproHistoryFamily);
      END;

      IF recConnectorSetup_L."EntryApproHistorySub-Family" <> '' THEN BEGIN
        recEntry_L.SETFILTER("Sub-Family Code", recConnectorSetup_L."EntryApproHistorySub-Family");
      END;

      recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorApproHistory := '';
      NbApprox_G := recEntry_L.COUNT;
      Compteur_G := 1;
      Diag_G.OPEN(Text60005,Compteur_G,NbApprox_G);

      IF recEntry_L.FINDSET(FALSE, FALSE) THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          Compteur_G := Compteur_G + 1;
          Diag_G.UPDATE();
          // --> 20180222-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-BSI01
          {before
          // --> 20160921-WI00-ADV01.01.00-20871-BSI01
          IF (recEntry_L."Invoiced Quantity" <> 0) AND (recEntry_L."Sales Amount (Actual)" <> 0) THEN BEGIN
          // <-- 20160921-WI00-ADV01.01.00-20871-BSI01
          after}
          IF (recEntry_L."Invoiced Quantity" <> 0) OR (recEntry_L."Sales Amount (Actual)" <> 0) THEN BEGIN
          // <-- 20180222-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-BSI01
            // --> 20190327-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-CJY02
            IF NOT Table33165HistoCRM_L.GET(recEntry_L."Entry No.") THEN BEGIN
            // <-- 20190327-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-CJY02
              cduCatchError_L.SetTypeSync(Type_G::HistoryAppro, recAutomation_L, recAutomation_L);

              IF cduCatchError_L.RUN() THEN BEGIN

                //COMMIT;

                COMMIT;

                //erreur : on sauvegarde ce que l'on a pu faire
                recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorApproHistory := COPYSTR(GETLASTERRORTEXT, 1, MAXSTRLEN(recConnectorSetup_L.ErrorApproHistory));
                recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
                COMMIT;
                recEntry_L.FINDLAST;
              END;
            // --> 20190327-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-CJY02
            END;
            // <-- 20190327-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-CJY02

              // <-- 20160921-WI00-ADV01.01.00-20871-BSI01
          END;
          // --> 20160921-WI00-ADV01.01.00-20871-BSI01
        UNTIL recEntry_L.NEXT = 0;
      END;
      recConnectorSetup_L.MODIFY;
      COMMIT;
      Diag_G.CLOSE;
      // --> 20190212-WI00-ADV01.01.00-33165-CJY01
      {BEFORE
      MESSAGE('Traitement Termin'');
      AFTER}
      End_L := CREATEDATETIME(TODAY,TIME);
      MESSAGE('Traitement Termin' : d'but %1, fin %2',Start_L,End_L);
